\section{Review of the vacuum incoming state}\label{sec:vac}
\subsection{Two outgoing gluons}
To order $g^2$, there are three  diagrams contributing to the production of two gluons in the final state.

\subsubsection{Two $\rho$ part}

We first look into the contribution arising from the emission of two gluons from the valence degree of freedom.
\begin{figure}[h]
   \tikzfig{vacuumto2g2rho}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}^{1}\right\rangle &=\,\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\,dq^{+}\, dk^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\,\int\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\, d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\left|g_{j}^{b}(k-p)\, g_{l}^{c}(p)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{j}^{b}(k-p)\, g_{l}^{c}(p)\left|H_{g}\right|g_{i}^{a}(q)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(q)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{gg}(p,\, k-p)\, E_{g}(q)}.
\end{align}
Using the appropriate expressions (Eqs.~\eqref{m2}, \eqref{m3}), we obtain
\begin{align}\label{ggrhorho1}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}^{1}\right\rangle & = \frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\,dk^{+}\, dq^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\,\frac{1}{\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{2q^{+}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{2p^{+}}+\frac{(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}{2(k^{+}-p^{+})}\right)} \bigg(\delta^{ac}\delta_{il}\delta^{(3)}(p-q)\,\frac{g\left(\mathbf{k}^{j}-\mathbf{p}^{j}\right)\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{k} +\mathbf{p})}{4\pi^{3/2}|k^{+}-p^{+}|^{3/2}}\notag \\ & +\delta^{ab}\delta_{ji}\delta^{(3)}(k-p-q)\,\frac{g\mathbf{p}^{l}\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{p})}{4\pi^{3/2}|p^{+}|^{3/2}}\bigg) \left(\frac{g\mathbf{q}^{i}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{q})}{4\pi^{3/2}|q^{+}|^{3/2}}\right)\left|g_{j}^{b}(k-p)\, g_{l}^{c}(p)\right\rangle. 
\end{align} 
\begin{align}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}^{1}\right\rangle &=\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\,dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{g^{2}\mathbf{p}^{j}\left(\mathbf{k}^{i}-\mathbf{p}^{i}\right)}{8\pi^{3}\left(\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}+(1-\xi)\mathbf{p}^{2}\right)}\bigg(\frac{\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})\sqrt{\xi}}{\mathbf{p}^{2}\sqrt{1-\xi}}\,\notag \\ & +\,\frac{\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\sqrt{1-\xi}}{(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\sqrt{\xi}}\bigg)\left|g_{i}^{a}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})\,g_{j}^{b}(\xi k^{+},\,\mathbf{p})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Using the decomposition,
\begin{align}\label{decomposition}
\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p}) &= \frac{1}{2}\left[\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p})\right]\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\left\{ \rho^{b}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p})\right\} \\
&= \frac{i}{2}f^{bad}\rho^{d}(-\mathbf{k})\,+\,\frac{1}{2}\left\{ \rho^{b}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p})\right\}, 
\end{align}
we can separate the contributions
\begin{align}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}^{1}\right\rangle \equiv \left|\Psi_{gg[\rho,\rho]}^{1a}\right\rangle + \left|\Psi_{gg\{\rho,\rho\}}^{1b}\right\rangle.
\end{align}
The first term is given by:
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{gg[\rho,\rho]}^{1a}\right\rangle &= -\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dk^{+} \int d^{2}k\, d^{2}p\, \int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\, \frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\,p^{i}(k^{j}-p^{j})\rho^{c}(-k)\, \sqrt{p^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})}}{16\pi^{3}\, \left(p^{+}(k-p)^{2}+(k^{+}-p^{+})p^{2}\right)}\notag \\ &  \left[\frac{1}{(k^{+}-p^{+})\,p^{2}} - \frac{1}{(k-p)^{2}\,p^{+}}\right]\, \ket{g^{b}_{j}(k^{+}-p^{+}, k-p),g^{a}_{i}(p^{+},p)}
\end{align}
\begin{align}\label{part 1}
\left|\Psi_{gg[\rho,\rho]}^{1a}\right\rangle &=\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\, dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{-ig^{2}f^{abc}\mathbf{p}^{i}\left(\mathbf{k}^{j}-\mathbf{p}^{j}\right)\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k})\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}}{16\pi^{3}\left(\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}+(1-\xi)\mathbf{p}^{2}\right)} \left(\frac{1}{(1-\xi)\mathbf{p}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}\right)\notag \\ & \left|g_{j}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})\,g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\,\mathbf{p})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
The second term contains the two $\rho$ factors:
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{gg\{\rho,\rho\}}^{1b}\right\rangle & =  \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dk^{+} \int d^{2}k\, d^{2}p\, \int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\, \frac{g^{2}p^{i}(k^{j}-p^{j})\,\{\rho^{a(-p),\rho^{b}(-k+p)}\}}{16\pi^{3}\,p^{2}(k-p)^{2}\, \sqrt{p^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})}}\, \ket{g^{b}_{j}(k^{+}-p^{+}, k-p),g^{a}_{i}(p^{+},p)}
\end{align}
\begin{align}\label{part 2}
\left|\Psi_{gg\{\rho,\rho\}}^{1b}\right\rangle &= \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{g^{2}\mathbf{p}^{i}\left(\mathbf{k}^{j}-\mathbf{p}^{j}\right)\left\{ \rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\} }{16\pi^{3}\mathbf{p}^{2}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}}  \left|g_{j}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})\,g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\,\mathbf{p})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
We transform Eq.~ \eqref{part 2} in the coordinate space using Eq.~ \eqref{i1}
\begin{align}\label{gg_rhorho}
\left|\Psi_{gg\{\rho,\rho\}}^{1b}\right\rangle &=\,-\int_{\mathbf{x},\,\mathbf{y},\,\mathbf{z},\,\mathbf{z}^{\prime}}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\frac{g^{2}\left\{ \rho^{a}(\mathbf{y}),\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{x})\right\} (y-z)^{i}(x-z^{\prime})^{j}}{16\pi^{3}\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}(y-z)^{2}(x-z^{\prime})^{2}}\left|g_{j}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\mathbf{z}^{\prime})\, g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\mathbf{z})\right\rangle .
\end{align}

\subsubsection{Single $\rho$ part}
There are two diagrams for this particular section and we first look into a single gluon state from vacuum splitting into two gluons.
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{vacuumto2g}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{ggtwo}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}^{2}\right\rangle & = \,\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\, dq^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\left|g_{j}^{b}(q)\, g_{l}^{c}(p)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{j}^{b}(q)\, g_{l}^{c}(p)\left|H_{ggg}\right|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(k)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{gg}(p,\, q)\, E_{g}(k)}.
\end{align}
By using the relevant matrix elements, Eq.~ \eqref{m1} and Eq.~ \eqref{m3}, we get
\begin{align}\label{ggmid}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}^{2}\right\rangle 
&=\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,d^{2}\mathbf{p}\:\frac{igf^{acb}}{16\pi^{3/2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{2p^{+}}+\frac{(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}{2(k^{+}-p^{+})}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{2k^{+}}\right)\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})}}\notag \\ &
\left(\left[2\mathbf{p}^{i}-\frac{2p^{+}}{k^{+}}\mathbf{k}^{i}\right]\delta_{jl}+\left[\frac{k^{+}+p^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}}(\mathbf{k}^{j}-\mathbf{p}^{j})-\mathbf{k}^{j}-\mathbf{p}^{j}\right]\delta_{il}+\left[2\mathbf{k}^{l}-\frac{2k^{+}}{p^{+}}\mathbf{p}^{l}\right]\delta_{ij}\right)
\left(\frac{g\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\mathbf{k}^{i}}{4\pi^{3/2}|k^{+}|^{3/2}}\right)\left|g_{j}^{b}(k-p)\, g_{l}^{c}(p)\right\rangle. 
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}^{2}\right\rangle 
&=\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{k^{+}}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, \frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(-k)k^{l}}{8\pi^{3}\,k^{2}\,\sqrt{p^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})}\,\left(\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}+\frac{(k-p)^{2}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}\right)}\,\Bigg[\left(\frac{p^{l}k^{+}-p^{+}k^{l}}{k^{+}}\right)\delta_{ij}\notag \\ & +\left(\frac{p^{+}k^{j}-k^{+}p^{j}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}\right)\delta_{il} +\left(\frac{k^{i}p^{+}-k^{+}p^{i}}{p^{+}}\right)\delta_{jl}\Bigg]\, \ket{g^{b}_{j}(k^{+}-p^{+}, k-p),g^{a}_{i}(p^{+},p)}
\end{align}
By introducing new variables 
$$\mathbf{p}\,=\,\xi \mathbf{k}\,+\,\widetilde{\mathbf{p}},$$
and Eq.~ \eqref{ggmid} becomes
\begin{align}\label{contgg}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}^{2}\right\rangle\, & =\,-\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}\mathbf{k}^{i}}{8\pi^{3}\left(\mathbf{k}^{2}\xi(1-\xi)+\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{k}^{2}}\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{i}\delta_{jl}-\frac{1}{1-\xi}\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{j}\delta_{il}-\frac{1}{\xi}\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{l}\delta_{ij}\right)\notag \\ & \left|g_{j}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}-\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})\,g_{l}^{c}(\xi k^{+},\,\xi\mathbf{k}+\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
The instantaneous contributions are as follows
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{vacuumto2ginst}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{ggthree}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}^{3}\right\rangle &= \,-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\left|g_{i}^{b}(k-p)\, g_{j}^{c}(p)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{i}^{b}(k-p)\, g_{j}^{c}(p)\left|H_{gg-inst}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{gg}(k-p,\, p)}.
\end{align}
By using the relevant matrix elements, Eq.~ \eqref{m4}, and changing the variables we get
\begin{align}\label{instgg3}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}^{3}\right\rangle \,& =\, -\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\left|g_{i}^{b}(k-p)\, g_{j}^{c}(p)\right\rangle  \frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}(2p^{+}-k^{+})\rho^{a}(-k)\delta_{ij}}{2(2\pi)^{3}\sqrt{p^{+}(k-p)^{+}}k^{+ 2}\bigg(\frac{(k-p)^{2}}{(k-p)^{+}}+\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}\bigg)}
\end{align}
Relabeling 
\begin{align}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}^{3}\right\rangle \,& =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\left|g_{i}^{b}(k-p)\, g_{j}^{a}(p)\right\rangle  \frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}(2p^{+}-k^{+})\rho^{c}(-k)\delta_{ij}}{2(2\pi)^{3}\sqrt{p^{+}(k-p)^{+}}k^{+ 2}\bigg(\frac{(k-p)^{2}}{(k-p)^{+}}+\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}\bigg)}
\end{align}
\begin{align}\label{contgg3}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}^{3}\right\rangle \,& =\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,d^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})(1-2\xi)\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}}{16\pi^{3}\left(\mathbf{k}^{2}\xi(1-\xi)+\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{2}\right)}\delta_{ij}\notag \\ & \left|g_{i}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}-\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})\,g_{j}^{c}(\xi k^{+},\,\xi\mathbf{k}+\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})\right\rangle .\end{align}
where $\mathbf{p}\,=\,\xi \mathbf{k}\,+\,\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}$. 
\subsection{One outgoing gluon}
There is one diagram at order $(g)$, which represents a single outgoing gluon emission. At order $(g^{3})$, there are several contributions for a single outgoing gluon state. 
\subsubsection{Single $\rho$ part}
We first examine the contribution corresponding to the emission of a single gluon from the valence degree of freedom, which results in the leading order contribution.
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{vacuumtog}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{LO}\right\rangle \, & =\- -\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dk^{+} \int d^{2}\mathbf{k} \ket{g^{a}_{i}(\mathbf{k})}\frac{\left\langle g_{i}^{a}(\mathbf{k})\left|\, H_{g}\,\right|0\right\rangle}{E_{g}(\mathbf{k})}.
\end{align}
Using the matrix element from Eq.~ \eqref{m1} and $E_{g}(k) = \frac{k^{2}}{2k^{+}}$
\begin{equation}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{LO}\right\rangle \,=\- -\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g\, \mathbf{k}^{i}}{2\pi^{3/2}\sqrt{k^{+}}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\left|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle.
\end{equation}
Using the transformation equation (Eq.~ \eqref{i1}) we get the wave function in co-ordinate space.
\begin{align}\label{lowav}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{LO}\right\rangle \,& =\,\frac{ig}{2\pi^{3/2}}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\,\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\int_{\mathbf{x},\, \mathbf{z}}\,\frac{ (x-z)^{i}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\rho^{a}(\mathbf{x})\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{z})\right\rangle .
 \end{align}
Now, we will examine the contribution at order $g^{3}$ with one $\rho$ involvement.
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{loop}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{gtwoloop}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{1}\right\rangle & = -\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\, dq^{+}\, dr^{+}\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\, d^{2}\mathbf{r} \left|g_{l}^{d}(r)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{l}^{d}(r)\left|H_{ggg}\right|g_{k}^{c}(q)\, g_{j}^{b}(p)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{k}^{c}(q)\, g_{j}^{b}(p)\left|H_{ggg}\right|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(k)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{g}(r)\, E_{gg}(p,\, q)\, E_{g}(k)},
\end{align}
Using the matrix elements from Eqs.~ \eqref{m1} and \eqref{m3}, and after the application of the momentum conservation law, which introduces delta functions, both $q$ and $r$ are determined to be $q = k-p$ and $r=k$, we obtain 
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{1}\right\rangle & = -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dk^{+}\, \int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda} dp^{+}\, \int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{g^{3}f^{abc}f^{dbc}\rho^{a}(-k)\,k^{i}}{64\pi^{3/2}\,(2\pi)^{3}k^{+}p^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})\,\left(\frac{k^{2}}{2k^{+}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{p^{2}}{2p^{+}}+\frac{(k-p)^{2}}{2(k^{+}-p^{+})}\right)\,k^{+\,3/2}}\notag \\ & \left[\left(2p^{l}-\frac{2p^{+}}{k^{+}}k^{l}\right)\delta_{jk}+\left(2k^{j}-\frac{2k^{+}}{p^{+}} p^{j}\right)\delta_{lk}+\left(\frac{(k^{+}+p^{+})}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}(k^{k}-p^{k})-k^{k}-p^{k}\right)\delta_{lj}\right]\times \notag \\ & \left[\left(2p^{i}-\frac{2p^{+}}{k^{+}}k^{i}\right)\delta_{jk}+\left(2k^{j}-\frac{2k^{+}}{p^{+}} p^{j}\right)\delta_{ik}+\left(\frac{(k^{+}+p^{+})}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}(k^{k}-p^{k})-k^{k}-p^{k}\right)\delta_{ij}\right]\, \ket{g^{d}_{l}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}
\end{align}
Introducing $p^{+} = \xi k^{+}$ and $p = \xi k +\tilde{p}$, we get
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{1}\right\rangle & = -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{\tilde{p}}\, \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}} d\xi\, \frac{g^{3}f^{abc}f^{dbc}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{8\pi^{3/2}\,(2\pi)^{3}\, \xi(1-\xi)\,k^{4}\left(k^{2}\xi\,(1-\xi)+\tilde{p}^{2}\right)}\notag \\ & \left[2\tilde{p}^{l}\delta_{jk}-\frac{2}{\xi}\tilde{p}^{j}\delta_{lk}-\frac{2}{(1-\xi)}\tilde{p}^{k}\delta_{lj}\right] \times \left[2\tilde{p}^{i}\delta_{jk}-\frac{2}{\xi}\tilde{p}^{j}\delta_{ik}-\frac{2}{(1-\xi)}\tilde{p}^{k}\delta_{ij}\right]\, \ket{g^{d}_{l}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}
\end{align}
Simplifying we get
\begin{align}
     \left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{1}\right\rangle & = -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{\tilde{p}}\, \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}} d\xi\, \frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}\tilde{p}^{2}}{16\pi^{9/2}\, k^{4}\,\left(k^{2}\xi\,(1-\xi)+\tilde{p}^{2}\right)}\times \left[1+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}+\frac{1}{(1-\xi)^{2}}\right]\,\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}
\end{align}
Integrating wrt $\tilde{p}$, we get
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{1}\right\rangle & = -\mu^{2\epsilon}\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}} \, \int \frac{d^{d}k}{(2\pi)^{d}}\, \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}} d\xi\, \frac{g^{3}\,N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k) k^{i}}{k^{2}\sqrt{\pi}}\times \left[\frac{d}{2(4\pi)^{d/2}}\Gamma\left(-\frac{d}{2}\right)\,\left(\xi(1-\xi)k^{2}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}-1}\right]\times\notag \\ &  \left[\xi(1-\xi)-2+\frac{1}{\xi(1-\xi)}\right]\,\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}
\end{align}
By performing $\Gamma$ expansion and setting $\epsilon \rightarrow 0$, we get
\begin{align}
     \left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{1}\right\rangle & = - \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}} \,\int d^{2}k\, \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}} d\xi\, \frac{g^{3}\,N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k) k^{i}}{16\pi^{7/2}\,k^{2}}\times \left[-2+\xi(1-\xi)+\frac{1}{\xi(1-\xi)}\right]\times \left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}\xi(1-\xi)}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}
\end{align}
Integrating over $\xi$ \textcolor{red}{and setting $\Lambda$ to be $0$ wherever possible} we get\\
\begin{align}\label{gtwoloop1}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{1}\right\rangle\,=\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\mathbf{k}^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}
\left[\left(\frac{11}{3}+4\ln\,\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)\right)\times\left(-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\ln\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{\mu_{\overline{MS}}^{2}}\right)\right)+2\ln^{2}\,\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{2\pi^{2}}{3}\right]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}.
\end{align}
{\color{blue}\noindent\textbf{Correction.} (X1) The exact $\xi$ integral, $\delta=\Lambda/k^+$, replaces the bracket by
\begin{align}
&\Big(\frac{11}{3}-8\delta+2\delta^2-\frac43\delta^3+4\ln\frac{\delta}{1-\delta}\Big)\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right]-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{2\pi^2}{3}+\frac{46}{3}\delta-\frac43\delta^2+\frac89\delta^3\notag\\
&+\Big(\frac{22}{3}-8\delta+2\delta^2-\frac43\delta^3\Big)\ln(1-\delta)-\Big(8\delta-2\delta^2+\frac43\delta^3\Big)\ln\delta-2\ln^2(1-\delta)+2\ln^2\delta-4\ln\delta\ln(1-\delta)-8\,{\rm Li}_2(\delta).\notag
\end{align}}

\subsubsection{Two $\rho$ part}
This subsection contains terms which is of order $g^{3}$ with two $\rho$ involvement. We start with the instantaneous interactions and the gluon emitted from the valence degree of freedom can either lose or gain momentum (longitudinal). 
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{Misha_2rho_single_g}
    \label{fig:4}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{koa}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\:\text{inst.}}^{2}\right\rangle \,& = \,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,d^{2}\mathbf{p}\:\left|g_{j}^{b}(k)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{j}^{b}(k)\left|H_{gg-inst}\right|g_{i}^{a}(p)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(p)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{g}(k)E_{g}(p)}.
\end{align}
First we consider the process when the longitudinal momentum flows from the valence degree of freedom.
\begin{align}\label{gthreed}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\:\text{inst.}}^{2a}\right\rangle \,& = \,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\:\left|g_{j}^{b}(k)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{j}^{b}(k)\left|H_{gg-inst}\right|g_{i}^{a}(p)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(p)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{g}(k)E_{g}(p)},
\end{align}
Using the matrix elements from equations \eqref{m1} and  \eqref{m5}
\begin{align}\label{instgg}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\:\text{inst.}}^{2a}\right\rangle & =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda} dp^{+}\, \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(p-k)\rho^{a}(-p)p^{j}(p^{+}+k^{+})\sqrt{k^{+}}}{16\pi^{9/2}\, p^{+}\,(p^{+}-k^{+})^{2}\, k^{2}p^{2}}\,\delta_{ij}\,\left|g_{i}^{b}( k^{+},\,\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Integrating wrt $p^{+}$ \textcolor{red}{and setting $\Lambda \rightarrow 0$ wherever possible} we get
\begin{align}
  \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\:\text{inst.}}^{2a}\right\rangle & =  \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p})\mathbf{p}^{i}}{8\pi^{9/2} \mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{2}}\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}-1-\ln\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)\right)\left|g_{i}^{b}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle
\end{align}
{\color{blue}\noindent\textbf{Correction.} (X2) Exactly, $\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}-1-\ln\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\to E\equiv\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}-\frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-\Lambda}+\ln\frac{k^{+}-\Lambda}{\Lambda}$, here, in Eq.~\eqref{grho3}, in the one-$\rho$ term below, and in Eq.~\eqref{tworho2}.}

Using the decomposition principle (see Eq.~ \eqref{decomposition}), we split Eq.~ \eqref{instgg} into one $\rho$ and two $\rho$ components.
\begin{align}\label{grho3}
\left|\Psi_{g\{\rho,\rho\}\:\text{inst.}}^{2a}\right\rangle &=\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\mathbf{p}^{i}\left\{ \rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\} }{16\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{2}}\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\,-\,1\,-\,\ln\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)\right)\left|g_{i}^{b}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle .  
\end{align}
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\,[\rho,\rho]\:\text{inst.}}^{2a}\right\rangle &=\,-\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{g^{3}\,N_{c}\mathbf{p}^{i}\rho^{a}(-k)}{16\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{2}}\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\,-\,1\,-\,\ln\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)\right)\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle = 0
\end{align}
Since, 
$$\int\, d^{2}p\, \frac{p^{i}}{p^{2}} = 0$$ 
Next, we consider the momentum flowing into the valence degree of freedom.
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{Misha_Out_2rho_single_g}
    \label{fig:5}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{gthreeu}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\:\text{inst.}}^{2b}\right\rangle \,=\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\:\left|g_{j}^{b}(p)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{j}^{b}(p)\left|H_{gg-inst}\right|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(k)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{g}(p)E_{g}(k)}.
\end{align}
Using the matrix elements from equations \eqref{m1} and  \eqref{m5}
\begin{align}\label{eq:gg-uu}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\:\text{inst.}}^{2b}\right\rangle & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda} dp^{+}\, \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(k-p)\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}(p^{+}+k^{+})\sqrt{p^{+}}}{16\pi^{9/2}\, k^{+}\,(p^{+}-k^{+})^{2}\, k^{2}p^{2}}\,\delta_{ij}\,\left|g_{j}^{b}( p^{+},\,\mathbf{p})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Using the decomposition principle (see Eq.~ \eqref{decomposition}), we split Eq.~ \eqref{eq:gg-uu} into one $\rho$ and two $\rho$ components.
\begin{align} \label{eq:ACrhorho}
 \left|\Psi_{g\{\rho,\rho\}\:\text{inst.}}^{2b}\right\rangle & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda} dp^{+}\, \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\{\rho^{c}(k-p),\rho^{a}(-k)\}\,k^{i}(p^{+}+k^{+})\sqrt{p^{+}}}{32\pi^{9/2}\, k^{+}\,(p^{+}-k^{+})^{2}\, k^{2}p^{2}}\,\delta_{ij}\,\left|g_{j}^{b}( p^{+},\,\mathbf{p})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Introducing new variables $\xi = \frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$ and $p\leftrightarrow k$, we get
\begin{align}\label{grhorho4}
    \left|\Psi_{g\{\rho,\rho\}\:\text{inst.}}^{2b}\right\rangle & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}} d\xi\, \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\{\rho^{c}(-k+p),\rho^{a}(-p)\}\,p^{i}}{32\pi^{9/2}\, \, k^{2}p^{2}}\,\frac{(1+\xi)\sqrt{\xi}}{(1-\xi)^{2}}\,\left|g_{i}^{b}( \xi k^{+},\,\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
The corresponding one $\rho$ term is as follows and it vanishes due to $p$ integration. 
\begin{align}
 \left|\Psi_{g\,[\rho,\rho]\:\text{inst.}}^{2b}\right\rangle & = -\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}} d\xi\, \frac{g^{3}\,N_{c}\,\rho^{b}(-k)\,p^{i}}{32\pi^{9/2}\, k^{2}p^{2}}\,\times\frac{(1+\xi)\sqrt{\xi}}{(1-\xi)^{2}}\,\left|g_{i}^{b}( \xi k^{+},\,\mathbf{k})\right\rangle = 0
\end{align}
\pagebreak
Next, we consider a diagram in which two gluons emitted from the vacuum combine to form a gluon.
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{Misha_2rho_3_single_g}
    \label{fig:8}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{ggtogrhorho}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{3}\right\rangle & = -\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\, dq^{+}\, dr^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\, d^{2}\mathbf{r} \left|g_{m}^{d}(p)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{m}^{d}(p)\left|H_{ggg}\right|g_{k}^{c}(r)\, g_{j}^{b}(q)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{k}^{c}(r)\, g_{j}^{b}(q)\left|H_{g}\right|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(k)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{g}(k)\, E_{gg}(q,\, r)\, E_{g}(p)}.
\end{align}
Using the matrix elements from \eqref{m1}, \eqref{m2} and \eqref{m3}, we get 
\begin{align}\label{part2}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{3}\right\rangle \, & =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\, dq^{+}\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}  \frac{igf^{bcd}}{\left(\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{2q^{+}}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{2p^{+}}+\frac{(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}{2(k^{+}-p^{+})}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{2k^{+}}\right)16\pi^{3/2}\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})}}\notag \\ &\left[\left(2\mathbf{p}^{l}-\frac{2p^{+}}{k^{+}}\mathbf{k}^{l}\right)\delta_{nj}+\left(2\mathbf{k}^{j}-\frac{2k^{+}}{p^{+}}\mathbf{p}^{j}\right)\delta_{ln}+\left(\frac{k^{+}+p^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}}(\mathbf{k}^{n}-\mathbf{p}^{n})-\mathbf{k}^{n}-\mathbf{p}^{n}\right)\delta_{lj}\right]\notag \\ &\bigg(\delta^{ac}\delta^{ni}\delta^{(3)}(q-k+p)\frac{g\mathbf{p}^{j}\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})}{4\pi^{3/2}|p^{+}|^{3/2}}+\delta^{ab}\textcolor{red}{\delta^{ni}}\textcolor{blue}{\delta^{ji}}\delta^{(3)}(q-p)\frac{g(\mathbf{k}^{n}-\mathbf{p}^{n})\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})}{4\pi^{3/2}|k^{+}-p^{+}|^{3/2}}\bigg)\left(\frac{g\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{q})\mathbf{q}^{i}}{4\pi^{3/2}|q^{+}|^{3/2}}\right)\left|g_{l}^{d}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Introducing $\xi = \frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$, we obtain
 \begin{align}\label{3grhorho}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{3}\right\rangle & =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{bad}\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})}{16\pi^{9/2}\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\left((1-\xi)\mathbf{p}^{2}+\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\right)\mathbf{k}^{2}\sqrt{k^{+}}}\notag \\ & \left[\left(2\mathbf{p}^{l}-2\xi \mathbf{k}^{l}\right)\mathbf{p}\cdot(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})+\left(2\mathbf{k}\cdot \mathbf{p}-\frac{2}{\xi}\mathbf{p}^{2}\right)(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{l}\right.\left.+\left(\frac{1+\xi}{1-\xi}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}-\mathbf{k}^{2}+\mathbf{p}^{2}\right)\mathbf{p}^{l}\right]\left|g_{l}^{d}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Using the decomposition principle (i.e., \eqref{decomposition}), we split \eqref{3grhorho} into one $\rho$ and two $\rho$ components.
\begin{align}\label{onerho2}
\left|\Psi_{g[\rho,\rho]}^{3}\right\rangle & = -\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\mathbf{k}^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\left(3\ln\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\ln\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{\mu_{\overline{MS}}^{2}}\right)\right]+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}+2\ln^{2}\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)\right)\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle .
\end{align}
{\color{blue}\noindent\textbf{Correction.} (X3) The $\xi$ integral is done with $\Lambda\to0$. Exactly ($\delta=\Lambda/k^+$) the bracket is
\begin{equation*}
3\ln\frac{\delta}{1-\delta}\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right]+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}+2\ln^2\delta-2\ln^2(1-\delta)-2\ln\delta\ln(1-\delta)-4\,{\rm Li}_2(\delta).
\end{equation*}}

\begin{align}\label{tworho2}
\left|\Psi_{g\{\rho,\rho\}}^{3}\right\rangle & = -\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\} }{32\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{p}^{2}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\bigg[\ln\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)\mathbf{k}\cdot \mathbf{p}\,\mathbf{k}^{i}-\bigg(\ln\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)\mathbf{k}^{2}\notag \\ & +2\left(\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}-1-\ln\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)\right)(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\bigg)\mathbf{p}^{i}\bigg]\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
{\color{blue}\noindent\textbf{Correction.} (X4) Exactly, the anticommutator part of Eq.~\eqref{3grhorho} is
$\int\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,d^2\mathbf{p}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\{\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p}),\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\}}{32\pi^{9/2}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}V^i\ket{g^a_i(k^+,\mathbf{k})}$ with
\begin{equation*}
V^i=(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^i\Big[\frac{2\,\mathbf{k}\cdot(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})}{\mathbf{p}^2}(\ell-{\cal L})-2\Big(\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}-\frac{k^+}{k^+-\Lambda}\Big)\Big]-\frac{2\,\mathbf{p}\cdot(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})}{\Delta}{\cal L}\,\mathbf{k}^i+2(\ell+{\cal L})\mathbf{p}^i,
\end{equation*}
$\ell=\ln\frac{k^+-\Lambda}{\Lambda}$, $\Delta=(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^2-\mathbf{p}^2$, ${\cal L}=\ln\frac{k^+(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^2-\Lambda\Delta}{k^+\mathbf{p}^2+\Lambda\Delta}$. In the sum with Eq.~\eqref{grho3} (with $E$ exact), the part odd under $\mathbf p\to\mathbf k-\mathbf p$ (the only one that survives) is exactly the first term of Eq.~\eqref{combinedgrhorho} with $\ln\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}\to\ln\frac{\Lambda}{k^+-\Lambda}$.}

\pagebreak 
We look into an instantaneous contribution which gives two gluons in which one is absorbed to the valence.  
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{Misha_2rho_insta_single_g}
    \label{fig:7}
\end{figure}
\begin{align} \label{gfour}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{4}\right\rangle & =\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\, dq^{+}\, dk^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}\, dp^{+}\,\int\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\, d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\left|g_{l}^{d}(p)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{l}^{d}(p)\left|H_{g}\right|g_{j}^{b}(q)\, g_{i}^{a}(k-q)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{j}^{b}(q)\, g_{i}^{a}(k-q)\left|H_{gg-inst}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{g}(p)E_{gg}(q,\, k-q)}.
\end{align}
Using the matrix elements from Eqs.~ \eqref{m2} and \eqref{m4} we get,
 \begin{align}\label{inst4}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{4}\right\rangle \,& = \frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,dq^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\, \int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\frac{1}{\left(\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{2q^{+}}+\frac{(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q})^{2}}{2(k^{+}-q^{+})}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{2p^{+}}\right)}\bigg(\delta^{dc}\delta_{il}\delta^{(3)}(k-p-q)\frac{g\mathbf{q}^{j}\rho^{b}(\mathbf{q})}{4\pi^{3/2}|q^{+}|^{3/2}}\notag \\ & +\delta^{db}\delta_{lj}\delta^{(3)}(q-p)\frac{g(\mathbf{k}^{i}-\mathbf{q}^{i})\rho^{c}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q})}{4\pi^{3/2}|k^{+}-q^{+}|^{3/2}}\bigg) \left(\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}(k^{+}-2q^{+})\delta_{ij}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})}{2(2\pi)^{3}\sqrt{(k^{+}-q^{+})q^{+}}(k^{+})^{2}}\right)\left|g_{l}^{d}(p^{+},\mathbf{p})\right\rangle .
\end{align}
Integration wrt $q$, we get
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{4}\right\rangle \,& = \,- \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}\frac{dp^{+}}{\sqrt{p^{+}}}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}(2p^{+}-k^{+})\,\rho^{c}(k-p)\rho^{a}(-k)\,(k^{i}-p^{i})}{64\pi^{9/2}\,\left(\frac{p^{2}}{2p^{+}}+\frac{(k-p)^{2}}{2(k^{+}-p^{+})}\right)\times \left(\frac{p^{2}}{2p^{+}}\right)\, \left(k^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})\right)^{2}}\left|g_{i}^{b}(p^{+},\mathbf{p})\right\rangle.    
\end{align}
Introducing $\xi = \frac{p^+}{k^+}$, we get
    \begin{align}\label{psif}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{4}\right\rangle & = -\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})(\mathbf{k}^{i}-\mathbf{p}^{i})(2\xi-1)\xi^{3/2}}{16\pi^{9/2}\left(\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}+(1-\xi)\mathbf{p}^{2}\right)\,\mathbf{p}^{2}(1-\xi)}\left|g_{i}^{b}(\xi k^{+},\mathbf{p})\right\rangle .
\end{align}
Next, we look into another contribution where a gluon emitted from the vacuum splits into two gluons, with one of them being absorbed.
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{Misha_2rho_loop_single_g}
    \label{fig:7}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{grhorhoabs}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{5}\right\rangle & = -\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\, dq^{+}\, dr^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\, d^{2}\mathbf{r}\left|g_{m}^{d}(k)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{m}^{d}(k)\left|H_{g}\right|g_{k}^{c}(r)\, g_{j}^{b}(q)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{k}^{c}(r)\, g_{j}^{b}(q)\left|H_{ggg}\right|g_{i}^{a}(p)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(p)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{g}(p)\, E_{gg}(q,\, r)\, E_{g}(k)}.
\end{align}
Using the matrix elements from Eqs.~ \eqref{m1},\eqref{m2} and \eqref{m3}, we obtain the following
\begin{align}\label{subsgrhorhoabs}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{5}\right\rangle & =-\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,dp^{+}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dq^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,d^{2}\mathbf{q}\frac{1}{\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{2p^{+}}\right)\left(\frac{(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q})^{2}}{2(k^{+}-q^{+})}+\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{2q^{+}}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{2k^{+}}\right)}\Bigg[\delta^{dc}\delta^{km}\delta^{(3)}(k-p-q)\frac{g\mathbf{q}^{j}\rho^{b}(\mathbf{q})}{4\pi^{3/2}|q^{+}|^{3/2}}\notag \\ & +\delta^{db}\delta^{jm}\delta^{(3)}(p-q)\frac{g(\mathbf{k}^{k}-\mathbf{q}^{k})\rho^{c}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{q})}{4\pi^{3/2}|k^{+}-q^{+}|^{3/2}}\Bigg]\times\Bigg[\bigg(2\mathbf{q}^{i}-\frac{2q^{+}}{k^{+}}\mathbf{k}^{i}\bigg)\delta_{jk}+\bigg(2\mathbf{k}^{j}-\frac{2k^{+}}{q^{+}}\mathbf{q}^{j}\bigg)\delta_{ik}+\bigg(\frac{k^{+}+q^{+}}{k^{+}-q^{+}}(\mathbf{k}^{k}-\mathbf{q}^{k})\notag \\ & -\mathbf{k}^{k}-\mathbf{q}^{k}\bigg)\delta_{ij}\Bigg]\frac{igf^{abc}}{16\pi^{3/2}\sqrt{k^{+}q^{+}(k^{+}-q^{+})}}\left(\frac{g\mathbf{k}^{i}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})}{4\pi^{3/2}|k^{+}|^{3/2}}\right)\left|g_{m}^{d}(p^{+},\mathbf{p})\right\rangle .
\end{align}
Integrating wrt $q$ and introducing $p^{+} = \xi k^{+}$ we get
\begin{align}\label{psiff}
 \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{5}\right\rangle & =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\xi^{3/2}}{16\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{2}\left(\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}+(1-\xi)\mathbf{p}^{2}\right)(1-\xi)}\notag \\
&\times\left[\left((2\xi-1)\mathbf{k}^{2}-\mathbf{p}^{2}-\frac{1+\xi}{1-\xi}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\right)\,\mathbf{k}^{i}+\left(\frac{\xi-1}{\xi}\mathbf{k}\cdot \mathbf{p}+\frac{1-\xi^{2}}{\xi}\mathbf{k}^{2}\right)\,2\mathbf{p}^{i}\right]\left|g_{i}^{b}(\xi k^{+},\, \mathbf{p})\right\rangle.  
\end{align}
By combining Eqs.~. \eqref{psif} and \eqref{psiff}, we split them into one $\rho$ and two $\rho$ components using the decomposition principle (Eq.~ \eqref{decomposition}), we get 
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{4}\right\rangle + \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{5}\right\rangle & =  \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{4+5}\right\rangle + \left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{4+5}\right\rangle
\end{align}
We write it by doing a transformation as $p\leftrightarrow k$ and we get 
\begin{align}\label{4+5tworho}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{4+5}\right\rangle & =
\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p})\right\} \xi^{3/2}}{32\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{2}\left(\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}+(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}\right)(1-\xi)}\notag \\ & \left(\left[\frac{2}{\xi}\left(\mathbf{p}^{2}-\mathbf{k}\cdot \mathbf{p}\right)-\mathbf{p}^{2}+2\mathbf{k}\cdot \mathbf{p}\right]\mathbf{k}^{j}-\left[\mathbf{k}^{2}+\frac{1+\xi}{1-\xi}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\right]\mathbf{p}^{j}\right)\left|g_{j}^{b}(\xi k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
and
\begin{align}\label{4+5onerho}
   \left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{4+5}\right\rangle & =  \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\mathbf{k}^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\sqrt{\xi}(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg(\left(\xi-2\right)\ln\left(\xi\right)+\left(\xi+1\right)\ln\left(1-\xi\right)-\left(\xi-2\right)\notag \\ & \left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\ln\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{\mu_{\overline{MS}}^{2}}\right)\right]\Bigg)\left|g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
A convenient way to represent \eqref{4+5tworho} using \eqref{grhorho4}, we get
\begin{align}\label{4+5tworhosimpl}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{4+5}\right\rangle & =-\left|\Psi_{g\{\rho,\rho\}\:\text{inst.}}^{2b}\right\rangle \notag \\ & 
+\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p})\right\} \xi^{3/2}}{32\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{2}\left(\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}+(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}\right)(1-\xi)}\notag \\ & \Bigg(\left[\frac{2}{\xi}\left(\mathbf{p}^{2}-\mathbf{k}\cdot \mathbf{p}\right)-\mathbf{p}^{2}+2\mathbf{k}\cdot \mathbf{p}\right]\mathbf{k}^{i}+\frac{1}{\xi}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{i}\Bigg)\left|g_{i}^{b}(\xi k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
The benefit of this final representation becomes clear in the next subsection, where we combine $\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}^{4+5}\right\rangle$ and $\left|\Psi_{g\{\rho,\rho\}\:\text{inst.}}^{2b}\right\rangle$. In this form, the mutual cancellation between the contributions becomes immediately transparent.
\subsubsection{Three $\rho$ part}
We will consider three contributions in which a gluon emitted from the vacuum is absorbed, followed by the emission of another gluon. There are three different possible time orderings for this process. All these contributions are at order $g^{3}$. 
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{Misha_2rho_loopVal1_single_g}
    \label{fig:1}
\end{figure}
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{Misha_2rho_loopVal2_single_g}
    \label{fig:2} 
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{3rho}
 \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{6}\right\rangle  & = -\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\, dq^{+}\, dr^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\, d^{2}\mathbf{r}\left|g_{l}^{d}(k)\right\rangle \frac{\left\langle g_{l}^{d}(k)\left|H_{g}\right|g_{j}^{b}(r)\, g_{k}^{c}(q)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{j}^{b}(r)\, g_{k}^{c}(q)\left|H_{g}\right|g_{i}^{a}(p)\right\rangle \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(p)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle }{E_{g}(k)\, E_{gg}(r,\, q)\, E_{g}(p)}.
\end{align}
Using the matrix elements from Eqs.~. \eqref{m1} and \eqref{m2} we get,
\begin{align}\label{stseve}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{6}\right\rangle & =-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\, dq^{+}\, dr^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\, d^{2}\mathbf{r}\frac{1}{\textcolor{red}{\left(\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{2q^{+}}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{r}^{2}}{2r^{+}}+\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{2p^{+}}\right)}\textcolor{blue}{\left(\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{2p^{+}}\right)\left(\frac{\mathbf{r}^{2}}{2r^{+}}+\frac{\mathbf{q}^{2}}{2q^{+}}\right)}\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{2k^{+}}\right)}\bigg(\delta^{dc}\delta_{kl}\delta^{(3)}(q-k)\,\frac{g\mathbf{r}^{j}\rho^{b}(\mathbf{r})}{4\pi^{3/2}|r^{+}|^{3/2}}\notag \\ & +\delta^{db}\delta_{jl}\delta^{(3)}(k-r)\,\frac{g\mathbf{q}^{k}\rho^{c}(\mathbf{q})}{4\pi^{3/2}|q^{+}|^{3/2}}\bigg)\left(\delta^{ab}\delta_{ij}\delta^{(3)}(r-p)\,\frac{g\mathbf{q}^{k}\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{q})}{4\pi^{3/2}|q^{+}|^{3/2}}+\delta^{ac}\delta_{ik}\delta^{(3)}(p-q)\,\frac{g\mathbf{r}^{j}\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{r})}{4\pi^{3/2}|r^{+}|^{3/2}}\right)\notag \\ & \bigg(\frac{g\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p})\mathbf{p}^{i}}{4\pi^{3/2}|p^{+}|^{3/2}}\bigg)\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Simplifying we get
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{6}\right\rangle & = -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\, \int\, d^{2}k\, d^{2}p\, \frac{g^{3}\, p^{2}k^{i}\rho^{b}(p)}{64\pi^{9/2}\,\left(\frac{k^{2}}{2k^{+}}\right)\left(\frac{k^{2}}{2k^{+}}+\frac{p^{2}}{2p^{+}}\right)|p^{+}|^{3} |k^{+}|^{3/2}}\notag \\ & \left[\frac{\rho^{b}(-p)\rho^{a}(-k)}{\frac{k^{2}}{2k^{+}}} + \frac{\rho^{a}(-k)\rho^{b}(-p)}{\frac{p^{2}}{2p^{+}}}\right]\, \left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Using the decomposition principle (i.e., \eqref{decomposition}) we get
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{6}\right\rangle & = -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\, \int\, d^{2}k\, d^{2}p\, \frac{2\,g^{3}\, k^{i}\rho^{b}(p)}{64\pi^{9/2}\,\left(\frac{k^{2}}{2k^{+}}\right)|p^{+}|^{2} |k^{+}|^{3/2}} \left[\frac{\rho^{b}(-p)\rho^{a}(-k)}{\frac{k^{2}}{2k^{+}}} + \frac{if^{abc}\rho^{c}(-k-p)}{{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{2k^{+}}+\frac{p^{2}}{2p^{+}}\right)}}\right]\, \left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Using the decomposition principle, we will write it as $\rho, \rho\rho, \rho\rho\rho$ terms i.e.,
$$ \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{6}\right\rangle = \left|\Psi_{g\,[\rho,\rho]}^{6\, a}\right\rangle + \left|\Psi_{g\,\{\rho,\rho\}}^{6\, b}\right\rangle +  \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{6\, c}\right\rangle   $$
\begin{align}
\left|\Psi_{g\,[\rho,\rho]}^{6\, a}\right\rangle & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, dp^{+}\, \int d^{2}k \, d^{2}p \frac{g^{3}N_{c}k^{i}\rho^{a}(-k)}{16\pi^{9/2}p^{+}\left(\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}k^{2}+p^{2}\right)\,k^{2}}\, \left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Integrating wrt $p$ and setting $p^{+} = \xi k^{+}$, we get
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\,[\rho,\rho]}^{6\, a}\right\rangle & = \mu^{2\epsilon}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}} d\xi\, \int \frac{d^{d}k}{(2\pi)^{d}} \, \frac{g^{3}\,N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{\sqrt{\pi}\xi k^{2}} \times \frac{1}{(4\pi)^{d/2}}\,\Gamma\left[1-\frac{d}{2}\right]\, \left(\frac{1}{\xi k^{2}}\right)^{1-d/2}\, \left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Expanding the $\Gamma$ function and setting $\epsilon \rightarrow 0$, we get
\begin{align}
\left|\Psi_{g\,[\rho,\rho]}^{6\, a}\right\rangle & = -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}} d\xi\, \int d^{2}k\, \frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{16\pi^{7/2}\,\xi k^{2}}\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{\xi k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right]\, \left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Integrating wrt $\xi$, we get
\begin{align}\label{onerhopart}
    \left|\Psi_{g\,[\rho,\rho]}^{6\, a}\right\rangle & = -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \int d^{2}k\, \frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{16\pi^{7/2}\, k^{2}}\,\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right]}\,\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon} + \log{\left[\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right]}\right]\,\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle\notag \\ & -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \int d^{2}k\, \frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\, k^{2}}\,\left[\log^{2}{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]} - \log^{2}{\left[\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right]}\right]\,\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle
\end{align}
%Simplifying we get
%\begin{align}
 %   \left|\Psi_{g\,[\rho,\rho]}^{6\, a}\right\rangle & = -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \int d^{2}k\, \frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\, k^{2}}\,\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}+\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}+\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\right]\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
%\end{align}
Lets look at 2 $\rho$ contribution
\begin{align}
\left|\Psi_{g\,\{\rho,\rho\}}^{6\, b}\right\rangle & = -\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, dp^{+}\, \int d^{2}k \, d^{2}p \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}k^{i}\{\rho^{b}(p),\rho^{c}(-k-p)\}}{16\pi^{9/2}p^{+}\left(\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}k^{2}+p^{2}\right)\,k^{2}}\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}
\end{align}
Introducing $\xi = \frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$, we get
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\,\{\rho,\rho\}}^{6\, b}\right\rangle & =-\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}}d\xi\, \int d^{2}k \, d^{2}p \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}k^{i}\{\rho^{b}(p),\rho^{c}(-k-p)\}}{16\pi^{9/2}\,\xi\,\left(\xi k^{2}+p^{2}\right)\,k^{2}}\, \left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Integrating wrt $\xi$ and doing $p\rightarrow -p$, we get
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\,\{\rho,\rho\}}^{6\, b}\right\rangle & =-\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \int d^{2}k \, d^{2}p \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}k^{i}\{\rho^{b}(-p),\rho^{c}(-k+p)\}}{16\pi^{9/2}\,k^{2}p^{2}}\,\left[\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}+\log{\left(\frac{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right) k^{2}+p^{2}}{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}} \right)k^{2}+p^{2}}\right)}\right]\,\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
\textcolor{red}{Setting $\Lambda = 0$, wherever possible} \textcolor{blue}{(nothing is set to zero: the next line is the previous one multiplied out)} 
\begin{align}\label{tworhoafter}
    \left|\Psi_{g\,\{\rho,\rho\}}^{6\, b}\right\rangle & =-\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \int d^{2}k \, d^{2}p \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}k^{i}\{\rho^{b}(-p),\rho^{c}(-k+p)\}}{16\pi^{9/2}\,k^{2}p^{2}}\,\left[\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}+\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda\, k^{2} + k^{+}\,p^{2}}{\vee k^{2}+k^{+}\,p^{2}}\right)}\right]\,\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Now at $\rho\rho\rho$ term
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{6\, c}\right\rangle & = -\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\, \int\, d^{2}k\, d^{2}p\, \frac{g^{3}\, k^{i}\rho^{b}(p)\rho^{b}(-p)\rho^{a}(-k)\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}{8\pi^{9/2}\,|p^{+}|^{2}\,k^{4}}\,\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle. 
\end{align}
Integrating wrt $p^{+}$, we get
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{6\, c}\right\rangle & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dk^{+}\, \int\, d^{2}k\, d^{2}p\, \frac{g^{3}\, k^{i}\rho^{b}(p)\rho^{b}(-p)\rho^{a}(-k)\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}{8\pi^{9/2}\,k^{4}}\, \left[\frac{1}{\vee}-\frac{1}{\Lambda}\right]\,\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
\textcolor{red}{Using the argument when $\vee$ is very large or $\Lambda$ is very small, we get} \textcolor{blue}{(X5) Not needed: keep $\frac{1}{\vee}-\frac{1}{\Lambda}$. It cancels exactly against the term $\frac{2k^{+}}{k^{2}}\big(\frac1\Lambda-\frac1\vee\big)$ of Eq.~\eqref{zetainteg}.}
\begin{align}\label{threerhoafter}
        \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{6\, c}\right\rangle & = -\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dk^{+}\, \int\, d^{2}k\, d^{2}p\, \frac{g^{3}\, k^{i}\rho^{b}(p)\rho^{b}(-p)\rho^{a}(-k)\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}{8\pi^{9/2}\,k^{4}\, \Lambda}\,\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Finally, we look into a term where a gluon emitted from the vacuum is absorbed back, and then another gluon is emitted from the vacuum. This term is a contribution of the norm from the first order perturbation theory.
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{Misha_2rho_loopVal3_single_g}
    \label{fig:3}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{degen}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{7}\right\rangle \, & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\, dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\:\left|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle \frac{\left|\left\langle g_{j}^{b}(p)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle \right|^{2}\left\langle g_{i}^{a}(k)\left|H_{g}\right|0\right\rangle (2E_{g}(p)+E_{g}(k))}{2E_{g}^{2}(k)E_{g}^{2}(p)}.
\end{align}
After inserting the relevant matrix element (Eq.~ \eqref{m1}) and changing the variables $\xi = \frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$, we get
\begin{align} \label{virtual}
  \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{7}\right\rangle & =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{g^{3}\mathbf{k}^{i}\rho^{b}(\mathbf{p})\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})}{16\pi^{9/2}\xi^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\,\left(\frac{\xi}{\mathbf{p}^{2}}\,+\,\frac{2}{\mathbf{k}^{2}}\right)\,\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Integration wrt $\xi$, we get
\begin{align} \label{zetainteg}
  \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{7}\right\rangle & =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{g^{3}\mathbf{k}^{i}\rho^{b}(\mathbf{p})\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})}{16\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\,\left[\frac{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}}{p^{2}}+\frac{2k^{+}}{k^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{\Lambda} - \frac{1}{\vee}\right)\right]\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle. 
\end{align}
\textcolor{red}{Using the argument when $\vee$ is very large or $\Lambda$ is very small, we get} \textcolor{blue}{(X5) Not needed; Eq.~\eqref{3rhosmom} follows exactly from Eqs.~\eqref{zetainteg} and the exact line before Eq.~\eqref{threerhoafter}.}
\begin{align}
     \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}^{7}\right\rangle & \approx \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{g^{3}\mathbf{k}^{i}\rho^{b}(\mathbf{p})\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})}{16\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\,\left[\frac{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}}{p^{2}}+\frac{2k^{+}}{k^{2}\,\Lambda}\right]\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})\right\rangle. 
\end{align}
 \subsection{Final results}
Now, we collect all the terms corresponding to one $\rho$, two $\rho$ and three $\rho$ components. Thus the NLO wave function is decomposed as follows
\begin{align}\label{NLOWF}
\left|\Psi^{\text{NLO}}\right\rangle & = \mf{N}\,\left|0\right\rangle +\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\right\rangle  
+\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}\right\rangle +\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\right\rangle +\left|\Psi_{g\rho}\right\rangle +\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}\right\rangle +
\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}\right\rangle .
\end{align}
where
\begin{equation}\label{lowav1mom}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{LO}\right\rangle \,=\- -\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g\, \mathbf{k}^{i}}{2\pi^{3/2}\sqrt{k^{+}}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\left|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle .
\end{equation}
Using the transformation equation (Eq.~ \eqref{i1}) we get the wave function in co-ordinate space.
\begin{align}\label{lowav1}
\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{LO}\right\rangle \,& =\,\frac{ig}{2\pi^{3/2}}\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\,\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\int_{\mathbf{x},\, \mathbf{z}}\,\frac{ (x-z)^{i}}{(x-z)^{2}}\,\rho^{a}(\mathbf{x})\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{z})\right\rangle .
 \end{align}
$\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}\right\rangle$ is obtained from \eqref{part 1}, \eqref{contgg}, \eqref{contgg3}
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{gg\rho}\right\rangle  & = \left|\Psi^{1a}_{gg[\rho,\rho]}\right\rangle + \left|\Psi^{2}_{gg\rho}\right\rangle + \left|\Psi^{3}_{gg\rho}\right\rangle,
\end{align}
\begin{align}\label{psiggrhomom}
    \left|\Psi_{gg\rho}\right\rangle  & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}}{8\pi^{3}\left(\mathbf{k}^{2}\xi(1-\xi)+\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{2}\right)\mathbf{k}^{2}}\notag \\
&\Bigg[\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}\left(\xi \mathbf{k}^{l}+\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{l}\right)\left((1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{j}-\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{j}\right)\left(\xi((1-\xi)\mathbf{k}-\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})^{2}-(1-\xi)(\xi \mathbf{k}+\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})^{2}\right)}{2\xi(1-\xi)(\xi \mathbf{k}+\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})^{2}((1-\xi)\mathbf{k}-\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})^{2}}+\frac{\delta^{jl}}{2}\left((1-2\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}-2\mathbf{k}\cdot\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}\right)+\frac{1}{\xi}\mathbf{k}^{j}\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{l}\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{1-\xi}\mathbf{k}^{l}\widetilde{\mathbf{p}}^{j}\Bigg] \left|g_{j}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}-\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})\, g_{l}^{c}(\xi k^{+},\,\xi\mathbf{k}+\widetilde{\mathbf{p}})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Rearranging
\begin{align}\label{psiggrhomomre}
    \left|\Psi_{gg\rho}\right\rangle  & = -\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k})\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}}{8\pi^{3}\left(\mathbf{k}^{2}\xi(1-\xi)+\mathbf{p}^{2}\right)\mathbf{k}^{2}}\notag \\
&\Bigg[\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}\left(\xi \mathbf{k}^{i}+\mathbf{p}^{i}\right)\left((1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{j}-\mathbf{p}^{j}\right)\left(\xi((1-\xi)\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}-(1-\xi)(\xi \mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})^{2}\right)}{2\xi(1-\xi)(\xi \mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})^{2}((1-\xi)\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}}+\frac{\delta^{ji}}{2}\left((1-2\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}-2\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}\right)+\frac{1}{\xi}\mathbf{k}^{j}\mathbf{p}^{i}\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{1-\xi}\mathbf{k}^{i}\mathbf{p}^{j}\Bigg] \left|g_{j}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})\, g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\,\xi\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
We can transform Eq.~ \eqref{psiggrhomom} into coordinate space using Eqs.~. \eqref{i2} and \eqref{i7}.
\begin{align}\label{ggrhopos}
 \left|\Psi_{gg\rho}\right\rangle  & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\int_{\mathbf{x},\, \mathbf{z},\, \mathbf{w}}\,\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(\mathbf{x})\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}}{8\pi^{3}\left((1-\xi)(x-w)^{2}+\xi (x-z)^{2}\right)}\Bigg(\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2(z-w)^{2}}\left((x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right)\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-w)^{i}(z-w)^{j}}{\xi (z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}(z-w)^{i}}{(1-\xi)(z-w)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-w)^{i}}{2\xi (x-z)^{2}} -\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-w)^{i}}{2(1-\xi)(x-w)^{2}}\Bigg)\left|g_{j}^{b}(\xi k^{+},\,\mathbf{z})\, g_{i}^{a}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,\mathbf{w})\right\rangle
\end{align}
$\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\right\rangle$ is obtained from \eqref{part 2}
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\right\rangle & \equiv \left|\Psi_{gg\{\rho,\rho\}}\right\rangle
\end{align}
\begin{align}\label{ggrhorhomom}
\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\right\rangle & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{g^{2}\mathbf{p}^{i}\left(\mathbf{k}^{j}-\mathbf{p}^{j}\right)\left\{ \rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\} }{16\pi^{3}\mathbf{p}^{2}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}}  \left|g_{j}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})\,g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\,\mathbf{p})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
%Rearranging, 
%\begin{align}\label{ggrhorhomomre}
%\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\right\rangle & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{g^{2}(\xi k^{i}+ \mathbf{p}^{i})\left(\mathbf{k}^{j}(1-\xi)-\mathbf{p}^{j}\right)\left\{ \rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p}-\xi k),\,\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{k}(1-\xi))\right\} }{16\pi^{3}(\xi k +\mathbf{p})^{2}(\mathbf{k}(1-
 %   \xi)-\mathbf{p})^{2}\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}} \notag \\ &  \left|g_{j}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\,\mathbf{k}(1-\xi)-\mathbf{p})\,g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\,\xi k + \mathbf{p})\right\rangle.
%\end{align}
We transform Eq.~ \eqref{ggrhorhomom} into coordinate space using Eq.~ \eqref{i1}. 
\begin{align} \label{ggrhorhopos}
    \left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\right\rangle & =  -\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}dk^{+}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\int_{\mathbf{x},\,\mathbf{y},\,\mathbf{z},\,\mathbf{w}}\,\frac{g^{2}\left\{ \rho^{a}(\mathbf{y}),\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{x})\right\} (y-z)^{i}(x-w)^{j}}{16\pi^{3}\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}(y-z)^{2}(x-w)^{2}}\left|g_{j}^{b}((1-\xi)k^{+},\mathbf{w})\, g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\mathbf{z})\right\rangle .
\end{align}
We split $\left|\Psi_{g\rho}\right\rangle$ into two parts: 
$$\left|\Psi_{g\rho}\right\rangle = \left|\Psi^{A}_{g\rho}\right\rangle + \left|\Psi^{B}_{g\rho}\right\rangle $$
$\left|\Psi^{A}_{g\rho}\right\rangle$ is obtained from \eqref{gtwoloop1}, \eqref{onerho2}, \eqref{onerhopart} and $\left|\Psi^{B}_{g\rho}\right\rangle$ is obtained from \eqref{4+5onerho} 
\begin{align}
     \left|\Psi^{A}_{g\rho}\right\rangle & =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\,k^{2}}\,\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right]\times\, \left[\frac{11}{3}+\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}-2\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\right]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},k)}\notag \\ & + \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\,k^{2}}\, \left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{3} - \frac{67}{9} +\log^{2}\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)-\log^{2}\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)\right]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},k)}
\end{align}
\begin{align}
 \left|\Psi^{B}_{g\rho}\right\rangle & = \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\mathbf{k}^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\sqrt{\xi}(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg(\left(\xi-2\right)\ln\left(\xi\right)+\left(\xi+1\right)\ln\left(1-\xi\right)-\left(\xi-2\right)\notag \\ & \left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\ln\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{\mu_{\overline{MS}}^{2}}\right)\right]\Bigg)\left|g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Adding we get
\begin{align}
     \left|\Psi_{g\rho}\right\rangle & =  \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\,k^{2}}\,\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right]\times\, \left[\frac{11}{3}+\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}-2\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\right]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},k)}\notag \\ & + \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\,k^{2}}\, \left[\frac{\pi^{2}}{3} - \frac{67}{9} +\log^{2}\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)-\log^{2}\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)\right]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},k)}\notag \\ & + \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\mathbf{k}^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\sqrt{\xi}(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg(\left(\xi-2\right)\ln\left(\xi\right)+\left(\xi+1\right)\ln\left(1-\xi\right)-\left(\xi-2\right)\notag \\ & \left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\ln\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{\mu_{\overline{MS}}^{2}}\right)\right]\Bigg)\left|g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
Combining and simplifying we get
\begin{align}\label{combinedgrho}
     \left|\Psi_{g\rho}\right\rangle & =  \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\,k^{2}}\,\Bigg[\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right]\times\, \left[\frac{11}{3}+\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}-2\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\right]\notag \\ & + \frac{\pi^{2}}{3} - \frac{67}{9} +\log^{2}\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)-\log^{2}\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)\Bigg] \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},k)}\notag \\ & + \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\mathbf{k}^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\sqrt{\xi}(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg[\left(\xi-2\right)\ln\left(\xi\right)+\left(\xi+1\right)\ln\left(1-\xi\right)-\left(\xi-2\right)\notag \\ & \left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\ln\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{\mu_{\overline{MS}}^{2}}\right)\right]\Bigg]\left|g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\right\rangle.
\end{align}
%\begin{align}
  %   \left|\Psi_{g\rho}\right\rangle & =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\,k^{2}}\,\Bigg\{\Bigg[\left(-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right)\times \left(\frac{11}{3}+\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\right)-\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\notag \\ & -\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+2\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}+\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}+\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\right] \Bigg]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}\notag \\ & +\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{1}{\sqrt{\xi}(1-\xi)}\Bigg(\left(\xi-2\right)\ln\left(\xi\right)+\left(\xi+1\right)\ln\left(1-\xi\right)+\left(\xi-2\right) \left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\ln\left(\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{\mu_{\overline{MS}}^{2}}\right)\right]\Bigg)\left|g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\right\rangle\Bigg\} 
%\end{align}

%    \left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\right\rangle

  %  \left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\right\rangle

 We transform Eq.~ \eqref{combinedgrho} into coordinate space using Eqs.~. \eqref{i1} and \eqref{i8}.
 \begin{align}\label{grhoposition}
     \left|\Psi_{g\rho}\right\rangle & = -\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int_{x,z} \frac{ig^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(x)(x-z)^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\,\bigg[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}-2\gamma -\log{\left(\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,\Bar{\mu}_{\mathrm{MS}}^{2}}{4}\right)}\bigg]\times\notag \\ & \bigg[\frac{11}{3}+ \log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}+\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1\right]}-\log\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)\bigg]\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{z})\right\rangle \notag \\ & -\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int_{x,z} \frac{ig^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(x)(x-z)^{i}}{32\pi^{7/2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\,\bigg[\frac{\pi^{2}}{3} - \frac{67}{9} + \log^{2}{\left[1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right]}+\log{\left[1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right]}\,\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]}\bigg]\, \left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{z})\right\rangle
\end{align}
{\color{blue}\noindent\textbf{Correction.} (X6) Written in the momentum $k^+$ of the outgoing gluon, the $\xi$ integral of the $\ket{\Psi^B_{g\rho}}$ part runs over $\frac{k^+}{\vee}<\xi<\frac{k^+}{k^++\Lambda}$ (not up to $1$ or $1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}$), and this part exists only for $k^+<\vee-\Lambda$. Exactly, for $\Lambda<k^+<\vee-\Lambda$ the two brackets become ($\delta=\Lambda/k^+$, $P\equiv-\frac2\epsilon-2\gamma-\ln\frac{(x-z)^2\bar\mu^2}{4}$)
\begin{align*}
&P\Big[\frac{11}{3}+\ln\frac{\Lambda^2}{k^{+2}-\Lambda^2}+\ln\Big(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee}\Big)-8\delta+2\delta^2-\frac43\delta^3\Big]+\frac{\pi^2}{3}-\frac{67}{9}+\ln^2\Big(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee}\Big)+\ln\Big(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee}\Big)\ln\frac{\vee}{k^+}+R_\delta,\\
&R_\delta=\frac{46}{3}\delta-\frac43\delta^2+\frac89\delta^3+\Big(\frac{22}{3}-8\delta+2\delta^2-\frac43\delta^3\Big)\ln(1-\delta)-\Big(8\delta-2\delta^2+\frac43\delta^3\Big)\ln\delta-2\ln\delta\ln(1-\delta)-4\,{\rm Li}_2(\delta)-\ln^2(1+\delta)+\ln(1+\delta)\ln\delta,
\end{align*}
(note $\frac{11}{3}+\ln\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}+\ln(\frac{\vee}{k^+}-1)-\ln\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}=\frac{11}{3}+2\ln\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}+\ln(1-\frac{k^+}{\vee})$). For $\vee-\Lambda<k^+<\vee$ only $\ket{\Psi^A_{g\rho}}$ contributes:
$P\big[\frac{11}{3}-8\delta+2\delta^2-\frac43\delta^3+\ln\frac{\delta}{1-\delta}-2\ln\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\big]+\frac{\pi^2}{3}-\frac{67}{9}+\ln^2\delta-\ln^2\frac{\vee}{k^+}+R_\delta+\ln^2(1+\delta)-\ln(1+\delta)\ln\delta$.}

$\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}\right\rangle$ is obtained from \eqref{grho3}, \eqref{grhorho4}, \eqref{tworho2}, \eqref{4+5tworhosimpl},\eqref{tworhoafter}
\begin{align}\label{combinedgrhorho}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}\right\rangle & =  \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\}}{32\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{p}^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg\{\Bigg[\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\left[\frac{k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}-\frac{(k\cdot p)\,k^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]-2k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\notag \\ & -2k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda\,k^{2} + k^{+}p^{2}}{\vee\, k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}}\right)}\Bigg]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})} \notag \\ & -\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{\xi^{3/2}}{\left(\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}+(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}\right)(1-\xi)}\Bigg[\left(\frac{2}{\xi}\left(\mathbf{p}^{2}-\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}\right)-\mathbf{p}^{2}+2\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}\right)\mathbf{k}^{i} +\frac{1}{\xi}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{i}\Bigg]\left|g_{i}^{a}(\xi k^{+},\,\mathbf{k})\right\rangle \Bigg\} 
\end{align}
%Using $p^{+} = \xi k^{+}$, we get 
%\begin{align}\label{combinedgrhorho}
  %  \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}\right\rangle & =  \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\}}{32\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{p}^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg\{\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\left[\frac{k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}-\frac{k\cdot p\,k^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]-2\,k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\notag \\ & -2\,k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}p^{2}}{\vee\, k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}}\right)}\notag \\ &  -\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{\xi}{\left(\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}+(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}\right)(1-\xi)}\Bigg[\left(\frac{2}{\xi}\left(\mathbf{p}^{2}-\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}\right)-\mathbf{p}^{2}+2\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}\right)\mathbf{k}^{i} +\frac{1}{\xi}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{i}\Bigg] \Bigg\} \,\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}
%\end{align}
Integrating wrt $\xi$ and applying the limits, we get the following \textcolor{blue}{(X7: the exact limits are $\frac{k^+}{\vee}<\xi<\frac{k^+}{k^++\Lambda}$ for the last line, which exists only for $k^+<\vee-\Lambda$. In the result below $\ln\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}\to\ln\frac{\Lambda}{k^+-\Lambda}$ in the first term (X4), $-k^{i}\ln\frac{\vee}{k^+}\to-k^{i}\ln\frac{\vee}{k^++\Lambda}$, and add $-\frac{(2p^{2}-2\,k\cdot p+k^{2})k^{i}+k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\ln\big[1+\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}\frac{k^{2}}{(k-p)^{2}}\big]$.)}
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}\right\rangle & =  \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\}}{32\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{p}^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg[\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\left[\frac{k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}-\frac{(k\cdot p)\,k^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]-2k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\notag \\ & -2k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda\,k^{2} + k^{+}p^{2}}{\vee\, k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}}\right)}+\frac{(2p^{2}-2\,k\cdot p + k^{2})\,k^{i} + k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\, \log{\left[1+\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1\right)\frac{k^{2}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]}\notag \\ & - \frac{(p^{2}\,k^{i}+k^{2}\,p^{i})}{(k-p)^{2}}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee - k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}-k^{i}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\Bigg]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)}
\end{align}
By Fourier transform, we get the wave function in coordinate space
\begin{align}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}\right\rangle & =  \frac{1}{2\pi}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\v{z},\v{x},\v{y}}\, e^{i\v{k}\cdot (\v{z}-\v{y})} e^{i\v{p}\cdot (\v{y}-\v{x})}\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{b}(\mathbf{x}),\,\rho^{c}(\mathbf{y})\right\}}{32\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{p}^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg\{\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\left[\frac{k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}-\frac{k\cdot p\,k^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ & -2k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda\,k^{2} + k^{+}p^{2}}{\vee\, k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}}\right)}-2k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}+\frac{(2p^{2}-2\,k\cdot p + k^{2})\,k^{i} + k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\, \log{\left[1+\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1\right)\frac{k^{2}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]}\notag \\ & - \frac{(p^{2}\,k^{i}+k^{2}\,p^{i})}{(k-p)^{2}}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee - k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}-k^{i}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\Bigg]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},z)}
\end{align}
\textcolor{red}{Setting $\Lambda $ to be $0$ wherever possible, we get} \textcolor{blue}{(X7) Not needed: keep $\log\frac{\Lambda k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}}{\vee k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}}$ as in the line above.}
\begin{align}\label{combinedgrhorhopos}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}\right\rangle & =  \frac{1}{2\pi}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\int_{\v{z},\v{x},\v{y}}\, e^{i\v{k}\cdot (\v{z}-\v{y})} e^{i\v{p}\cdot (\v{y}-\v{x})}\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{b}(\mathbf{x}),\,\rho^{c}(\mathbf{y})\right\}}{32\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{p}^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg[\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\left[\frac{k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}-\frac{k\cdot p\,k^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ & -2k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}p^{2}}{\vee\, k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}}\right)}-2k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}+\frac{(2p^{2}-2\,k\cdot p + k^{2})\,k^{i} + k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\, \log{\left[1+\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1\right)\frac{k^{2}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]}\notag \\ & - \frac{(p^{2}\,k^{i}+k^{2}\,p^{i})}{(k-p)^{2}}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee - k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}-k^{i}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\Bigg]\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},z)}
\end{align}

%In co ordinate space we get
%\begin{align}
%    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho}\right\rangle & = -\int\, \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int_{x,y,z,z'}\, \frac{g^{3}\,f^{abc}\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}}{16\pi^{9/2}}\,\Bigg\{\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\bigg[\frac{(y-z')\cdot (z-z')\, (x-z)^{i}}{(y-z')^{2}\, (z-z')^{2}\, (x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(y-z')\cdot (x-z')\, (z-z')^{i}}{(y-z')^{2}\, (x-z')^{2}\, (z-z')^{2}}\notag \\ & - \frac{(x-z')\cdot (z-z')\, (y-z)^{i}}{(x-z')^{2}\, (z-z')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z')\cdot (y-z')\, (z-z')^{i}}{(x-z')^{2}\, (y-z')^{2}\, (z-z')^{2}}\bigg] -\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\bigg[\frac{(x-z')\cdot (y-z')\, (y-z)^{i}}{(x-z')^{2}\,(y-z')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(y-z')\cdot (x-z')\, (x-z)^{i}}{(y-z')^{2}\,(x-z')^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\bigg]\Bigg\}\,\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{z})}\notag \\ &  - \int\, \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\frac{g^{3}\,f^{abc}\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}}{32\pi^{7/2}}\, \left[\frac{(y-z)^{i}}{(y-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)^{i}}{(x-z)^{2}}\right]\times \log{\left[\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}\right]}\,\log{\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}\right]}\,\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{z})} \notag \\ & -\int \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\Bigg\{ \int\, \frac{d^{2}k}{2\pi}\, d^{2}p\, \int_{x,y,z}\, e^{ik\cdot (z-x)}\, e^{ip\cdot (x-y)}\, \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}}{16\pi^{9/2}}\, \frac{k^{i}}{k^{2}\,p^{2}}\, \log{\left[\frac{p^{2}}{k^{2}}\right]}-x\leftrightarrow y \Bigg\}\,\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{z})}\notag \\ & + \int\, \frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \Bigg\{ \int\, \frac{d^{2}k}{2\pi}\, d^{2}p\, \int_{x,y,z}\,e^{ik\cdot (z-y)}\, e^{ip\cdot (y-x)}\, \frac{ig^{3}\,f^{abc}\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}}{16\pi^{9/2}}\, \Bigg[\frac{k^{i}}{p^{2}k^{2}}\,\log{\left(1+\frac{\vee\, k^{2}}{k^{+}\, p^{2}}\right)}\Bigg]-x\leftrightarrow y\Bigg\}\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{z})}
%\end{align}
$\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}\right\rangle$ is obtained from \eqref{threerhoafter} and \eqref{zetainteg}
\begin{align}\label{3rhosmom}
    \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}\right\rangle & = \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int d^{2}\mathbf{k}\, d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{g^{3}\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{p})\,\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\, \mathbf{k}^{i}}{16\pi^{9/2}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{2}}\, \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})}
\end{align}
We transform Eq.~\eqref{3rhosmom} into co-ordinate space using Eq.~\eqref{i1}
\begin{align}\label{3rhospos}
 \left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}\right\rangle & =  -\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\frac{dk^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\int_{\mathbf{w},\, \mathbf{x},\, \mathbf{y},\, \mathbf{u},\, \mathbf{z}}\frac{ig^{3}\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{x})\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{y})\,\rho^{a}(\mathbf{w})\, (x-u)\cdot (y-u)\,(w-z)^{i}}{16\pi^{9/2}(x-u)^{2}(y-u)^{2}(w-z)^{2}}\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{z})\right\rangle .
\end{align}
Normalization condition for vacuum incoming state is given as
\begin{align}
    \mf{N} & = 1-\frac{1}{2}\Bigg[\left\langle \Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\right.\right\rangle + \left\langle \Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\left|\Psi_{g\rho}\right.\right\rangle +\left\langle \Psi_{g\rho}\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\right.\right\rangle +\,\left\langle \Psi_{g\:\rho}^{LO}\left|\Psi_{g\:\rho\rho}\right.\right\rangle \,+\,\left\langle \Psi_{g\rho\rho}\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\right.\right\rangle \,+\,\left\langle \Psi_{gg\rho}\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}\right.\right\rangle \,+\,\left\langle \Psi_{gg\rho}\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\right.\right\rangle \notag \\ & +\,\left\langle \Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\left|\Psi_{gg\rho}\right.\right\rangle\,+\,\left\langle \Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\left|\Psi_{gg\rho\rho}\right.\right\rangle \,+\,\left\langle \Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\left|\Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}\right.\right\rangle \,+\,\left\langle \Psi_{g\rho\rho\rho}\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\right.\right\rangle \textcolor{red}{\Bigg)}\textcolor{blue}{\Bigg]}\,\textcolor{red}{-\,\frac{1}{8}}\textcolor{blue}{+\,\frac{3}{8}}\left\langle \Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\right.\right\rangle \left\langle \Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\left|\Psi_{g\rho}^{\text{LO}}\right.\right\rangle,
\end{align}
Since we are looking for terms that contribute to single gluon production and to the vacuum expectation value of the Hamiltonian in the dressed state, we will require the normalization only up to order $g^{2}$.
\begin{align}\label{normmom}
    \mf{N} & = 1- \frac{g^2}{8 \pi^3 }\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} dp^{+}\,
  \int d^2p\, \frac{\rho^a(-\v{p}) \rho^a(\v{p})}{p^{+}\v{p}^2}
\end{align}
Integrating wrt $p^{+}$
\begin{align}
\mf{N} & =  
 1 - \frac{g^2}{8 \pi^3 }\,\ln\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)\,
\int d^2p \frac{\rho^a(-\v{p}) \rho^a(\v{p})}{\v{p}^2}
\end{align}
By performing Fourier transform we get the normalization coefficient as
\begin{align}\label{normpos}
\mf{N} =  
 1 - \frac{g^2}{8 \pi^3 }\,\ln\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)\, 
  \int_{\v{x}, \v{y},\v{z}} \frac{X\cdot Y}{X^2 Y^2}   \rho^a(\v{x}) \rho^a(\v{y})
\end{align}
\section{Calculation of the single gluon incoming state}\label{sec:singleglue}
\subsection{No outgoing gluon}
To order $g^2$, only one diagram contributes: the absorption of the incoming gluon by the valence $\rho$: 
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{g_tovacum}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}%
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{\rho} }   & =
   \ket{0}\frac{\bra{0}H_{g}\ket{g^a_{i}(k)}}{E_{g}(k)}.
\end{align}   
Using the matrix elements from Eq.~ \eqref{m1},
\begin{align}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{\rho} }   & =     \frac{g}{ 2\pi^{3/2}}\frac{k^i \rho^{a}(\v{k})}{\sqrt{k^+} \v{k}^{2}} \ket{0},
 \end{align}
Here we used he LCPT transition element of Eq.~~\eqref{m1}. The vertical dashed line in the diagram denotes the intermediate state. In the transverse position space, we obtain
\begin{equation}
  \ket{  \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z}') \right)_{\rho} }  = \frac{ig}{2\pi^{3/2}\sqrt{ k^+}}\int_{x}\frac{X^{\prime i}}{X^{\prime 2}} \rho^{a}(\v{x})\ket{0}\,, 
\end{equation}
with the help of Eq.~\eqref{i1}. 
\subsection{One outgoing gluon}
\subsubsection{Single $\rho$ part; instantaneous contributions}
We start with the instantaneous interactions and   first consider  the process when the plus momentum flows from the valence degree of freedom. 
\begin{figure}[h]
     \tikzfig{gtog_inst}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{oneginst1}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }
   & = \int_{k^{+}+\Lambda}^{\vee}\,
   d p^+ \int d^2p %
   \ket{g_{j}^{b}(p)} 
   \frac{ \bra{g_{j}^{b}(p)}H_{gg\,\text{inst.}}\ket{g_{i}^{a}(k)}}{E_{g}(k)-E_{g}(p)}
\end{align}
Using the matrix elements from Eq.~ \eqref{m5}
\begin{align}\label{oneginst11}
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }   & = i \frac{g^2 f^{abc}}{(2\pi)^3} 
   \int_{k^+ + \Lambda}^{\vee} %
   d p^+ \int d^2p %
   \frac{\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}} (k^++p^+) } {(p^+-k^+)^2} \frac{\rho^c(-\v{p}+\v{k})}{p^{+}\v{k}^2 - k^{+}\v{p}^2} \delta_{ij}
   \ket{g_{i}^{b}(p^{+},\v{p})}
\end{align}
Using $p^{+} = \xi k^{+}$,
\begin{align}
 \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }   & = i \frac{g^2 f^{abc}}{(2\pi)^3} 
   \int_{ 1 + \frac{\Lambda}{k^+}   }^{  \frac{\vee}{k^+} } %
   d \xi \int d^2p %
   \frac{\sqrt{\xi} (1+\xi) } {(1-\xi)^2} \frac{\rho^c(-\v{p}+\v{k})}{\xi \v{k}^2 - \v{p}^2} 
   \ket{g_{i}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{p})} 
\end{align}
Performing Fourier transform with the help of Eq.~\eqref{i3}, we get 
\begin{align}%
  \label{Eq:onegluoninstX1}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z}') \right)^{1}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }%
   &= 
   i \frac{g^2 f^{abc}}{(2\pi)^3}\, \int_{ 1 + \frac{\Lambda}{k^+}   }^{  \frac{\vee}{k^+} } %
   d \xi  \int_{x,z} %
   \frac{\sqrt{\xi} (1+\xi) } {(1-\xi)^2} \frac{\rho^c(\v{x})}{\xi X^{2} - X^{'2} } 
   \ket{g_{i}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{z})} 
\end{align}
Note that the integration region over the ratio $\xi = p^+/k^+$ is not conventional, as $\xi>1$. Similar for the  momentum flowing into the valence degree of freedom we get identical expression modulo the integration limits:
\begin{figure}
\tikzfig{kinem}
\caption{Kinematic constraint on soft gluon plus momentum. The intersection of shaded regions defines allowed range of integration with respect to $p^+$.
  The red region is due to constrain $ \Lambda < p^+ - k^+ < \vee $, the green region --  $ \Lambda < p^+  < \vee $
and  $ \Lambda <  k^+ < \vee$.
}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{oneginst2}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }%
   &= i \frac{g^2 f^{abc}}{(2\pi)^3} 
   \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda} {k^{+}}} d \xi %
   \int d^2p %
   \frac{\sqrt{\xi} (1+\xi) } {(1-\xi)^2} \frac{\rho^c(-\v{p}+\v{k})}{\xi \v{k}^2 - \v{p}^2} 
   \ket{g_{i}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{p})} 
\end{align}
and 
\begin{align}%
 \label{Eq:onegluoninstX2}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z}') \right)^{2}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }%
   &= 
   i \frac{g^2 f^{abc}}{(2\pi)^3} \int_{ \frac{\Lambda}{k^+}   }^{ 1- \frac{\Lambda  }{k^+} } %
   d \xi \int_{x,z} %
   \frac{\sqrt{\xi} (1+\xi) } {(1-\xi)^2} \frac{\rho^c(\v{x})}{\xi X^{2} - X^{'2} } 
   \ket{g_{i}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{z})} 
\end{align}
\subsubsection{Single $\rho$ part}
Non-instantaneous interaction also leads to two possible contribution to $g^2$ order. We consider first the diagram when the valence particle transfers plus momentum to the gluon 
(see Eq.~\eqref{m3} for three gluon interaction vertex)
\begin{figure}[h]
\tikzfig{gandgtog}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{onegrho1}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho} }%
   & =
   \int d^3 r d^3p d^3q %
   \ket{g^{b}_{j}(p)}
   \frac{ \bra{g^{b}_{j}(p)}H_{ggg}\ket{g^{c}_{l}(q)g^{d}_{m}(r)}\bra{g^{c}_{l}(q)g^{d}_{m}(r)}H_{g}\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)}}
   {2(E_{g}(k)-E_{gg}(q,r))(E_{g}(k)-E_{g}(p))}
   \end{align}
   Using the matrix element from Eqs.~. \eqref{m2} and \eqref{m3}
   \begin{align}\label{onegrho12}
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho} }   & = \textcolor{red}{-}\textcolor{blue}{+}\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}}{8\pi^{3}}\,\int_{k^{+}+\Lambda}^{\vee} dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}p\,\frac{\rho^{c}(k-p)(p-k)^{l}}{\sqrt{p^{+}(p^{+}-k^{+})k^{+}}(p^{+}-k^{+})^{3/2}} \frac{1}{\bigg(\frac{k^{2}}{k^{+}}-\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}\bigg)\frac{(k-p)^{2}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}}\Bigg[\bigg(2k^{j}-p^{j}+\frac{p^{+}-2k^{+}}{p^{+}}p^{j}\bigg)\notag \\ & \delta_{il} +\bigg(2p^{i}-k^{i}-\frac{2p^{+}-k^{+}}{k^{+}}k^{i}\bigg)\delta_{jl}+\bigg(\frac{p^{+}+k^{+}}{p^{+}-k^{+}}(p-k)^{l} - k^{l} - p^{l}\bigg)\delta_{ij}\Bigg]\,\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p)}
\end{align}
Using $\xi = \frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$, we get
\begin{align}
 \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho} } &  = - \frac{ig^2 f^{abc}} {4 \pi^3}
