   \int_{1+ \frac{\Lambda}{k^+}   }^{\frac{\vee}{k^+}} %
   d \xi 
   \int d^2p \,
   \frac{\sqrt{\xi}}{1-\xi} \frac{(p^m-k^m) (p^l -\xi k^l)} {(\v{p}-\v{k})^2 (\v{p}^2 - \xi \v{k}^2)}
   \rho^c(-(\v{p}-\v{k}))\,\left( 
  \delta_{il} \delta_{jm}
  - \frac{1}{\xi}\delta_{jl} \delta_{im}
  - \frac{1}{1-\xi}\delta_{ij} \delta_{lm}
   \right)\notag \\ & \ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+,\v{p})}
\end{align}
Performing the Fourier transform with help of Eq.~\eqref{i4}, we obtain
\begin{align}%
\label{Eq:onegrhoX1}
  \notag
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i} ( k^+, \v{z}') \right)^{1}_{g \rho} }%
   \notag
   &=
   \textcolor{red}{+}\textcolor{blue}{-}\frac{ig^2 f^{abc}} {4 \pi^3} 
   \int_{1+ \frac{\Lambda}{k^+}   }^{\frac{\vee}{k^+}} %
   d \xi 
   \int_{x,z}\,
   \frac{\sqrt{\xi}}{1-\xi} 
   \frac{X^{'m} - \xi X^m} {X^{'2} - \xi X^2}
   \frac{X^{'l} -  X^l } {(X^{'} -  X)^2}
   \rho^c(\v{x})
  \notag \\ & \times \left( 
  \delta_{il} \delta_{jm}
  - \frac{1}{\xi}\delta_{jl} \delta_{im}
  - \frac{1}{1-\xi}\delta_{ij} \delta_{lm}
   \right)\,\ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+,\v{z})}
\end{align}
The process when gluon transfers plus momentum two the valence degree of freedom gives 
\begin{figure}[h]
\tikzfig{gto3g_torho}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{onegrho2}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho} }%
   & =  \int d^{3}r \int d^{3}q \int d^{3}p
  \ket{g^{b}_{j}(p)}
  \frac{\bra{g^{b}_{j}(p)}H_{g}\ket{g^{c}_{l}(q)g^{d}_{m}(r)}\bra{g^{c}_{l}(q)g^{d}_{m}(r)}H_{ggg}\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)}}{2(E_{g}(k)-E_{gg}(q,r))(E_{g }(k)-E_{g}(p))}
\end{align}
Using the matrix element from Eqs.~. \eqref{m2} and \eqref{m3}
\begin{align}\label{onegrho22}
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho} }   &= \int_{\Lambda}^{k^{+}-\Lambda}dp^{+}\,\int d^{2}p\,\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}(k-p)^{l}\rho^{c}(k-p)}{8\pi^{3}\sqrt{k^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})p^{+}}(k^{+}-p^{+})^{3/2}}\frac{1}{\frac{k^{2}}{k^{+}}-\frac{(k-p)^{2}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}-\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}}\times \frac{1}{\frac{k^{2}}{k^{+}}-\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}}\notag \\ & \Bigg[\bigg(2p^{i}-k^{i}+\frac{k^{+}-2p^{+}}{k^{+}}k^{i}\bigg)\delta_{jl} +\bigg(2k^{j}-p^{j}-\frac{2k^{+}-p^{+}}{p^{+}}p^{j}\bigg)\delta_{il}+\bigg(\frac{k^{+}+p^{+}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}\textcolor{red}{+}(k-p)^{l}-p^{l}-k^{l}\bigg)\delta_{ij}\Bigg]\,\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p)}
\end{align}
Having $p^{+} = \xi k^{+}$,
\begin{align}
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho} }   & = \frac{i g^2 f^{abc}} {4\pi^3} 
   \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}   }^{ 1- \frac{\Lambda }{k^+} } %
   d \xi 
  \frac{\xi^{3/2}}{1-\xi} 
   \int d^{2}p\,
  \frac{(k^m - p^m) (p^l - \xi k^l)} {(\v{p}^2-\xi \v{k}^2) (\v{p} - \xi \v{k})^2}
   \rho^c(\v{k}-\v{p})
  \notag \\ & \times 
  \left(\delta_{il} \delta_{jm} - \frac{1}{\xi} \delta_{jl} \delta_{im} - \frac{1}{1-\xi} \delta_{lm}\delta_{ij} \right)\,\ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+,\v{p})}
\end{align}
Using Eq.~\eqref{i5}, we do the Fourier transform
\begin{align}%
\label{Eq:onegrhoX2}
  \notag
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z}') \right)^{2}_{g \rho} }%
   &= - \frac{i g^2 f^{abc}} {4\pi^3} 
   \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^+}   }^{ 1- \frac{\Lambda }{k^+} } %
   d \xi 
  \frac{\xi^{3/2}}{1-\xi} 
   \int_{x,z}
   \frac{X^{'l} - X^l} {X^{'2} - \xi X^2}
   \frac{X^{'m} -  \xi X^m} {(X^{'} -  \xi X)^2}
   \rho^c(\v{x})
  \notag \\ & \times 
  \left(\delta_{il} \delta_{jm} - \frac{1}{\xi} \delta_{jl} \delta_{im} - \frac{1}{1-\xi} \delta_{lm}\delta_{ij} \right)\,\ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+,\v{z})}
\end{align}

In all four contributions, one might get worried about the presence of the $\frac{1}{(1-\xi)^2}$ singularity. Although, it is outside of the integration limits of $\xi$, the limits do pinch it leading to a potentially dangerous contribution proportional to $\frac{k^+}{\Lambda}$. However, a simple analysis of Eqs.~.~\eqref{Eq:onegluoninstX1}  and \eqref{Eq:onegrhoX1}  (as well as Eqs.~.~\eqref{Eq:onegluoninstX2}  and \eqref{Eq:onegrhoX2}) near $\xi = 1$ demonstrates that this integration boundary singularity  $\frac{1}{(1-\xi)^2}$ cancels.   
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%5
\subsubsection{Two $\rho$ part}
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%
In the expressions below we explicitly remove contributions when the outgoing gluon is identical to the incoming gluon.  
\begin{figure}[h]
    \tikzfig{gtovactog_tworho}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho \rho} }%
   & =\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} 
   d p^+ \int d^2p %
   \ket{g_{j}^{b}(p^+, \v{p})}%
   \frac{\bra{g_{j}^{b}(p)}H_{g}\ket{0}\bra{0}H_{g}\ket{g_{i}^{a}(k)}}{E_{g}(k) (E_{g}(k) - E_{g}(p))}
   \label{m1grr}
\end{align}  
\begin{align}
 \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho \rho} }  & =  
   \frac{g^2}{4\pi^3}%
   \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}}
   d \xi\int d^2p\frac{k^{i}p^{j}\rho^{b}(-\v{p})\rho^{a}(\v{k})}{\sqrt{\xi}(\xi \v{k}^{2} -\v{p}^{2}) \v{k}^{2}}\ket{g_{j}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{p})}\,.
\end{align}
Here we used the matrix elements of Eq.~\eqref{m1}. To find the Fourier transform in the transverse coordinate we use Eq.~\eqref{i2}:  
\begin{align}%
   \label{m1grrX}
  \ket{  \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z'}) \right)^{1}_{g \rho \rho} }  %
   & = %
   \frac{g^2}{4\pi^3}%
    \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}}
   d \xi%
   \int_{x,y,z} \frac{{X'}^{i}Y^{j}\rho^{b}(y)\rho^{a}(x)}{\sqrt{\xi}(\xi Y^{2}-{X'}^{2}) {Y}^{2}}\ket{g_{j}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{z})}\,. 
\end{align} 
\begin{figure}[h]
     \tikzfig{gg_tworho}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}%
  \notag
\ket{  \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho \rho}} 
   & =  \frac{1}{2}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} d r^+ dq^+ dp^+ %
   \int d^2 r d^2q d^2p%
   \ket{g_{j}^{b}(p)}\,
   \frac{\bra{g_{j}^{b}(p)}H_{g}\ket{g_{l}^{c}(q),g_{m}^{d}(r)}\bra{g_{l}^{c}(q),g_{m}^{d}(r)}H_{g}\ket{g_{i}^{a}(k)}}{(E_{g}(k)-E_{gg}(q,r))(E_{g}(k)-E_{g}(p))}%
\end{align}
With the help of Eq.~\eqref{m2}, we obtain  
\begin{align}
 \ket{  \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho \rho}}  & = - \frac{g^2}{4\pi^3}%
   \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}} d \xi %
   \int d^{2}p 
   \sqrt{\xi} \frac{ k^{i}p^{j}\rho^{a}(\v{k})\rho^{b}(-\v{p}) }{ p^{2}(\xi \v{k}^{2}-\v{p}^{2}) }%
   \ket{g_{j}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{p})}%
   \label{m2grr}
\end{align}
Taking Fourier transform in the transverse space we get 
\begin{align}%
  \ket{  \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z'}) \right)^{2}_{g \rho \rho}}  %
   & = %
   -\frac{g^2}{4 \pi^3}%
   \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}} d \xi \sqrt{\xi}%
   \int_{x,y,z} \frac{{X'}^{i}Y^{j}\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{b}(y)}{(\xi Y^{2}-{X'}^{2}) {X}^{'2}}\ket{g_{j}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{z})}, 
   \label{m2grrX}
\end{align}
where again we applied Eq.~\eqref{i2}. In the case of the matrix elements with two $\rho$ we have an ambiguity due to the commutator~\footnote{In momentum space,
$   [\rho^{a}(\v{k}), \rho^b(-\v{p})] = i f^{abc} \rho^c(\v{k}-\v{p}) $. 
} 
\begin{align}
  [\rho^{a}(\v{x}), \rho^b(\v{y})] = i f^{abc} \rho^c(\v{x}) \delta^2(\v{x}-\v{y})\,
\end{align}
which reduces the number of valence degrees of freedom by one. We thus choose to represent 
\begin{align}
  \rho^{a}(\v{x}) \rho^b(\v{y}) =   \frac12 \{\rho^{a}(\v{x}), \rho^b(\v{y})\} + \frac12  [\rho^{a}(\v{x}), \rho^b(\v{y})]  
\end{align}
where the first term depends on two valence degrees of freedom and is irreducible. Therefore the sum of   $\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho \rho} }$ and  $\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho \rho} }$ can be rewritten as 
\begin{align}
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho \rho} }
+\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho \rho} }  = 
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \{\rho, \rho\} } }
+\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g [\rho, \rho] } }  
\end{align}
with 
\begin{align}\label{onegrho3mom}
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{g \{\rho, \rho\} } }
 = &  
 - \frac{g^2}{8\pi^3}%
  \int d^3p\frac{k^{i}p^{j}} {\v{k}^{2} \v{p}^2 }\frac{1}{\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}}}
  \{\rho^{a}(\v{k}),\rho^{b}(-\v{p}) \}
  \ket{g_{j}^{b}(p^+,\v{p})}\,
\end{align}
\begin{align}\label{onegrho3}
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{g \{\rho, \rho\} } }
 = &  
 - \frac{g^2}{8\pi^3}%
 \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}} \frac{d \xi } {\sqrt{\xi}   }
  \int d^2p\frac{k^{i}p^{j}} {\v{k}^{2} \v{p}^2 } 
  \{\rho^{a}(\v{k}),\rho^{b}(-\v{p}) \}
  \ket{g_{j}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{p})}\,
\end{align}
and 
\begin{align}\label{onegrho4mom}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{g [\rho, \rho] } }
 & = 
  \frac{ig^2 f^{abc}}{8\pi^3}\, \int\, \frac{dp^{+}}{\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}}}
 \int d^2p\frac{k^{i}p^{j}} {\v{k}^{2} \v{p}^2 }  \frac{\v{p}^2 k^{+}+ p^{+} \v{k}^2}{\v{p}^2 k^{+}- p^{+} \v{k}^2}  \rho^{c}(\v{k} -\v{p})\,  \ket{g_{j}^{b}(p^+,\v{p})}\,
\end{align}
\begin{align}\label{onegrho4}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{g [\rho, \rho] } }
 & = 
  \frac{ig^2 f^{abc}}{8\pi^3}%
 \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}}\, \frac{d \xi } {\sqrt{\xi}   }
 \int d^2p\frac{k^{i}p^{j}} {\v{k}^{2} \v{p}^2 }  \frac{\v{p}^2 + \xi \v{k}^2}{\v{p}^2 - \xi \v{k}^2}  \rho^{c}(\v{k} -\v{p})  \ket{g_{j}^{b}(\xi k^+,\v{p})}\,
\end{align}

Similarly in the coordinate space 
\begin{align}\label{gtworhoX}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z}') \right)_{g \{\rho, \rho\} } }
 = -\frac{g^{2}}{8\pi^{3}}\, \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}}\, \frac{d\xi}{\sqrt{\xi}}\int_{x,y,z}\frac{X^{\prime i} Y^{j}}{X^{\prime 2}Y^{2}}\{\rho^{a}(\v{x}),\rho^{b}(\v{y})\} \ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^{+},\v{z})}
\end{align}
and 
\begin{align}\label{gtworhoX2}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z}') \right)_{g [\rho, \rho] } }
 = -\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}}{8\pi^{3}}\, \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}}\, \frac{d\xi}{\sqrt{\xi}} \int_{x,z}\frac{X^{\prime i}X^{j}}{X^{\prime 2}X^{2}}\frac{(X^{\prime 2}+\xi X^{2})}{(\xi X^{2}-X^{\prime 2})}\rho^{c}(\v{x})\ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^{+},\v{z})}
\end{align}
\subsection{Two outgoing gluons}   
\subsubsection{$\rho$ independent part}
\begin{figure}[h]
\tikzfig{g_togg}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}\label{rhoindep}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg} } & =
   \int d^{3}p\, d^{3}q \, 
   \ket{g^{c}_{l}(q),g^{b}_{j}(p)}
   \frac{\bra{g^{c}_{l}(q),g^{b}_{j}(p)} H_{ggg} \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)}}{2(E_g(k)-E_{gg}(p,q))}
\end{align}
Using Eq.~\eqref{m3}, 
\begin{align}
 \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg} } & = \int d^{3}p\,\int d^{3}q\,\frac{igf^{abc}\delta^{(3)}(k-p-q)}{8\pi^{3/2}\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}q^{+}}\bigg(\frac{k^{2}}{k^{+}}-\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}-\frac{q^{2}}{q^{+}}\bigg)}\Bigg[\bigg(p^{i}-q^{i}+\frac{q^{+}-p^{+}}{k^{+}}k^{i}\bigg)\delta_{jl}+\bigg(k^{j}+q^{j}-\frac{k^{+}+q^{+}}{p^{+}}p^{j}\bigg)\delta_{il}\notag \\ & +\bigg(\frac{k^{+}+p^{+}}{q^{+}}q^{l}-p^{l}-k^{l}\bigg)\delta_{ij}\Bigg]  \ket{g^{c}_{l}(q),g^{b}_{j}(p)}
 \end{align}
 Simplifying we get 
 \begin{align}
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg} } &  = -\frac{ i g f^{abc}\sqrt{k^+}} {4\pi^{3/2}} 
  \int_{ \frac{\Lambda}{k^+} }^{1 -  \frac{\Lambda}{k^+} } d\xi  \sqrt{\xi (1-\xi)} 
   \int d^{2}p\,  
   \frac{p^m - \xi k^m  }{( \v{p}- \xi \v{k} )^2 } \left[ \delta_{im} \delta_{jl}
     - \frac{1}{\xi}\delta_{jm} \delta_{il}
   - \frac{1}{1-\xi} \delta_{lm}\delta_{ij}
 \right]\notag \\ & 
 \ket{g^{c}_{l}((1-\xi)k^+,\v{k}-\v{p}),g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+,\v{p})} 
 \,.
\end{align}
The Fourier transform can be obtained by shifting integration variable $p$ and using Eq.~\eqref{i1}: 
\begin{align}\label{gindeppos}
    \ket{\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^{+},z^{\prime})} & =  - \frac{g f^{abc}}{4\pi^{3/2}}\sqrt{k^+} \int d^2 z \int_{ \frac{\Lambda}{k^+} }^{1 -  \frac{\Lambda}{k^+} }  d\xi \sqrt{\frac{1-\xi} {\xi} }  \frac{(z-z^{\prime})^{m}}{(z-z^{\prime})^{2}}  \left[ \delta_{im} \delta_{jl}
     -\frac{1}{\xi}\delta_{jm} \delta_{il}
   -\frac{1}{1-\xi} \delta_{lm} \delta_{ij}
 \right] \notag \\ & \ket{g^{c}_{l}((1-\xi)k^{+},z),g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^{+},\frac{z^{\prime}-(1-\xi)z}{\xi})}
\end{align}
\subsubsection{One $\rho$ part}
\begin{figure}[h]
\tikzfig{gtog_plusg}
\end{figure}
\begin{align}
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg\rho} }
   & = \int d^{3}q\, d^{3}p 
   \ket{g^{c}_{l}(q),g^{b}_{j}(p)}
 \frac{\bra{g^{c}_{l}(q),g^{b}_{j}(p)}H_{g}\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)}}{2(E_{g}(k)-E_{gg}(p,q))}
\end{align}
Using the matrix elements from Eq.~ \eqref{m2}
\begin{align}
 \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg\rho} }   & = - \frac{g}{2\pi^{3/2}} \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dp^+}{\sqrt{p^+}}
   \int d^2 p \frac{p^j}{\v{p}^2} \rho^b(-\v{p})
   \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k),g^{b}_{j}(p)}
\end{align}
Using $\xi = \frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}$, we get
\begin{align}
\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg\rho} }   & = - \frac{g}{2\pi^{3/2}} \sqrt{k^+} \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^+} }^{\frac{\vee}{k^+} } \frac{d\xi}{\sqrt{\xi}}
   \int d^2 p \frac{p^j}{\v{p}^2} \rho^b(-\v{p})
   \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k),g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+,\v{p})}\,.
\end{align}
In the transverse coordinate space, this reads 
\begin{align}
  \notag
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z}') \right)_{gg\rho} }%
  & = \frac{ig}{2\pi^{3/2}} \sqrt{k^+} \int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^+} }^{\frac{\vee}{k^+} }\,\frac{d\xi}{\sqrt{\xi}}
   \int_{x,z} \frac{X^j}{X^2} \rho^{b}(x)
   \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^+,\v{z}'),g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+,\v{z})}\,.
\end{align}
(or)
\begin{align}%
  \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k^+, \v{z}') \right)_{gg\rho} }%
  & = \frac{ig}{2\pi^{3/2}}\,\int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\, \frac{dp^+}{\sqrt{p^+}}
   \int_{x,z} \frac{X^j}{X^2} \rho^{b}(x)
   \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^+,\v{z}'),g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+,\v{z})}\,.
\end{align}
\subsection{Normalization}
The final result for the single gluon wave function to order $g^2$ is given by the sum of contribution presented in the previous section, i.e. 
\begin{align}
  \ket{\Psi^{a}_{i}(k)}%
 & = \mf{N} \ket{ g_i^a(k) }%
 \\ \notag & + 
 \ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{\rho} }%
+\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg} }%
+\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg\rho} }%
\\ & +\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }%
+\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }%
+\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho} }%
+\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho} }%
+\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g\rho\rho} }%
+\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g\rho\rho} }. %.
\label{Eq:intergluon}
\end{align}
In the second (third) line of this equation, we list all contribution of order $g$ ($g^2$). 
The first line is proportional to non-interacting gluon state. The proportionality factor  $\mf{N}$  is to be determined from the normalization 
condition of the interacting gluon state: 
\begin{align}
  \bra{\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k')}%
  {\Psi^{a}_{i}(k)} \rangle% 
  = \delta^{a a'} \delta_{i i'} \delta^{3}(k-k'). 
\end{align}
For our purposes we need to determine  $\mf{N}$ only to $g^2$ order. Therefore, the number of terms contributing to normalization is rather limited, 
e.g. we cannot have a product of any terms withing the third line or the product of two states from the second and the third lines. The following terms cancel in the normalization 
 $$  \bra{g^{a'}_{i'}(k')}\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } } + \bra{g^{a'}_{i'}(k')}\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }+\bra{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))^{1}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)} +\bra{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))^{2}_{g \rho\ {\rm inst.} } }\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)}  = 0 $$
 $$\bra{g^{a'}_{i'}(k')}\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g\rho\rho} }+ \bra{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))^{1}_{g\rho\rho} }\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)} + \bra{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))_{\rho} }\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{\rho} } = 0$$
 $$\bra{g^{a'}_{i'}(k')}\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{1}_{g \rho} }+\bra{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))^{1}_{g \rho} }\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)} +\bra{g^{a'}_{i'}(k')}\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)^{2}_{g \rho} }+\bra{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))^{2}_{g \rho} }\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)} = 0$$
$$\bra{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))_{gg\rho} }\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg} } + \bra{(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k))_{gg} }\ket{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))_{gg\rho} } = 0 $$
$$\bra{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))_{gg} }\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg} } = -\frac{g^2N_c}{8\pi^3} \left(  2 \ln \bigg(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}} \bigg) + \frac{11}{6}  \right) \,\int \frac{d^2\tilde p}{\tilde p^2} = 0 $$
The remaining terms are from
\begin{align}
   & \bra{g^{a}_{i}(k)}\ket{(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k))^{2}_{g\rho\rho} } + \bra{(\Psi^{a'}_{i'}(k'))^{2}_{g\rho\rho} }\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)}  = -\frac{g^2}{4 \pi^3 \sqrt{k^+ k^{'+}}} \frac{k^{i} k^{'i'}}{\v{k}^2\v{k}^{'2'}} \rho^{a'} (-\v{k}') \rho^a (\v{k}) \notag \\ & \bra{(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k))_{gg\rho} }\ket{ \left(\Psi^{a}_{i}(k) \right)_{gg\rho} } = \frac{g^2}{4 \pi^3 \sqrt{k^+ k^{'+}}} \frac{k^{i} k^{'i'}}{\v{k}^2\v{k}^{'2'}}\rho^a (\v{k}) \rho^{a'} (-\v{k}')+ 
 \delta^{a a'} \delta_{i i'}  \delta^3(k-k')  
  \frac{g^2}{4 \pi^3 } \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee} \frac{dp^{+}}{p^{+}}\,
  \int d^2p \frac{\rho^a(-\v{p}) \rho^a(\v{p})}{\v{p}^2} \nonumber
\end{align}
Putting together and therefore the normalization coefficient is given by 
\begin{align}
\mf{N} & =  
 1 - \frac{g^2}{8 \pi^3 }\, \int_{\Lambda}^{\vee}\,\frac{dp^{+}}{p^{+}}
\int d^2p \frac{\rho^a(-\v{p}) \rho^a(\v{p})}{\v{p}^2}
\end{align}
(or)
\begin{align}
\mf{N} & =  
 1 - \frac{g^2}{8 \pi^3 }\,\ln\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)\,
\int d^2p \frac{\rho^a(-\v{p}) \rho^a(\v{p})}{\v{p}^2}
\end{align}
By performing Fourier transform we get the normalization coefficient as
\begin{align}
\mf{N} =  
 1 - \frac{g^2}{8 \pi^3 }\,\ln\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)\, 
  \int_{\v{x}, \v{y},\v{z}} \frac{X\cdot Y}{X^2 Y^2}   \rho^a(\v{x}) \rho^a(\v{y})
\end{align}
\newpage
\section{Parametrization of the Evolution operator $(\Omega)$}
To parameterize the unitary evolution operator $\Omega$ it is convenient to perform normal ordering of the creation-annihilation operators of the soft sector  
\begin{align}\label{Eq:OmegaExp}
    \Omega  & = \mf{N} \notag \\ & +
                \int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} 
                \bigg[ 
                   \mf{A}^{b}_{1j}(p)a^{b}_{j}(p) 
                +  \mf{A}^{b}_{2j} (p) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p)\bigg] \notag \\ & 
               +\int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \frac{d^3q}{(2\pi)^3} 
               \bigg[\mf{B}^{cb}_{1kj}(p,q) a^{b}_{j}(p)a^{c}_{k}(q) 
                   + \mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p,q) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p)a^{\dagger c}_{k}(q)
               + \mf{B}^{cb}_{3kj} (p,q) a^{\dagger c}_{k}(q)a^{b}_{j}(p)
               \bigg] \notag \\ & 
               + \int 
               \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \frac{d^3q}{(2\pi)^3} 
               \bigg[
                \mf{C}^{ dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q) a^{\dagger d}_{l}(q)  a^{\dagger c}_{k}(p-q)  a^{b}_{j}(p)
               + \mf{C}^{dcb}_{2lkj}(p,q) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p) a^{c}_{k}(p-q) a^{d}_{l}(q)\bigg] +\ldots
\end{align}
The symmetry of the combinations of the creation annihilation operators infers the following identities on the coefficients: 
\begin{align}
    \mf{B}^{cb}_{1kj}(p,q) &= \mf{B}^{bc}_{1jk}(q,p), \\   
    \mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p,q) &= \mf{B}^{bc}_{2jk}(q,p),  \\ 
    \mf{C}^{ dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q) & = \mf{C}^{ cdb}_{1klj}(p,p-q), \\  
    \mf{C}^{ dcb}_{2lkj}(p,q) & = \mf{C}^{ cdb}_{2klj}(p,p-q)\,.   
\end{align}
\begin{align}
    \Omega^{\dagger}  & = \mf{N} \notag \\ & +
                \int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} 
                \bigg[ 
                   \mf{A}^{\dagger b}_{1j}(p)a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p) 
                +  \mf{A}^{\dagger b}_{2j} (p) a^{b}_{j}(p)\bigg] \notag \\ & 
               +\int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \frac{d^3q}{(2\pi)^3} 
               \bigg[\mf{B}^{\dagger cb}_{1kj}(p,q) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p)a^{\dagger c}_{k}(q) 
                   + \mf{B}^{\dagger cb}_{2kj}(p,q) a^{b}_{j}(p)a^{c}_{k}(q)
               + \mf{B}^{\dagger cb}_{3kj} (p,q) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p)a^{c}_{k}(q)
               \bigg] \notag \\ & 
               + \int 
               \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \frac{d^3q}{(2\pi)^3} 
               \bigg[
                \mf{C}^{\dagger dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p) a^{c}_{k}(p-q)  a^{d}_{l}(q)
               + \mf{C}^{\dagger dcb}_{2lkj}(p,q) a^{\dagger d}_{l}(q) a^{\dagger c}_{k}(p-q)a^{b}_{j}(p) \bigg] +\ldots
\end{align}
Using the unitarity condition of $\Omega^{\dagger}\Omega = 1$, establish the relation between daggered and undaggered coefficients. We will keep only the terms required to obtain the coefficients at particular orders for the NLO cross section.
Note that coefficients have different orders in $g$, and some of them mix the orders, e.g., $\mf{A}_{1}$ contains both $g$ and $g^3$ contributions. For our calculation, we have to consider  $\Omega$ to $g^3$ order. However, we stress that not all possible $g^3$ order contributions are included. In this expansion, we kept only the coefficients that have two gluons after applying the evolution to the vacuum of a single gluon state. Higher gluon numbers do not contribute to the single gluon production cross-section. The coefficients $\mf{A}_{1}$, $\mf{A}_{2}, \mf{A}^{\dagger}_{1}$ and $\mf{A}^{\dagger}_{2}$ contain terms of order $g$ and $g^{3}$.  Thus, we express them according to their respective orders as follows:
\begin{align}
    \mf{A}^{b}_{1j}(p) &=  \mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{1j}(p) +  \mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{1j}(p)\\ 
    \mf{A}^{b}_{2j}(p) &=  \mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p) +  \mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p)
\end{align}
Similarly, we can write it for $\mf{A}^{\dagger}_{1}$ and $\mf{A}^{\dagger}_{2}$ as well.
For $\mf{N}$, we have 
\begin{align}
    \mf{N}  &=  \mf{N}^{(0)} +  \mf{N}^{(2)} = 1 +  \mf{N}^{(2)}\,. 
\end{align}
We will only have the terms that are required for computing single gluon production. 
\begin{align}
     \Omega^{\dagger}\Omega & = 1 = \mf{N}^{2} + \int \frac{d^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}} \mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{2j}(p)\mf{A}^{(1)\,b}_{2j}(p) \notag \\ & +\int \frac{d^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, a^{\dagger\, b}_{j}(p) \bigg[\mf{N}^{\dagger\, (2)}\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p)+\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1j}(p)\mf{N}^{(2)} + 2\int\frac{d^{3}q}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, c}_{2k}(q)\mf{B}^{cb}_{2\,kj}(p,q)+\int\frac{d^{3}q}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\mf{B}^{\dagger\, cb}_{3kj}(p,q)\mf{A}^{(1)\,c}_{2k}(q)\notag \\ & +2\int\frac{d^{3}q}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\mf{C}^{\dagger\, dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q)\mf{B}^{cd}_{2kl}(q,p-q)+\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p)+\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(3)\, b}_{1j}(p)+\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p)+\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1j}(p)\bigg]\notag \\ & + \int \frac{d^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{d^{3}q}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, a^{\dagger\, b}_{j}(p)a^{\dagger\, c}_{k}(q) \bigg[\mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p,q)+\frac{1}{2}\mf{A}^{(1)\, \dagger\, b}_{1j}(p)\mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{2k}(q)+\frac{1}{2} \mf{A}^{(1)\, \dagger\, c}_{1k}(q)\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p)+\mf{B}^{\dagger\, cb}_{1kj}(p,q)\notag \\ & +\mf{C}^{\dagger\, cbd}_{2kjl}(q+p,q)\mf{A}^{(1)\,d}_{2l}(q+p)\bigg] \notag \\ & 
     + \int \frac{d^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{d^{3}q}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, a^{\dagger\, b}_{j}(p)a^{ c}_{k}(q) \bigg[\mf{B}^{\dagger cb}_{3kj}(p,q)+\mf{B}^{bc}_{3jk}(q,p)+\mf{A}^{\dagger b}_{1j}(p)\mf{A}^{c}_{1k}(q) + \mf{A}^{\dagger c}_{2k}(p)\mf{A}^{b}_{2j}(q) \notag \\ & 
    + 2 \mf{A}^{\dagger d}_{2l}(q-p)\mf{C}^{bdc}_{1jlk}(q,p)
    + 2 
    \mf{C}^{\dagger cdb}_{1klj}
    (p,q)\mf{A}^{d}_{2l}(p-q) \bigg] \notag \\ & 
    + \int \frac{d^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \frac{d^{3}q}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, a^{\dagger\, d}_{l}(q)a^{\dagger\, c}_{k}(p-q)a^{b}_{j}(p)\bigg[\mf{C}^{\dagger\, dcb}_{2lkj}(p,q)+\mf{C}^{dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q)\bigg]\notag \\ & + \ldots
\end{align}

where we used the following identity
\begin{equation}
    \bigg[a^{a}_{i}(k),a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p)\bigg] = a^{a}_{i}(k)a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p) - a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p)a^{a}_{i}(k)= (2\pi)^{3}\delta^{ab}\delta_{ij}\delta^{(3)}(k-p)
\end{equation}
to normal order relevant operators and we  already took into account that $\mf{N} = \mf{N}^\dagger  $. These gives following five relation between the coefficients: 
\begin{align}
 \mf{N}^{2}  &= 1-  \int \frac{d^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}} \mf{A}^{\dagger b}_{2j}(p) \mf{A}^{b}_{2j}(p) \quad
 \text{or} \quad \mf{N}   = 1- \frac{1}{2} \int \frac{d^{3}p}{(2\pi)^{3}} \mf{A}^{b}_{1j}(p) \mf{A}^{\dagger\, b}_{1j}(p) \\ 
 \mf{A}^{(1)\, \dagger\,  b}_{1j}(p) & =  -\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p) \\  
 \mf{A}^{(3)\, \dagger\, b}_{1j}(p) & = -\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p)- \mf{N}^{\dagger\, (2)}\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p) + \mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p)\mf{N}^{(2)} +\int \frac{d^{3}q}{(2\pi)^{3}}\bigg[2\mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{1k}(q)\mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p,q)-\mf{B}^{\dagger\, cb}_{3kj}(p,q)\mf{A}^{(1)\,c}_{2k}(q)\notag \\ & -2\,\mf{C}^{\dagger\, dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q)\mf{B}^{cd}_{2kl}(q,p-q)\bigg] \\ 
 \mf{B}^{\dagger\, cb}_{1kj}(p,q) & = \frac{1}{2}\,\bigg[-2\,\mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p,q)+\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p)\mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{2k}(q)+ \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{2k}(q)\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p)+2\, \mf{C}^{cbd}_{1kjl}(q+p,q)\mf{A}^{(1)\,d}_{2l}(q+p)\bigg]
\\ 
  \mf{C}^{\dagger dcb}_{2lkj}(p,q) & =  -\mf{C}^{dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q)
\end{align}
\textcolor{red}{The fact that $A^{(1)}$ can at most be linear in $\rho$ at $g$ order we find} \textcolor{blue}{(E5) This is not correct: $\mf N^{(2)}\propto\int K(x,y)\rho^a(x)\rho^a(y)$ does not commute with $\rho^b(u)$, $[\int_{x,y}K\rho^a\rho^a,\rho^b(u)]=if^{abc}\int_xK(x,u)\{\rho^a(x),\rho^c(u)\}$. Keep $-\mf N^{\dagger(2)}\mf A^{(1)}_{2}+\mf A^{(1)}_{2}\mf N^{(2)}=-[\mf N^{(2)},\mf A^{(1)}_{2}]$ in the relation above; the two equations below and the simplified relation are to be removed.} 
\begin{align}    \color{red}\mf {N}^{(2)}\mf{A}^{(1)c}_{1k}(q) &\color{red}= \mf{A}^{(1) c}_{1k}(q) \mf {N}^{(2)} \\
    \color{red}\mf {N}^{(2)}\mf{A}^{(1)c}_{2k}(q) &\color{red}= \mf{A}^{(1) c}_{2k}(q) \mf {N}^{(2)}
\end{align}
\textcolor{red}{and thus
the relation for $\mf{A}^{(3)\, \dagger\, b}_{1j}(p)$ simplifies to:}
\begin{align}
 \color{red}\mf{A}^{(3)\, \dagger\, b}_{1j}(p) & \color{red}= -\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p) +\int \frac{d^{3}q}{(2\pi)^{3}}\bigg[2\, \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{1k}(q)\mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p,q)-\mf{B}^{\dagger\, cb}_{3kj}(p,q)\mf{A}^{(1)\,c}_{2k}(q)-2\,\mf{C}^{\dagger\, dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q)\mf{B}^{cd}_{2kl}(q,p-q)\bigg]
 \end{align}
 Partially implementing the constraints on the coefficients we get:
\begin{align}
    \Omega  & = \mf{N}  \notag \\ & +
                \int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} 
                \bigg[ 
                   \mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{1j}(p)a^{b}_{j}(p) 
                -  \mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1j} (p) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p)+ \mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{1j}(p)a^{b}_{j}(p) 
                +  \mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j} (p) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p)\bigg] \notag \\ & 
               +\int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \frac{d^3q}{(2\pi)^3} 
               \bigg[\mf{B}^{cb}_{1kj}(p,q) a^{b}_{j}(p)a^{c}_{k}(q) 
                   + \mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p,q) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p)a^{\dagger c}_{k}(q)
               + \mf{B}^{cb}_{3kj} (p,q) a^{\dagger c}_{k}(q)a^{b}_{j}(p)
               \bigg] \notag \\ & 
               + \int 
               \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \frac{d^3q}{(2\pi)^3} 
               \bigg[
                \mf{C}^{ dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q) a^{\dagger d}_{l}(q)  a^{\dagger c}_{k}(p-q)  a^{b}_{j}(p)
               - \mf{C}^{\dagger\, dcb}_{1lkj}(p,q) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p) a^{c}_{k}(p-q) a^{d}_{l}(q)\bigg] +\ldots
\end{align}
The coefficients $\mf{A}^{(1)}_{1}, \mf{A}^{(3)}_{2}, \mf{B}_{2}, \mf{B}_{3}, \mf{C}_{1}$ are obtained directly from our LCPT calculations. The other coefficients, such as $\mf{B}_{1}$ and $\mf{A}^{(3)}_{1}$ follow from the constraints. In the transverse coordinate space, we have
\begin{align}
    \Omega & = \mf{N} \notag \\ & +
                \int \frac{d p^+}{2\pi} 
             \int d^2 x  
                \bigg[\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{1j}(p^{+},x)a^{b}_{j}(p^{+},x) 
                -  \mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1j} (p^{+},x) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p^{+},x)+ \mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{1j}(p^{+},x)a^{b}_{j}(p^{+},x) 
                +  \mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j} (p^{+},-x) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p^{+},x)\bigg] \notag \\ & 
               +\int \frac{d p^+}{2\pi} \frac{d q^+}{2\pi} \int d^2x\, d^2y  
               \bigg[\mf{B}^{cb}_{1kj}(p^{+}, x;q^{+}, y) a^{b}_{j}(p^{+},x)a^{c}_{k}(q^{+},y) 
                   + \mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p^{+},-x; q^{+},-y) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p^{+},x)a^{\dagger c}_{k}(q^{+},y)
            \notag \\ & 
                  + \mf{B}^{cb}_{3kj} (p^{+},x; q^{+}, -y) a^{\dagger c}_{k}(q^{+},y)a^{b}_{j}(p^{+},x)\bigg] \notag \\ & 
               + \int 
               \frac{d p^+}{2\pi} \frac{dq^+}{2\pi} 
               \int d^2x\, d^2y\,  d^2 z\, 
               \bigg[\mf{C}^{ dcb}_{1lkj}(p^{+},z-y; q^{+},y-x) a^{\dagger d}_{l}(q^{+},x)  a^{\dagger c}_{k}(p^{+}-q^{+}, y)  a^{b}_{j}(p^{+},z)
               \notag \\ & 
               -  \mf{C}^{\dagger\, dcb}_{1lkj}(p^{+},z-y; q^{+}, y-x) a^{\dagger b}_{j}(p^{+},z) a^{c}_{k}(p^{+}-q^{+},y) a^{d}_{l}(q^{+},x)\bigg] + \ldots
\end{align}
Moreover, the coefficients that were constrained by the requirement of unitarity of the evolution operator, i.e., $\Omega^{\dagger}\Omega = \mathcal{I}$, are as follows in their respective transverse coordinate space. 
\begin{align}
    \mf{A}^{(1)\, \dagger\,  b}_{1j}(p^{+},z) & =  -\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p^{+},-z)
\end{align}
\begin{align}
 \mf{A}^{(3)\, \dagger\, b}_{1j}(p^{+},x) & = -\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p^{+}, -x) +\int \frac{dq^{+}}{2\pi}\, \int d^{2}y\, \bigg[2\, \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{1k}(q^{+}, y)\mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p^{+}, -x;\, q^{+},-y)-\mf{B}^{\dagger\, cb}_{3kj}(p^{+}, x; q^{+}, -y)\mf{A}^{(1)\,c}_{2k}(q^{+},-y)\notag \\ & -2\,\int\, d^{2}z\, \mf{C}^{\dagger\, dcb}_{1lkj}(p^{+},x-z; q^{+},z-y)\mf{B}^{dc}_{2lk}(p^{+}-q^{+},-z; q^{+},-y)\bigg]
\end{align}
\begin{align}
    \mf{B}^{\dagger\, cb}_{1kj}(p^{+},x; q^{+},y) & = \frac{1}{2}\, \Bigg[-2\,\mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p^{+},-x; q^{+},-y)+\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p^{+},-x)\mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{2k}(q^{+},-y)+ \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{2k}(q^{+},-y)\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(p^{+},-x)\notag \\ & +2\,\int\, d^{2}z\, \bigg[\mf{C}^{cbd}_{1kjl}(q^{+}+p^{+},z-x; q^{+},x-y)\mf{A}^{(1)\,d}_{2l}(q^{+}+p^{+},-z)\bigg]\Bigg]
\end{align}
\begin{align}
     \mf{C}^{\dagger\, dcb}_{2lkj}(p^{+},z; q^{+},x) & =  -\mf{C}^{dcb}_{1lkj}(p^{+}, -z; q^{+}, -x)
\end{align}
Where we used the common  convention for the Fourier transforms 
\begin{align}
    &a(k^+, k) = \int d^2z\, e^{-i k z} a(k^+,z), \\ 
    &a^\dagger(k^+, k) = \int d^2z\, e^{i k z} a^\dagger(k^+, z), \\ 
    & f (k) = \int d^2x\, e^{ikx} f(x)\,.
\end{align}
In order to find the coefficients, we compute the ``evolved'' wave function and match it with the LCPT wave functions. There are three relevant contributions 
\begin{itemize}
\item{\texttt{Action on vacuum}} 
\begin{align} \label{Omegaonvacmom1}
    \Omega\ket{0} & = \mf{N}\ket{0} \notag \\ &
    +(2\pi)^{3/2}\int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3}\bigg[\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p)
    -\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1j}(p)\bigg]\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p)} \notag \\ & 
    +(2\pi)^{3}\int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \frac{d^3q}{(2\pi)^3}  \mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p,q)\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p)g^{c}_{k}(q)}
\end{align} 
In the last line it is convenient to introduce alternative coordinates, $k^+  = p^+ + q^+$ and so  $\xi = \frac{p^+}{k^+}$. This will give us 
\begin{align} \label{Omegaonvacmomzeta}
    \Omega\ket{0} & = \mf{N}\ket{0} \notag \\ &
    + (2\pi)^{3/2}\,\int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3}\,
    \bigg[\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p)
    -\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1j}(p)\bigg]\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p)} \notag \\ &
    + 2\pi \int dk^+ k^+ \,\int d \xi\,  \int \frac{d^2p}{(2\pi)^2}\, \frac{d^2q}{(2\pi)^2}\,  \mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(\xi k^+, \v p;  (1-\xi) k^+, \v q) \ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+, \v p)g^{c}_{k}((1-\xi) k^+, \v q)}
\end{align} 
In transverse coordinate space, we get 
\begin{align} \label{Omegaonvacpos}
    \Omega\ket{0} & = \mf{N}\ket{0} \notag \\ &
    + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{1/2}}\int dp^{+} \int d^{2}x\,
   \bigg[\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p^{+}, - x)
   -\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1j}(p^{+},x)\bigg]\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p^{+},x)} \notag \\ & 
   +\frac{1}{2\pi}\int dp^{+}\,  dq^{+} \, \int d^{2}z\, d^{2}x\, \mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(p^{+},-z;q^{+},-x)\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p^{+},z)g^{c}_{k}(q^{+},x)}%(\xi k^{+},z)g^{c}_{k}((1-\xi)k^{+},x)}
\end{align}
Similarly, changing to the momentum fraction,  
\begin{align} \label{Omegaonvacposzeta}
    \Omega\ket{0} & = \mf{N}\ket{0} \notag \\ &
    + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{1/2}}\int dp^{+} \int d^{2}x\,
    \bigg[\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p^{+}, -x)
   -\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1j}(p^{+},x)\bigg]\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p^{+},x)} \notag \\ & 
 +\frac{1}{2\pi}\int dk^{+} k^+  \int d \xi  \int d^{2}z d^{2}x\, \mf{B}^{cb}_{2kj}(\xi k^{+},-z; (1-\xi) k^{+},-x)\ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^{+},z),\,g^{c}_{k}((1-\xi)k^{+},x)}
\end{align}
These relations will help us fix coefficients $ \mf{N}$, $\mf{A}^{(1)}$, $\mf{A}^{(3)}$ and $\mf{B}_{2}$. 
The remaining two coefficients can be fixed by considering $\Omega$ acting on a single gluon state. 
\item{\texttt{Action on single gluon state}} 
\begin{align}\label{Omegaongmom}
    \Omega\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)} & =  \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3/2}} \bigg[\mf{A}^{(1)\, a}_{1i}(k)+\mf{A}^{(3)\, a}_{1i}(k)\bigg]\ket{0} \notag \\ &
   + \mf{N}\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)} \notag \\ & 
    +\int  \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \mf{B} ^{ba}_{3ji}(k,p)\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p)} \notag \\ & 
    + (2\pi)^{3/2}\int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3}  \bigg[\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p) - \mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1j}(p)\bigg]  \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)g^{b}_{j}(p)} \notag \\ & 
    + (2\pi)^{3/2}\int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \mf{C}^{bca}_{1jki}(k,p)\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p)g^{c}_{k}(k-p)}
\end{align}
We ignore final states with three or more gluons since we only need terms with up to two gluons in the final state for our problem. As before it is convenient to introduce the momentum fraction  $p^{+} = \xi k^{+}$,
\begin{align}\label{Omegaongmomzeta}
    \Omega\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3/2}} \bigg[\mf{A}^{(1)\, a}_{1i}( k)+\mf{A}^{(3)\, a}_{1i}(k)\bigg]\ket{0} \notag \\ & 
   +  \mf{N}\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)} \notag \\ & 
    + \int \frac{d\xi}{2\pi}k^{+}\int  \frac{d^{2}p}{(2\pi)^2} \mf{B} ^{ba}_{3ji}(k^{+},k; \xi k^{+},p)\ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^{+},p)} \notag \\ & 
    + (2\pi)^{3/2}\int \frac{d\xi}{2\pi} k^{+}\int \frac{d^2p}{(2\pi)^2}  \bigg[\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(\xi k^{+}, p) - \mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1j}(\xi k^{+},p)\bigg]  \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},k)g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^{+},p)} \notag \\ & 
    + (2\pi)^{3/2}\int \frac{d\xi}{2\pi}\, k^{+} \int \frac{d^{2}p}{(2\pi)^{2}}\mf{C}^{bca}_{1jki}(k^{+}, k; \xi k^{+}, p)\ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^{+}, p)g^{c}_{k}((1-\xi)k^{+}, k-p)} %\notag  \\ & + (2\pi)^{3}\int \frac{d^3p}{(2\pi)^3} \frac{d^3q}{(2\pi)^3} \mf{B}^{\dagger\,cb}_{kj} (p,q) \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k)g^{b}_{j}(p)g^{c}_{k}(q)} 
    + \ldots 
\end{align}
In transverse coordinate space we get,
\begin{align}\label{Omegaongpos}
    \Omega\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},z)} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{1/2}} \bigg[\mf{A}^{(1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+},z)+\mf{A}^{(3)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+},z)\bigg]\ket{0} \notag \\ & 
   + 
   \mf{N}\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},z )} \notag \\ & 
    + \frac{1}{2\pi}\int  dp^{+}  \int d^2x \,\mf{B}^{ba}_{3ji}(k^+, z;p^+, -x)\ket{g^{b}_{j}(p^+, x)} \notag \\ & 
   + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{1/2}}\int dp^{+}  \int d^{2}x\,\bigg[\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(p^{+},-x) - \mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1j}(p^{+},x)\bigg]  \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+}, z)g^{b}_{j}(p^{+}, x)} \notag \\ & 
    + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{1/2}}\int dp^{+} \int d^{2}x d^{2}y\, \mf{C}^{bca}_{1jki}(k^{+}, z-y; p^{+}, y-x) \ket{g^{b}_{j}(p^{+}, x)g^{c}_{k}(k^{+}-p^{+}, y)}  + \ldots 
\end{align}
(or)
\begin{align}\label{Omegaongposzeta}
    \Omega\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},z)} & = 
    \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{1/2}} \bigg[\mf{A}^{(1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+},z)+\mf{A}^{(3)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+},z)\bigg]\ket{0} \notag \\ & + 
    \mf{N}\ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+},z )} \notag \\ & 
    + \frac{1}{2\pi}\int  d\xi \, k^{+}\, \int d^2x \,\mf{B}^{ba}_{3ji}(k^+, z;\xi k^+, -x)\ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^+, x)} \notag \\ & 
    + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{1/2}}\int d\xi\, k^{+}\, \int d^{2}x\, \bigg[\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2j}(\xi k^{+},-x) - \mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1j}(\xi k^{+},x)\bigg]  \ket{g^{a}_{i}(k^{+}, z)g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^{+}, x)} \notag \\ & 
    + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{1/2}}\int d\xi \, k^{+}\, \int d^{2}x\, d^{2}y\, \mf{C}^{bca}_{1jki}(k^{+}, z-y; \xi k^{+}, y-x) \ket{g^{b}_{j}(\xi k^{+}, x)g^{c}_{k}((1-\xi)k^{+}, y)}   + \ldots  
\end{align}
\end{itemize}
Note that the factors of $\pi$ are from the normalization of the creation annihilation operators, see Appendix~\ref{sec:defn}. We are now in the position to match the coefficients of the evolution $\Omega$ operator by comparing it with the light cone wave functions derived from the vacuum, and single gluon incoming states in the sections \ref{sec:vac} and \ref{sec:singleglue}. The constraints originating from the incoming vacuum state lead to the following relations. $\mf{N}$ is obtained by comparing the first line of Eq.~ \eqref{Omegaonvacmom1} with Eq.~ \eqref{normmom} for momentum space. Thus, we get
\begin{equation}\label{Ncoeffmom}
    \mf{N} =  \mf{N}_0 +  \mf{N}_2 = 1- \frac{g^{2}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\int\, d^{2}p\,\frac{\rho^{b}(-p)\rho^{b}(p)}{p^{2}}
\end{equation}
In transverse co-ordinates,
\begin{equation}\label{eq:Ncoeffpos}
    \mf{N}  = 1 -
    \frac{g^{2}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\, \int_{x,y,z}\frac{(x-z)\cdot (y-z)}{(x-z)^{2}(y-z)^{2}}\rho^{a}(x)\rho^{a}(y)
\end{equation}
where  $ \mf{N}_0=1$ and  $\mf{N}_2$ is the term proportional to $g^2$. $\mf{A}^{(3)}_{2}$ and $\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)}_{1}$ are obtained by comparing second line of Eq.~ \eqref{Omegaonvacmom1} with Eq.~ \eqref{lowav1mom} for momentum space. 
\begin{align}
\label{A1coeffM}
  -\,\mf{A}^{a}_{2i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k}) =  \mf{A}^{\dagger\,a}_{ 1 i}(k^{+},\mathbf{k}) & = \sqrt{2} \frac{g\,k^{i}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})}{ \sqrt{k^{+}}\,\mathbf{k}^{2}} 
\end{align}
In transverse co-ordinates,
\begin{align}
\label{A1coeff}
  -\, \mf{A}^{a}_{2i}(k^{+},\textcolor{blue}{-}\mathbf{z}) =   \mf{A}^{\dagger\,a}_{1 i}(k^{+},\mathbf{z}) & = - \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}\pi}\frac{ig}{\sqrt{k^+}}\int_{x}\frac{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})^{i}}{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})^{2}} \rho^{a}(\mathbf{x}) 
\end{align}
$ \mf{A}^{(3)}_{2}$ is obtained by comparing the second line of Eq.~ \eqref{Omegaonvacmom1} with Eq.~ \eqref{combinedgrho} and Eq.~ \eqref{combinedgrhorho} for momentum space. 
\begin{align}
     \mf{A}^{(3)\, a}_{2i}(k^{+},k) & = \text{\color{red}(remove)}\color{red}\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{8\sqrt{2k^{+}}\pi^{2}\,k^{2}}\,\Bigg[\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right]\times\, \left[\frac{11}{3}+\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}-2\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\right] + \frac{\pi^{2}}{3} - \frac{67}{9} +\log^{2}\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)-\log^{2}\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)\Bigg]\notag \\ & \text{\color{red}(remove)}\color{red}+\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{8\sqrt{2k^{+}}\pi^{2}\,k^{2}}\, \Bigg[\frac{\pi^{2}}{3} - \frac{67}{9} + \log^{2}{\left[1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right]}+\log{\left[1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right]}\,\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]}\Bigg]\notag \\ & \text{\color{blue}(E4, new)}\color{blue}+\frac{g^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(-k)k^{i}}{8\sqrt{2k^{+}}\pi^{2}\,k^{2}}\,\Bigg[\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}+\log{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}_{\overline{MS}}}\right)}\right]\left[\frac{11}{3}+\log\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}+\log\Big(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1\Big)-\log\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right]\notag \\ & \color{blue}\qquad +\frac{\pi^{2}}{3} - \frac{67}{9} + \log^{2}{\left[1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right]}+\log{\left[1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right]}\,\log{\left[\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right]}\Bigg]\quad\text{\color{blue}(exact form: X6)}\notag \\ & +\int d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\}}{8\sqrt{2}\pi^{3}\,\mathbf{p}^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}\bigg[\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\left[\frac{k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}-\frac{k\cdot p\,k^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right] -2\,k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\notag \\ &  -2\,k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}p^{2}}{\vee\, k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}}\right)}\bigg]\notag \\ &   - \int d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p}),\,\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{k}+\mathbf{p})\right\}}{8\sqrt{2}\pi^{3}\,\mathbf{p}^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}\int_{\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}^{1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}}d\xi\,\frac{\xi}{\left(\xi(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})^{2}+(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}\right)(1-\xi)}\notag \\ & \left[\left(\frac{2}{\xi}\left(\mathbf{p}^{2}-\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}\right)-\mathbf{p}^{2}+2\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{p}\right)\mathbf{k}^{i} +\frac{1}{\xi}\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{i}\right] \notag \\ & +\int d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,\frac{g^{3}\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{p})\,\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})\,\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})\, \mathbf{k}^{i}}{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{3}\,\mathbf{k}^{2}\mathbf{p}^{2}\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)} 
\end{align}
$ \mf{A}^{(3)}_{2}$ in coordinate space is obtained by comparing second line of Eq.~ \eqref{Omegaonvacposzeta} with Eq.~ \eqref{grhoposition}, Eq.~ \eqref{combinedgrhorhopos} and Eq.~ \eqref{3rhospos}
\begin{align}
   \mf{A}^{(3)\,a}_{2i}(k^{+},-z)  & =  -\int_{x} \frac{ig^{3}N_{c}\rho^{a}(x)(x-z)^{i}}{16\sqrt{2}\pi^{3}\,(x-z)^{2}\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}\Bigg[\left[-\frac{2}{\epsilon}-2\gamma -\log{\left(\frac{(x-z)^{2}\,\mu^{2}}{4}\right)}\right]\times \left[\frac{11}{3}+ \log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}+\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1\right)}-\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\right]\notag \\ & -\frac{67}{9}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3} + \log{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)}^{2}+\log{\left(1-\frac{k^{+}}{\vee}\right)}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\Bigg] \notag \\ & +\int d^{2}\mathbf{p}\,d^{2}\mathbf{k}\,\int_{\v{x},\v{y}}\, e^{i\v{k}\cdot (\v{z}-\v{y})} e^{i\v{p}\cdot (\v{y}-\v{x})}\frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\left\{ \rho^{b}(\mathbf{x}),\,\rho^{c}(\mathbf{y})\right\}}{32\sqrt{2k^{+}}\,\pi^{5}\mathbf{p}^{2}\mathbf{k}^{2}}\Bigg[\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\left[\frac{k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}-\frac{k\cdot p\,k^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]-2\,k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\notag \\ & -2k^{i}\,\log{\left(\frac{k^{+}p^{2}}{\vee\, k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}}\right)}+\frac{(2p^{2}-2\,k\cdot p + k^{2})\,k^{i} + k^{2}p^{i}}{(k-p)^{2}}\, \log{\left[1+\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}-1\right)\frac{k^{2}}{(k-p)^{2}}\right]}\notag \\ & - \frac{(p^{2}\,k^{i}+k^{2}\,p^{i})}{(k-p)^{2}}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee - k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right)}-k^{i}\, \log{\left(\frac{\vee}{k^{+}}\right)}\Bigg]\notag \\ & -\,\int_{\mathbf{w},\, \mathbf{x},\, \mathbf{y},\, \mathbf{u}}\frac{ig^{3}\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{x})\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{y})\,\rho^{a}(\mathbf{w})\, (x-u)\cdot (y-u)\,(w-z)^{i}}{8\sqrt{2}\pi^{4}\,(x-u)^{2}(y-u)^{2}(w-z)^{2}\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\log{\left(\frac{\textcolor{red}{V}\textcolor{blue}{\vee}}{\Lambda}\right)} %\notag \\ &  -\int_{x,y,z'}\, \frac{g^{3}\,f^{abc}\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}}{8\sqrt{2}\pi^{4}\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\Bigg\{\log{\left(\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\bigg[\frac{(y-z')\cdot (z-z')\, (x-z)^{i}}{(y-z')^{2}\, (z-z')^{2}\, (x-z)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(y-z')\cdot (x-z')\, (z-z')^{i}}{(y-z')^{2}\, (x-z')^{2}\, (z-z')^{2}}\notag \\ & - \frac{(x-z')\cdot (z-z')\, (y-z)^{i}}{(x-z')^{2}\, (z-z')^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\,\frac{(x-z')\cdot (y-z')\, (z-z')^{i}}{(x-z')^{2}\, (y-z')^{2}\, (z-z')^{2}}\bigg] -\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}\,\bigg[\frac{(x-z')\cdot (y-z')\, (y-z)^{i}}{(x-z')^{2}\,(y-z')^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(y-z')\cdot (x-z')\, (x-z)^{i}}{(y-z')^{2}\,(x-z')^{2}\,(x-z)^{2}}\bigg]\Bigg\}\notag \\ &  -\int_{x,y}\,\frac{g^{3}\,f^{abc}\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}}{16\sqrt{2}\pi^{3}\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \left[\frac{(y-z)^{i}}{(y-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)^{i}}{(x-z)^{2}}\right]\times \log{\left[\frac{(y-z)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}\right]}\,\log{\left[\frac{(x-z)^{2}}{(x-y)^{2}}\right]} \notag \\ & -\Bigg\{ \int\, \frac{d^{2}k}{2\pi}\, d^{2}p\, \int_{x,y}\, e^{ik\cdot (z-x)}\, e^{ip\cdot (x-y)}\, \frac{ig^{3}f^{abc}\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}}{8\sqrt{2}\pi^{4}\, \sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \frac{k^{i}}{k^{2}\,p^{2}}\, \log{\left[\frac{p^{2}}{k^{2}}\right]}-x\leftrightarrow y \Bigg\}\notag \\ & +  \int\, \frac{d^{2}k}{2\pi}\, d^{2}p\, \int_{x,y,z}\,e^{ik\cdot (z-y)}\, e^{ip\cdot (y-x)}\, \frac{ig^{3}\,f^{abc}\,\{\rho^{b}(x),\rho^{c}(y)\}}{8\sqrt{2}\pi^{4}\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}\, \Bigg[\frac{k^{i}}{p^{2}k^{2}}\,\log{\left(1+\frac{\vee\, k^{2}}{k^{+}\, p^{2}}\right)}\Bigg]-x\leftrightarrow y \notag \\ & -\,\int_{\mathbf{w},\, \mathbf{x},\, \mathbf{y},\, \mathbf{u}}\frac{ig^{3}\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{x})\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{y})\,\rho^{a}(\mathbf{w})\, (x-u)\cdot (y-u)\,(w-z)^{i}}{8\sqrt{2}\pi^{4}\,(x-u)^{2}(y-u)^{2}(w-z)^{2}\,\sqrt{k^{+}}}\,\log{\left(\frac{V}{\Lambda}\right)}
\end{align}
$\mf{B}_{2}$ is obtained by comparing third line of Eq.~ \eqref{Omegaonvacmom1} with Eq.~ \eqref{psiggrhomom} and Eq.~ \eqref{ggrhorhomom} for momentum space.
\begin{align}
    \mf{B}^{ba}_{2ji}(k^{+},k;p^{+},p) & =  \frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\,p^{j}k^{i}\rho^{c}(-p-k)\, \sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}}{2\,\left(p^{+}k^{2}+k^{+}p^{2}\right)}\,\left[\frac{1}{k^{+}\,p^{2}} - \frac{1}{k^{2}\,p^{+}}\right]\,\textcolor{red}{\Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)}\textcolor{blue}{\Theta(p^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(k^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(\vee-k^{+}-p^{+})}\notag \\ & + \frac{g^{2}p^{j}k^{i}\,\{\rho^{b}(-p),\rho^{a}(-k)\}}{2\,p^{2}k^{2}\, \sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}}\,\textcolor{red}{\Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)}\textcolor{blue}{\Theta(p^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(k^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(\vee-k^{+}-p^{+})}\notag \\ & -\,\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(-p-k)\,(p+k)^{l}\,\left(p^{m}k^{+}-p^{+}k^{m}\right)}{(p+k)^{2}\,\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})\,\left(\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}+\frac{k^{2}}{k^{+}}\right)}\,\left[\frac{\delta_{lm}\delta_{ji}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})} - \frac{\delta_{im}\delta_{jl}}{k^{+}} - \frac{\delta_{jm}\delta_{il}}{p^{+}}\right]\,\textcolor{red}{\Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)}\textcolor{blue}{\Theta(p^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(k^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(\vee-k^{+}-p^{+})}\notag \\ & -\,\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(-p-k)\,(p^{+}-k^{+})\delta_{ji}}{2\,\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}\,(p^{+}+k^{+})^{2}\,\left(\frac{k^{2}}{k^{+}}+\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}\right)}\,\textcolor{red}{\Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)}\textcolor{blue}{\Theta(p^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(k^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(\vee-k^{+}-p^{+})}
\end{align}
$\mf{B}_{2}$ is obtained in coordinate space by comparing third line of eq. \eqref{Omegaonvacposzeta} with Eq.~ \eqref{ggrhopos} and Eq.~ \eqref{ggrhorhopos}.
%\begin{align}
 %    \mf{B}^{ba}_{2ji}(k^{+}, -\v{z}; p^{+}, -\v{u}) & =    \int_{\mathbf{x}}\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(\mathbf{x})\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}}{4\pi^{2}\left(p^{+}(x-u)^{2}+k^{+} (x-z)^{2}\right)(p^{+}+k^{+})}\Bigg(\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2(z-u)^{2}}\left((x-z)^{2}-(x-u)^{2}\right)\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-u)^{i}(z-u)^{j}}{(z-u)^{2}}\frac{(p^{+}+k^{+})}{k^{+}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}(z-u)^{i}}{(z-u)^{2}}\frac{(p^{+}+k^{+})}{p^{+}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-u)^{i}}{2(x-z)^{2}}\frac{(p^{+}+k^{+})}{k^{+}}\notag \\ &  -\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-u)^{i}}{2(x-u)^{2}}\frac{(p^{+}+k^{+})}{p^{+}}\Bigg)\,\times \Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right) \notag \\ & -\,\int_{\mathbf{x},\,\mathbf{y}}\frac{g^{2}\left\{ \rho^{a}(\mathbf{y}),\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{x})\right\} (y-z)^{i}(x-u)^{j}}{8\pi^{2}\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}(y-z)^{2}(x-u)^{2}}\,\times \Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)
%\end{align}
%Rearranging we get
\begin{align}
     \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+}, -\v{w}; p^{+}, -\v{z}) & =    \int_{\mathbf{x}}\frac{ig^{2}f^{cba}\rho^{a}(\mathbf{x})\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}}{4\pi^{2}\left[p^{+}(x-z)^{2}+k^{+} (x-w)^{2}\right]\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\,\Bigg[\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2(z-w)^{2}}\left((x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right)\notag \\ & +\frac{(p^{+}+k^{+})}{k^{+}}\,\left(\frac{(x-z)^{j}(z-w)^{i}}{(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-w)^{i}}{2(x-w)^{2}}\right)\notag \\ & +\frac{(p^{+}+k^{+})}{p^{+}}\,\left(\frac{(x-w)^{i}(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}} +\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-w)^{i}}{2(x-z)^{2}}\right)\Bigg]\times \textcolor{red}{\Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)}\textcolor{blue}{\Theta(p^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(k^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(\vee-k^{+}-p^{+})} \notag \\ & -\,\int_{\mathbf{x},\,\mathbf{y}}\frac{g^{2}\left\{ \rho^{c}(\mathbf{y}),\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{x})\right\} (y-w)^{i}(x-z)^{j}}{8\pi^{2}\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}(y-w)^{2}(x-z)^{2}}\,\times \textcolor{red}{\Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)}\textcolor{blue}{\Theta(p^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(k^{+}-\Lambda)\Theta(\vee-k^{+}-p^{+})}
\end{align}
In terms of longitudinal momentum fraction,
\begin{align}
     \mf{B}^{ba}_{2ji}(\xi k^{+}, -\v{z}; (1-\xi)k^{+}, -\v{u}) & = \int_{\mathbf{x}}\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}\rho^{c}(\mathbf{x})\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}}{4\pi^{2}k^{+}\left((1-\xi)(x-u)^{2}+\xi (x-z)^{2}\right)}\Bigg(\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2(z-u)^{2}}\left((x-z)^{2}-(x-u)^{2}\right)\notag \\ & +\frac{(x-u)^{i}(z-u)^{j}}{\xi (z-u)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}(z-u)^{i}}{(1-\xi)(z-u)^{2}}+\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-u)^{i}}{2\xi (x-z)^{2}} -\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-u)^{i}}{2(1-\xi)(x-u)^{2}}\Bigg)\,\times\Theta\left(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}-\xi\right)\notag \\ & -\int_{\mathbf{x},\,\mathbf{y}}\frac{g^{2}\left\{ \rho^{a}(\mathbf{y}),\,\rho^{b}(\mathbf{x})\right\} (y-z)^{i}(x-u)^{j}}{8\pi^{2}k^{+}\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)}(y-z)^{2}(x-u)^{2}}\,\times\Theta\left(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}-\xi\right)
\end{align}
The constraints originating from the incoming single gluon state provide the following {\it additional constraints}. $\mf{B}_{3}$ in momentum space is obtained by comparing third line of Eq.~ \eqref{Omegaongmom} with Eqs.~\eqref{oneginst11}, \eqref{onegrho12}, \eqref{onegrho22}, \eqref{onegrho3mom} and \eqref{onegrho4mom} 
\begin{align}
    \mf{B}^{ba}_{3ji}(k^{+},k; p^{+},p) & =  \frac{ig^2 f^{abc}\,\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}} (k^++p^+) } {(p^+-k^+)^2} \frac{\rho^c(-\v{p}+\v{k})}{p^{+}\v{k}^2 - k^{+}\v{p}^2} \delta_{ij}\left[\Theta\left(p^{+} - k^{+} - \Lambda\right)+\Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)\right]\notag \\ & -\frac{g^{2}\,k^{i}p^{j}}{k^{2}p^{2}\,\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}}}\left[\{\rho^{a}(k),\rho^{b}(-p)\}-if^{abc}\rho^{c}(k-p)\frac{p^{2}k^{+}+k^{2}p^{+}}{p^{2}k^{+}-k^{2}p^{+}}\right]\notag \\ & +\frac{2i\,g^{2}f^{abc}\,\rho^{c}(k-p)(k-p)^{l}\,\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})^{2}}\times \frac{\left(k^{m}p^{+}-k^{+}p^{m}\right)}{\left(k^{2}p^{+}-p^{2}k^{+}\right)}\,\left[\frac{\delta_{jm}\delta_{il}}{p^{+}}-\frac{\delta_{im}\delta_{jl}}{k^{+}}-\frac{\delta_{lm}\delta_{ij}}{(p^{+}-k^{+})}\right]\times \notag \\ & \left[\textcolor{red}{+}\textcolor{blue}{-}\frac{(k^{+}-p^{+})}{(k-p)^{2}}\,\Theta(p^{+}-k^{+}-\Lambda)+\frac{1}{\left(\frac{k^{2}}{k^{+}}-\frac{(k-p)^{2}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}-\frac{p^{2}}{p^{+}}\right)}\,\Theta(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+})\right]
\end{align}
$\mf{B}_{3}$ in coordinate space is obtained by comparing third line of Eq.~ \eqref{Omegaongposzeta} with eqs. \eqref{Eq:onegluoninstX1}, \eqref{Eq:onegluoninstX2}, \eqref{Eq:onegrhoX1}, \eqref{Eq:onegrhoX2}, \eqref{gtworhoX} and \eqref{gtworhoX2}
\begin{align}
   \mf{B}^{ba}_{3ji}(k^+, z;p^+, -u) & = i \frac{g^2 f^{abc}}{(2\pi)^{2}} \int_{x} %
   \frac{\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}(p^{+}+k^{+})} {(k^{+}-p^{+})^2} \frac{\rho^c(\v{x}) \delta_{ij}}{p^{+}(x-u)^{2} - k^{+}(x-z)^{2} }\left[\Theta\left(p^{+} - k^{+} - \Lambda \right)+\Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)\right]  \notag \\ & + \frac{ig^2 f^{abc}} {2\pi^{2} } \,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\, 
   \int_{x}  
   \frac{\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}}}{k^{+}-p^{+}} 
   \frac{(k^{+}(x-z)^{m} - p^{+} (x-u)^m)((x-z)^{k} -  (x-u)^{k})} {k^{+}(x-z)^{2} - p^{+} (x-u)^2}
   \rho^c(\v{x}) \bigg( 
  \delta_{ik} \delta_{jm}
  - \frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\delta_{jk} \delta_{im}
 \notag \\ &  - \frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}}\delta_{ij} \delta_{km}
   \bigg)\times\left[\textcolor{red}{+}\textcolor{blue}{-}\frac{1}{((x-z) -  (x-u))^2}\Theta\left(p^{+} - k^{+} - \Lambda \right)-\frac{p^{+}k^{+}}{(k^{+}(x-z) -  p^{+} (x-u))^2} \Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right)\right]\notag \\ & -\frac{g^{2}}{4\pi^{2} \sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}}\int_{x,y}\frac{(x-z)^{i} (y-u)^{j}}{(x-z)^{ 2}(y-u)^{2}}\{\rho^{a}(\v{x}),\rho^{b}(\v{y})\} \notag \\ &  -\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}}{4\pi^{2} \sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}} \int_{x}\frac{(x-z)^{i}(x-u)^{j}}{(x-z)^{2}(x-u)^{2}}\frac{(k^{+}(x-z)^{2} +p^{+} (x-u)^{2})}{(p^{+} (x-u)^{2}-k^{+}(x-z)^{2})}\rho^{c}(\v{x})
\end{align}
In terms of longitudinal momentum fraction,
\begin{align}
   \mf{B}^{ba}_{3ji}(k^+, z;\xi k^+, -u) & = i \frac{g^2 f^{abc}}{(2\pi)^{2} k^{+}}\,\int_{x}
   \frac{\sqrt{\xi} (1+\xi) } {(1-\xi)^2} \frac{\rho^c(\v{x}) \delta_{ij}}{\xi (x-u)^{2} - (x-z)^{2} }\left[\Theta\left(\xi - 1 - \frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)+\Theta\left(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}-\xi\right)\right]  \notag \\ & + \frac{ig^2 f^{abc}} {2\pi^{2} k^{+}}\,  
   \int_{x}  
   \frac{\sqrt{\xi}}{1-\xi} 
   \frac{((x-z)^{m} - \xi (x-u)^m)((x-z)^{k} -  (x-u)^{k})} {(x-z)^{2} - \xi (x-u)^2}
   \rho^c(\v{x}) \bigg( 
  \delta_{ik} \delta_{jm}
  - \frac{1}{\xi}\delta_{jk} \delta_{im}
 \notag \\ &  - \frac{1}{1-\xi}\delta_{ij} \delta_{km}
   \bigg)\times  \left[\textcolor{red}{+}\textcolor{blue}{-}\frac{1}{((x-z) -  (x-u))^2}\,\Theta\left(\xi - 1 - \frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}\right)-\frac{\xi}{((x-z) -  \xi (x-u))^2} \Theta\left(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}-\xi\right)\right]\notag \\ & -\frac{g^{2}}{4\pi^{2} k^{+}\sqrt{\xi}}\int_{x,y}\frac{(x-z)^{i} (y-u)^{j}}{(x-z)^{ 2}(y-u)^{2}}\{\rho^{a}(\v{x}),\rho^{b}(\v{y})\} \notag \\ &  -\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}}{4\pi^{2} k^{+}\sqrt{\xi}} \int_{x}\frac{(x-z)^{i}(x-u)^{j}}{(x-z)^{2}(x-u)^{2}}\frac{((x-z)^{2} +\xi (x-u)^{2})}{(\xi (x-u)^{2}-(x-z)^{2})}\rho^{c}(\v{x})
\end{align}
$\mf{C}_{1}$ in momentum space is obtained by equating fifth line of Eq.~ \eqref{Omegaongmom} with Eq.~ \eqref{rhoindep}. 
\begin{align}
    \mf{C}^{bca}_{1jki}(k^{+},k; p^{+},p) & = -\,\frac{igf^{abc}\, \left(p^{m}k^{+}-p^{+}k^{m}\right)\,\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})}}{\sqrt{2}\,\left(pk^{+}-p^{+}k\right)^{2}}\,\left[\frac{\delta_{im}\delta_{jk}}{k^{+}}-\frac{\delta_{ik}\delta_{mj}}{p^{+}}-\frac{\delta_{km}\delta_{ij}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}\right]\,\Theta\left(k^{+}-\Lambda-p^{+}\right) 
\end{align}
In terms of longitudinal momentum fraction, we get
\begin{align}
  \mf{C}^{bca}_{1jki}(k^{+}, k; \xi k^{+}, p) & = -\frac{ i g f^{abc}} {\sqrt{2 k^+}} 
    \sqrt{\xi (1-\xi)}\,  
   \frac{p^m - \xi k^m  }{( \v{p}- \xi \v{k} )^2 } \times
   \left[ \delta_{im} \delta_{jk}
     - \frac{1}{\xi}\delta_{jm} \delta_{ik}
   - \frac{1}{1-\xi} \delta_{km}\delta_{ij}
 \right]\, \Theta\left(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}-\xi\right)
\end{align}
Performing the Fourier transform to the transverse coordinate space we get
\begin{align}
   \mf{C}^{bca}_{1jki}(k^{+},z; \xi k^{+},x) = -\frac{gf^{abc}}{2\pi \sqrt{2k^{+}}}\sqrt{\xi(1-\xi)} \delta^{(2)}(z+\xi x)\frac{x^{m}}{x^{2}}\left[ \delta_{im} \delta_{jk}
     - \frac{1}{\xi}\delta_{jm} \delta_{ik}
   - \frac{1}{1-\xi} \delta_{km}\delta_{ij}
 \right]\,\Theta\bigg(1-\frac{\Lambda}{k^{+}}-\xi\bigg)
\end{align}
(or)
\begin{align}
   \mf{C}^{bca}_{1jki}(k^{+},z; p^{+},x) = -\frac{gf^{abc}}{2\pi \sqrt{2k^{+}}}\,\frac{\sqrt{p^{+}\,(k^{+}-p^{+})}}{k^{+}}\, \delta^{(2)}\left(z+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}} x\right)\frac{x^{m}}{x^{2}}\left[ \delta_{im} \delta_{jk}
     - \frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\delta_{jm} \delta_{ik}
   - \frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}} \delta_{km}\delta_{ij}
 \right]\,\Theta\bigg(k^{+}-p^{+}-\Lambda\bigg)
\end{align}


\section{Production cross section}

The production cross-section is given by 
\begin{equation}
\frac{d^3  N}{d^3 k} = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}} \int d^{2}w d^{2} w^\prime 
e^{-ik (w^{\prime}-w)}
\bra{0}\Omega^{\dagger} S^{\dagger}\Omega a^{a\dagger}_{i}(k^{+},w) 
a^{a}_{i} (k^{+} , w^\prime) \Omega^{\dagger} S\Omega\ket{0}
\end{equation}

To simplify the derivation we insert trivial combinations $S S^\dagger$ where it is appropriate: 
\begin{equation}
\frac{d^3  N}{d^3 k} = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int  d^{2}w d^{2} w^\prime 
e^{-ik (w^{\prime}-w)}
\bra{0}\Omega^{\dagger} S^{\dagger}\Omega  S^\dagger  S a^{a\dagger}_{i}(k^{+},w) 
 S S^\dagger a^{a}_{i} (k^{+} , w^\prime)  S S^\dagger  \Omega^{\dagger} S\Omega\ket{0}
\end{equation}
To simplify the notation we define 
\begin{align}
   \bar \Omega^\dagger =  S^\dagger \Omega^\dagger S  
\end{align}
and similarly for creation annihilation operators. Introducing complete set of states
\begin{align}
    \mathsf{1} & = \int dq^{+} \int d^{2}v \ket{g^{f}_{n}(q^{+},v)}\bra{g^{f}_{n}(q^{+},v)} + \int dq^{+} dr^{+} \int d^{2}v d^{2}u \ket{g^{f}_{n}(q^{+},v),g^{e}_{m}(r^{+},u)}\bra{g^{f}_{n}(q^{+},v),g^{e}_{m}(r^{+},u)}
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\frac{d^3  N}{d^3 k} &  = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\int_{w,w^\prime} e^{-i k (w^{\prime}-w)}\bra{0}\Omega^{\dagger}\bar{\Omega} \bigg[ \int dq^{+\prime} \int d^{2}v^{\prime} \ket{g^{f^{\prime}}_{n^{\prime}}(q^{+\prime},v^{\prime})}\bra{g^{f^{\prime}}_{n^{\prime}}(q^{+\prime},v^{\prime})}  + \frac{1}{2}\int dq^{+\prime} dr^{+\prime} \int d^{2}v^{\prime} d^{2}u^{\prime} \notag \\ &\ket{g^{f^{\prime}}_{n^{\prime}}(q^{+\prime},v^{\prime}),g^{e^{\prime}}_{m^{\prime}}(r^{+\prime},u^{\prime})}\bra{g^{f^{\prime}}_{n^{\prime}}(q^{+\prime},v^{\prime}),g^{e^{\prime}}_{m^{\prime}}(r^{+\prime},u^{\prime})}\bigg]\bar a^{a\dagger }_{i} (k^{+} , w)\bar a^{a}_{i} (k^{+} , w^\prime)\bigg[ \int dq^{+} \int d^{2}v \ket{g^{f}_{n}(q^{+},v)}\bra{g^{f}_{n}(q^{+},v)} \notag \\ & + \frac{1}{2}\int dq^{+} dr^{+} \int d^{2}v d^{2}u \ket{g^{f}_{n}(q^{+},v),g^{e}_{m}(r^{+},u)}\bra{g^{f}_{n}(q^{+},v),g^{e}_{m}(r^{+},u)} \bigg]\bar \Omega^{\dagger} \Omega\ket{0}
\end{align}
We can write this as
\begin{equation}
    \frac{d^3  N}{d^3 k}   = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\bigg[\underbrace{{\cal A}^{\dagger a}_{i}(k){\cal A}^{a}_{i}(k)}_{\text{Group I}} + 
    \int dq^+ \int_v
    \underbrace{{\cal A}^{\dagger ab}_{ij}(k; q^+, v){\cal A}^{ab}_{ij}(k; q^+, v)}_{\text{Group II}}\bigg]
\end{equation}
where \begin{align}
    {\cal A}^{a}_{i}(k) & = \sqrt{2\pi} \int d^{2}w^\prime\,e^{-ikw^\prime} U^{ab}(w^\prime)\bra{g^{b}_{i}(k^{+},w^\prime)}\Bar{\Omega}^{\dagger}\Omega\ket{0}
\end{align}
is the amplitude corresponding to one gluon at the final state and 
\begin{align}
     {\cal A}^{ab}_{ij}(k; q^+, v) & = \sqrt{2\pi} \int d^{2} w^{\prime}\,  e^{-ikw^\prime} U^{ac}(w^\prime)\bra{g^{b}_{j}(q^{+},v)g^{c}_{i}(k^{+},w^\prime)}\Bar{\Omega}^{\dagger}\Omega\ket{0}
\end{align}
 is the amplitude corresponding to two gluons at the final state. 
The scattering matrix operator present in $\bar \Omega$ rotates the soft and valence degrees of freedom according to  
\begin{align}
    \bar \rho^a(x)  &= S^\dagger  \rho^a(x) S 
    = U^{a b}(x)  \rho^b(x) \:, \notag  \\ 
    \bar a_i^a(k^+, x)  &= S^\dagger a_i^{a}(k^+,x) S 
    = U^{a b}(x)  a_i^b(k^+,x) \:, \notag  \\
    \bar a_i^{a\dagger}(k^+,x)  &= S^\dagger a_i^{a\dagger}(k^+,x) S 
    = U^{a b}(x)  a_i^{b\dagger}(k^+, x) \,. 
\end{align}
$\Omega$ (and $\bar \Omega$) has eight terms in total. 
However not all of the combinations of these terms would contribute either to  $  {\cal A}_i^a (k^+, k) $ or ${\cal A}^{ab}_{ij}(k^{+},k)$.  For example, $\Omega$, since it acts on vacuum, can only lead to a non-trivial contribution the terms involving the coefficients $\mf{N}$, $\mf{A}^\dagger\,$, and $\mf{B}^\dagger\,$ are considered.  
Take for example contribution defined by $\mf{N}$, $\bar \Omega$ produces non-trivial result only if terms involving $\bar {\mf{A}}^\dagger\,$, and $\bar {\mf{B}}^\dagger\,$ are considered. 
\begin{align}
    {\cal A}^{a}_i = \sum_{n=1}^4 {\cal A}^{a}_{n i} 
\end{align}
${\cal A}^{a}_{n i}$ are given below  
\begin{itemize}
    \item $\bar{\mf{A}} \mf{N}$
\begin{align} \label{eq:A1}
    {\cal A}_{1_{a} i} ^{a} (k)
 & = \int_{w^{\prime}}e^{-ikw^{\prime}}\bigg[\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})+\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})\mf{N}_{2}+\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(3)\,a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})\bigg]
\end{align}
\item $\bar{\mf{N}} \mf{A}$
 \begin{align} \label{eq:A2}
    {\cal A}_{1_{b} i} ^{a} (k)
 & =  \int_{w^{\prime}}e^{-ikw^{\prime}} U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\bigg[-\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})-\bar{\mf{N}}_{2}\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})+\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})\bigg]
 \end{align}
Combining eqs. \eqref{eq:A1} and \eqref{eq:A2} we get,
$$ {\cal A}^{a}_{1i}(k) = {\cal A}_{1_{a} i} ^{a} (k)+{\cal A}_{1_{b} i} ^{a} (k) $$
\begin{align}
        {\cal A}^{a}_{1i}(k) & = \int_{w^{\prime}} e^{-ikw^{\prime}}\Bigg\{\bigg[-U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})+\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+}, w^{\prime})\bigg]+\bigg[-U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\Bar{N_{2}}\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1i} (k^{+},w^{\prime})\notag \\ & +\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+}, w^{\prime})\mf{N}_{2}\bigg] + \bigg[U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})+\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(3)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+}, w^{\prime})\bigg]\Bigg\}
\end{align}
 \item $\bar{\mf{B}}_{3}\mf{A}$
\begin{align}
    {\cal A}_{2 i} ^{a} (k)
 & = - \int \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi} \int_{x,w^{\prime}} e^{-ikw^{\prime}} \Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger ba}_{3ji}(k^{+},w^{\prime}; p^{+},-x) U^{bc}(x) \mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, c}_{1j}(p^{+},x)
\end{align}
\item $\bar{{\mf{A}}} \mf{B}_{2}$
  \begin{align}
    {\cal A}_{3 i} ^{a} (k)
 & = -2 \int \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi} \int_{x,w^{\prime}} e^{-i k w^\prime}  \:U^{ab}(w^\prime)U^{dc}(x) \bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, d}_{1j}(p^+,x) {\mf{B}}^{cb}_{2ji} (k^+,-w^\prime;p^+, -x) \:
\end{align}
\item $\bar{{\mf{C}}} \mf{B}_{2}$ 
     \begin{align}
        {\cal A}_{4 i} ^{a} (k) & =  2  \int 
 \frac{d p^+}{2\pi}  
 \int_{z, x, w^\prime} e^{-i k w^\prime}  \:
                \bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, dca}_{1jki}(k^+,w^\prime-z; p^+, z-x)  
                 U^{db}(x)
                 U^{ce}(z) 
                {\mf{B}}^{be}_{2jk} (k^{+}-p^+, -z; p^+, -x)
    \end{align}
\end{itemize}
\begin{align}
    {\cal A}^{ab}_i = \sum_{n=1}^3 {\cal A}^{ab}_{n i} 
\end{align}
Combining all the contributions we get,
    \begin{align}
         {\cal A}^{a}_{i}(k) & = \int_{w^{\prime}}e^{-ikw^{\prime}} \Bigg\{\bigg[\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})-U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1i}(k^{+},
         w^{\prime})\bigg]+\bigg[\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})\mf{N}_{2} -U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\bar{\mf{N}}_{2}\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})\bigg]\notag 
         \\ & +\bigg[U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})-\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(3)\, a}_{2i}(k^{+}, - w^{\prime})\bigg]\notag 
         \\ & 
         +\int \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x}\,\bigg[\Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger\, ba}_{3ji}(k^{+}, w^{\prime}; p^{+}, -x)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\,b}_{1j}(p^{+}, x)
         -\Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger ba}_{3ji}(k^{+},w^{\prime}; p^{+},-x) U^{bc}(x) \mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, c}_{1j}(p^{+},x)\bigg]\notag
         \\ & 
         +2\,\int \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x}\,\bigg[ \Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, b}_{1j}(p^{+}, x)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{ba}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime};\,p^{+}, -x) -U^{ad}(w^\prime)U^{bc}(x) \bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, b}_{1j}(p^+,x) {\mf{B}}^{cd}_{2ji} (k^+, -w^\prime;p^+, -x)\bigg]\notag 
         \\ & 
         +2\, \int \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,z}\, \bigg[-\Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, bca}_{1jki}(k^{+},w^{\prime}-z;p^{+},z-x)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{bc}_{2jk}(k^{+}-p^{+},- z;p^{+},-x)
         \notag \\ & \qquad \qquad \qquad  +\bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, dca}_{1jki}(k^+,w^\prime-z; p^+, z-x)  
                 U^{db}(x)
                 U^{ce}(z) 
                {\mf{B}}^{be}_{2jk} (k^{+}-p^+, -z; p^+, -x) \bigg] \Bigg\}
   \end{align}   
\begin{align}
         {\cal A}^{a}_{i}(k) & = \int_{w^{\prime}}e^{-ikw^{\prime}} \Bigg\{\bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\,a}_{2i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})+U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2i}(k^{+},
         - w^{\prime})\bigg]\notag \\ & +\bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\,a}_{2i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})\mf{N}_{2}+ U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\bar{\mf{N}}_{2}\mf{A}^{ (1)\, b}_{2i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})\bigg]\notag 
         \\ & +\bigg[U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{2i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})-\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(3)\, a}_{2i}(k^{+}, - w^{\prime})\bigg]\notag 
         \\ & 
         +\int \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x}\,\bigg[-\Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger\, ba}_{3ji}(k^{+}, w^{\prime}; p^{+}, -x)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\,b}_{2j}(p^{+}, - x)
         + \Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger ba}_{3ji}(k^{+},w^{\prime}; p^{+},-x) U^{bc}(x) \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{2j}(p^{+},- x)\bigg]\notag
         \\ & 
         +2\,\int \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{x}\,\bigg[ \Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, b}_{1j}(p^{+}, x)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{ba}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime};\,p^{+}, -x) -U^{ad}(w^\prime)U^{bc}(x) \bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, b}_{1j}(p^+,x) {\mf{B}}^{cd}_{2ji} (k^+, -w^\prime;p^+, -x)\bigg]\notag 
         \\ & 
         +2\, \int \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,z}\, \bigg[-\Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, bca}_{1jki}(k^{+},w^{\prime}-z;p^{+},z-x)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{bc}_{2jk}(k^{+}-p^{+},- z;p^{+},-x)
         \notag \\ & \qquad \qquad \qquad  +\bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, dca}_{1jki}(k^+,w^\prime-z; p^+, z-x)  
                 U^{db}(x)
                 U^{ce}(z) 
                {\mf{B}}^{be}_{2jk} (k^{+}-p^+, -z; p^+, -x) \bigg] \Bigg\}
    \end{align}   
Group (II): 
\begin{itemize}
\item $\bar{\mf{B}}_{1} \mf{N}$
\begin{align}\label{eq: A1a}
    {\cal A}^{ab}_{1_{a}ij}(k; q^{+},v) & = \frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{w^{\prime}}e^{-i k w^\prime} \textcolor{red}{U^{bc}(v)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{\dagger bc}(v)}\Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger ca}_{1ji}(k^{+},w^{\prime};q^{+},v)
\end{align}
 \item $\bar{{\mf{N}}}\mf{B}_{2}$
\begin{align} \label{eq:A1b}
        {\cal A}^{ab}_{1_{b}ij}(k; q^{+},v) & = \frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}}  \int_{w^{\prime}}e^{-i k w^\prime}U^{ac}(w^{\prime}) \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime};q^{+},-v)
    \end{align}
Combining eqs. \eqref{eq: A1a} and \eqref{eq:A1b} we get,
$${\cal A}^{ab}_{1ij}(k) = {\cal A}^{ab}_{1_{a}ij}(k) + {\cal A}^{ab}_{1_{b}ij}(k)$$
\begin{align}
     {\cal A}^{ab}_{1ij}(k; q^{+},v) & = \frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}} \int_{w^{\prime}}e^{-i k w^\prime}\bigg[U^{ac}(w^{\prime}) \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime};q^{+},-v)+\textcolor{red}{U^{bc}(v)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{\dagger bc}(v)}\Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger ca}_{1ji}(k^{+},w^{\prime};q^{+},v)\bigg]
\end{align}
   \item $\bar{{\mf{A}}} \mf{A}$
\begin{align} 
     {\cal A}^{ab}_{2ij}(k; q^{+},v) & = -\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}} \int_{w^{\prime}} e^{-ikw^{\prime}}\bigg[U^{ac}(w^{\prime}) \Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, d}_{1j}(q^{+},v)U^{ db}(v) \mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, c}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})+\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime}) \mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1j}(q^{+},v)\bigg] 
\end{align}
\item $\bar{{\mf{C}}} \mf{A}$
    \begin{align}
         {\cal A}^{ab}_{3ij}(k; q^{+},v) & = \frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}}  \int_{w^{\prime},z} e^{-i k w^\prime}\Bar{\mf{C}}^{dac}_{1jik}(q^{+}+k^{+},z-w^{\prime}; q^{+},w^{\prime}-v)\textcolor{red}{U^{bd}(v)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{\dagger bd}(v)}U^{ce}(z) \mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\,e}_{1k}(q^{+}+k^{+},z)
    \end{align}
\end{itemize}
\begin{align}
     {\cal A}^{ab}_{ij}(k; q^+, v) & = \frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}} \int_{w^{\prime}}e^{-i k w^\prime}\Bigg\{U^{ac}(w^{\prime}) \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime};q^{+},-v)-\textcolor{red}{U^{bc}(v)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{\dagger bc}(v)}\Bar{\mf{B}}^{ca}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime}; q^{+},-v)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\bigg[U^{\dagger\, bc}(v)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, c}_{1j}(q^{+},v)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})-U^{ac}(w^{\prime}) \Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, d}_{1j}(q^{+},v)\textcolor{red}{U^{\dagger db}(v)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{db}(v)} \mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, c}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})\bigg]\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\bigg[U^{\dagger bc}(v)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger \, (1)\, c}_{1j}(q^{+},v)-\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime}) \mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1j}(q^{+},v)\bigg]\notag \\ & +\int_{z}\bigg[\Bar{\mf{C}}^{dac}_{1jik}(q^{+}+k^{+},z-w^{\prime}; q^{+},w^{\prime}-v)U^{\dagger\, bd}(v)U^{ce}(z) \mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\,e}_{1k}(q^{+}+k^{+},z)\notag \\ &-U^{\dagger\, bd}(v) \Bar{\mf{C}}^{dac}_{1jik}(q^{+}+k^{+},z-w^{\prime}; q^{+},w^{\prime}-v)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,c}_{1k}(q^{+}+k^{+},z)\bigg]\Bigg\}
\end{align}

\begin{align}
     {\cal A}^{ab}_{ij}(k; q^+, v) & = \frac{2}{\sqrt{2\pi}} \int_{w^{\prime}}e^{-i k w^\prime}\Bigg\{U^{ac}(w^{\prime}) \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime};q^{+},-v)-U^{\dagger\, bc}(v)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{ca}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime}; q^{+},-v)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\bigg[U^{\dagger\, bc}(v)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, c}_{2j}(q^{+},-v)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{2i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})-U^{ac}(w^{\prime}) \Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, d}_{2j}(q^{+},-v)\textcolor{red}{U^{\dagger db}(v)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{db}(v)} \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{2i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\bigg]\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\bigg[U^{\dagger bc}(v)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{2i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, c}_{2j}(q^{+},-v)-\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{2i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime}) \mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{2j}(q^{+},- v)\bigg]\notag \\ & -\int_{z}\bigg[\Bar{\mf{C}}^{dac}_{1jik}(q^{+}+k^{+},z-w^{\prime}; q^{+},w^{\prime}-v)U^{\dagger\, bd}(v)U^{ce}(z) \mf{A}^{(1)\,e}_{2k}(q^{+}+k^{+},-z)\notag \\ &-U^{\dagger\, bd}(v) \Bar{\mf{C}}^{dac}_{1jik}(q^{+}+k^{+},z-w^{\prime}; q^{+},w^{\prime}-v)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\,c}_{2k}(q^{+}+k^{+},-z)\bigg]\Bigg\}
\end{align}
From here onward, we will denote $\mf{A}_{2}$ as $\mf{A}$ and $\mf{A}^{\dagger}_{2}$ as $\mf{A}^{\dagger}$. We compute the production cross-section using the above amplitudes. It is calculated to order $g^{4}$. It is as follows:
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N}{d^{3}k} = 
     \underbrace{\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm LO}}{d^{3}k}}_{\text{Order}(g^{2})}
     + 
    \underbrace{ \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm (I)}}{d^{3}k}
     + \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm (II)}}{d^{3}k}}_{\text{Order}(g^{4})}
\end{align}
\subsection{Production cross section at the leading order}
The amplitude corresponding to leading-order production is
\begin{align}
   {\cal A}^{a}_{i}(k) & = \int_{w^{\prime}} e^{-ikw^{\prime}} \bigg[-U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})+\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+}, w^{\prime})\bigg]
\end{align}
and leading order ($g^2$) production cross-section is given by
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm LO}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}} \int_{w,w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)} \bigg[-\mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{1i}(k^{+},w)U^{\dagger ca}(w)+\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+}, w)\bigg]\times \bigg[-U^{ab}(w^{\prime})\mf{A}^{\dagger\,(1)\, b}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime})+\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+}, w^{\prime})\bigg]
\end{align}
where,
\begin{align}
    \Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{1i}(k^{+},w^{\prime}) & = \frac{ig}{\sqrt{2k^+}\pi}\int_{x^{\prime}}\frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{i}}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}} U^{ab}(x^{\prime})\rho^{b}(x^{\prime}) 
\end{align}
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm LO}}{d^{3}k} = \frac{\alpha_s } {4\pi^{4} (N_c^2-1)\, k^{+}}\int_{x,w,x^{\prime},w^{\prime}}
    e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)} 
    \frac{(x-w)}{(x-w)^{2}} \cdot
    \frac{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})}{(x^{\prime}-w^{\prime})^{2}}
   \langle  \rho^{b}(x) \rho^{b}(x^{\prime}) \rangle
    {\rm Tr} \Big\{\left[U^{\dagger}(x)-U^{\dagger}(w)\right]\left[U(x^{\prime})-U(w^{\prime})\right]\Big\}
\end{align}
\subsection{Production cross section at the Next to leading order}
The NLO part ($g^4$) is split into two groups. The first (second) group consists
of the contributions arising from the terms $A$ proportional to the vacuum (single gluons) state: 
\begin{align} \label{eq:CS1}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm (I)}}{d^{3}k} & =
    \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}  \int_{w, w^\prime} e^{-ik(w^\prime-w)}
    \notag \\ & \times \Bigg\{\bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})+\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b^{\prime}}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})U^{b^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})\bigg]\times\bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\,a}_{i}(k^{+},-w)\mf{N}_{2}+ U^{ab}(w)\bar{\mf{N}}_{2}\mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{i}(k^{+},-w)\bigg]\notag \\ & \quad +\bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})+\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b^{\prime}}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})U^{b^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})\bigg]\times\bigg[U^{ab}(w)\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{i}(k^{+},- w)-\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(3)\, a}_{i}(k^{+}, - w)\bigg]\notag \\ & \quad +\int\,\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi} \int_{x} \bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})+\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b^{\prime}}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})U^{b^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})\bigg]\times\notag \\ & \quad \quad \bigg[-\Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger\, ba}_{3ji}(k^{+}, w; p^{+}, -x)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\,b}_{j}(p^{+}, - x)
         + \Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger\, ba}_{3ji}(k^{+},w; p^{+},-x) U^{bc}(x) \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{j}(p^{+},- x)\bigg]\notag \\ & \quad  +2\, \int\,\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi} \int_{x}\,\bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})+\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b^{\prime}}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})U^{b^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})\bigg]\times \notag \\ & \quad \quad \bigg[U^{ac}(w)U^{bd}(x) \bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{j}(p^+,- x) {\mf{B}}^{cd}_{2ji} (k^+, -w;p^+, -x) - \Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{j}(p^{+}, -x)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{ba}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w;\,p^{+}, -x) \bigg] \notag \\ &+  2\, \int\, \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,z}\, \bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})+\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b^{\prime}}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})U^{b^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})\bigg]\times\notag \\ & \quad \quad \bigg[-\Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, bca}_{1jki}(k^{+},w-z;p^{+},z-x)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{bc}_{2jk}(k^{+}-p^{+},- z;p^{+},-x)
           +\bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, dca}_{1jki}(k^+,w-z; p^+, z-x)  \notag \\ &\quad \quad
                 U^{db}(x)
                 U^{ce}(z) 
                {\mf{B}}^{be}_{2jk} (k^{+}-p^+, -z; p^+, -x) \bigg]  \Bigg\}+ {\rm c.c.}
\end{align}

{\color{blue}\noindent\textbf{Correction.} (G4) The red/blue Wilson-line placements below matter only for a general adjoint $U$ ($U^\dagger=U^T\neq U$); with the convention $U^{bc}=U^{cb}$ both forms are equal.}
\begin{align}\label{eq:CS2}
     \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm (II)}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{4}{(2\pi)^{3}} \int\, \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)} \notag \\ & 
    \Bigg\{\bigg[\mf{B}^{\dagger\, bc^{\prime}}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime};p^{+},-z)\textcolor{red}{U^{c^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})}\textcolor{blue}{U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})} -\Bar{\mf{B}}^{\dagger\, c^{\prime}a}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w^{\prime}; p^{+},-z)\textcolor{red}{U^{bc^{\prime}}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)}\bigg]\times\notag \\ & \bigg[U^{ac}(w) \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w;p^{+},-z) -U^{\dagger\, bc}(z)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{ca}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w; p^{+},-z)\bigg]\notag \\ & + \frac{1}{4}\,\bigg[\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, c^{\prime}}_{j}(p^{+},-z)U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)-\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, c^{\prime}}_{i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\textcolor{red}{U^{bd^{\prime}}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{d^{\prime}b}(z)}\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, d^{\prime}}_{j}(p^{+},-z)\textcolor{red}{U^{c^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})}\textcolor{blue}{U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})}  \bigg]\times\notag \\ & \quad \quad\bigg[\textcolor{red}{U^{bc}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{cb}(z)}\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, c}_{j}(p^{+},-z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},-w)-U^{ac}(w) \Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, d}_{j}(p^{+},-z)U^{db}(z) \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{i}(k^{+},- w)\bigg] \notag \\ & +\frac{1}{4}\bigg[\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, c^{\prime}}_{j}(p^{+},-z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\textcolor{red}{U^{bc^{\prime}}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)} - \mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{j}(p^{+},- z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\bigg]\times\notag \\ & \quad \quad\bigg[\textcolor{red}{U^{bc}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{cb}(z)}\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, c}_{j}(p^{+},-z)-\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w) \mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{j}(p^{+},- z)\bigg]\notag \\ & + \int_{y,y^{\prime}}\bigg[\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\,e^{\prime}}_{k^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y^{\prime})U^{\dagger\, e^{\prime}c^{\prime}}(y^{\prime})U^{d^{\prime}b}(z) \Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, d^{\prime}ac^{\prime}}_{1jik^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},y^{\prime}-w^{\prime}; p^{+},w^{\prime}-z)  -\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\,c^{\prime}}_{k^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y^{\prime})\notag \\ & \Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, d^{\prime}ac^{\prime}}_{1jik^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},y^{\prime}-w^{\prime}; p^{+},w^{\prime}-z)U^{d^{\prime}b}(z)\bigg]\times\bigg[\Bar{\mf{C}}^{dac}_{1jik}(p^{+}+k^{+},y-w; p^{+},w-z)\textcolor{red}{U^{bd}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{db}(z)}U^{ce}(y) \mf{A}^{(1)\,e}_{k}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y)\notag \\ & -\textcolor{red}{U^{bd}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{db}(z)} \Bar{\mf{C}}^{dac}_{1jik}(p^{+}+k^{+},y-w; p^{+},w-z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\,c}_{k}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y)\bigg] \Bigg\}\notag \\ & + \Bigg\{\frac{4}{(2\pi)^{3}} \int\, \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\int_{z,w,w^{\prime}} e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)} \notag \\ & 
        \Bigg\{\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, c^{\prime}}_{j}(p^{+},-z)U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)-\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, c^{\prime}}_{i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\textcolor{red}{U^{bd^{\prime}}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{d^{\prime}b}(z)}\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, d^{\prime}}_{j}(p^{+},-z)\textcolor{red}{U^{c^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})}\textcolor{blue}{U^{ac^{\prime}}(w^{\prime})}  \bigg]\times\notag \\ & \bigg[U^{ac}(w) \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w;p^{+},-z) -U^{\dagger\, bc}(z)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{ca}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w; p^{+},-z)\bigg]\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, c^{\prime}}_{j}(p^{+},-z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\textcolor{red}{U^{bc^{\prime}}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)} - \mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{j}(p^{+},- z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\bigg] \times \notag \\ & \bigg[U^{ac}(w) \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w;p^{+},-z) -U^{\dagger\, bc}(z)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{ca}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w; p^{+},-z)\bigg]\notag \\ & -\int_{y^{\prime}}\bigg[\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\,e^{\prime}}_{k^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y^{\prime})U^{\dagger\, e^{\prime}c^{\prime}}(y^{\prime})U^{d^{\prime}b}(z) \Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, d^{\prime}ac^{\prime}}_{1jik^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},y^{\prime}-w^{\prime}; p^{+},w^{\prime}-z)  -\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\,c^{\prime}}_{k^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y^{\prime})\notag \\ & \Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, d^{\prime}ac^{\prime}}_{1jik^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},y^{\prime}-w^{\prime}; p^{+},w^{\prime}-z)U^{d^{\prime}b}(z)\bigg] \times \bigg[U^{ac}(w) \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w;p^{+},-z) -U^{\dagger\, bc}(z)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{ca}_{2ji}(k^{+},-w; p^{+},-z)\bigg]\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{4}\,\bigg[\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\, c^{\prime}}_{j}(p^{+},-z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\textcolor{red}{U^{bc^{\prime}}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{c^{\prime}b}(z)} - \mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b}_{j}(p^{+},- z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w^{\prime})\bigg]\times\notag \\ &  \bigg[\textcolor{red}{U^{bc}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{cb}(z)}\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, c}_{j}(p^{+},-z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},-w)-U^{ac}(w) \Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, d}_{j}(p^{+},-z)U^{db}(z) \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{i}(k^{+},- w)\bigg]\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{2}\, \int_{y^{\prime}}\bigg[\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\,e^{\prime}}_{k^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y^{\prime})U^{\dagger\, e^{\prime}c^{\prime}}(y^{\prime})U^{d^{\prime}b}(z) \Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, d^{\prime}ac^{\prime}}_{1jik^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},y^{\prime}-w^{\prime}; p^{+},w^{\prime}-z)  -\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\,c^{\prime}}_{k^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y^{\prime})\notag \\ & \Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, d^{\prime}ac^{\prime}}_{1jik^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},y^{\prime}-w^{\prime}; p^{+},w^{\prime}-z)U^{d^{\prime}b}(z)\bigg] \times  \bigg[\textcolor{red}{U^{bc}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{cb}(z)}\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, c}_{j}(p^{+},-z)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},-w)\notag \\ & -U^{ac}(w) \Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, d}_{j}(p^{+},-z)U^{db}(z) \mf{A}^{(1)\, c}_{i}(k^{+},- w)\bigg]\notag \\ & - \frac{1}{2}\,\int_{y^{\prime}}\bigg[\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\,e^{\prime}}_{k^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y^{\prime})U^{\dagger\, e^{\prime}c^{\prime}}(y^{\prime})U^{d^{\prime}b}(z) \Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, d^{\prime}ac^{\prime}}_{1jik^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},y^{\prime}-w^{\prime}; p^{+},w^{\prime}-z)  -\Bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\, (1)\,c^{\prime}}_{k^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},-y^{\prime})\notag \\ & \Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, d^{\prime}ac^{\prime}}_{1jik^{\prime}}(p^{+}+k^{+},y^{\prime}-w^{\prime}; p^{+},w^{\prime}-z)U^{d^{\prime}b}(z)\bigg] \times \bigg[\textcolor{red}{U^{bc}(z)}\textcolor{blue}{U^{cb}(z)}\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w)\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, c}_{j}(p^{+},-z)\notag \\ & -\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(1)\, a}_{i}(k^{+},- w) \mf{A}^{(1)\, b}_{j}(p^{+},- z)\bigg]\Bigg\} +  {\rm c.c.} \Bigg\}
\end{align}
