\appendix
\section{State definitions and normalization}\label{sec:defn}
Following conventional notation, $\left|0\right\rangle$ denotes the vacuum state of the free Hamiltonian on the soft Hilbert space.
The energy of this state will be set to zero, $E_{0}=0$. 
We define the vacuum state as 
\begin{equation}
 a_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\left|0\right\rangle =0,\qquad\qquad b_{i}^{\alpha}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\left|0\right\rangle =0,\qquad\qquad d_{i}^{\alpha}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\left|0\right\rangle =0,
\end{equation}
for any $\Lambda<k^{+}<\vee$. 
Additionally, we will use the following three eigenstates of free Hamiltonian:\\
\begin{itemize}
  \item One gluon state:  
    \begin{equation}
      \label{gsta}
  \left|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle \,\equiv\,\frac{a_{i}^{a\dagger}(k)}{(2\pi)^{3/2}}\left|0\right\rangle 
\end{equation}
or 
\begin{equation}
  \label{gstax}
\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{z})\right\rangle \,\equiv\,\frac{a_{i}^{a\dagger}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{z})}{(2\pi)^{1/2}}\left|0\right\rangle 
\end{equation}
Note that $\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{z})\right\rangle$ is different from a simple Fourier transform by a factor of $2\pi$: 
\begin{align}
    |g(k^+,\v z) \rangle  = 2\pi \int \frac{d^2k}{(2\pi)^2}  e^{-i \v k \v z }   |g(k) \rangle\,.
\end{align}
The normalization of the state is 
$$
\left\langle g_{j}^{b}(p)\left|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right.\right\rangle
=\delta^{ab}\delta_{ij}\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{p})\delta(k^{+}-p^{+})\;$$ 
and 
$$\;
\left\langle g_{j}^{b}(p^{+},\,\mathbf{z}^{\prime})\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\,\mathbf{z})\right.\right\rangle 
\,=\,\delta^{ab}\,\delta_{ij}\,\delta^{(2)}(\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{z}^{\prime})\,\delta(k^{+}-p^{+}).
$$
Thus 
\begin{equation}
 a_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\left|g^b_j(q)\right\rangle = (2\pi )^{3/2}\delta^{ab} \delta_{ij}\delta^{(3)}(k-q)  \left| 0 \right\rangle
\end{equation}




For completeness we  note $E_{g}(k)=\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{2k^{+}}.$ 
\item Two gluon state: 
  \begin{equation}\begin{split}\label{ggst}
&\left|g_{i}^{a}(k)\, g_{j}^{b}(p)\right\rangle \,\equiv\,\frac{a_{i}^{a\dagger}(k)\, a_{j}^{b\dagger}(p)}{(2\pi)^{3}}\left|0\right\rangle \qquad \\
&\left|g_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\,\mathbf{z})\, g_{j}^{b}(p^{+},\,\mathbf{z}^{\prime})\right\rangle \,\equiv\,\frac{a_{i}^{a\dagger}(k^{+},\,\mathbf{z})\, a_{j}^{b\dagger}(p^{+},\,\mathbf{z}^{\prime})}{2\pi}\left|0\right\rangle .\\
  \end{split}\end{equation}\\
  with the energy $E_{gg}(k,\, p)\,\equiv\,\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{2k^{+}}+\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{2p^{+}}$.\\
\item Quark anti-quark state:
  \begin{equation}
    \begin{split}
      \label{qqst}
    &\left|\bar{q}_{\lambda_{2}}^{\beta}(p)\, q_{\lambda_{1}}^{\alpha}(k)\right\rangle \,\equiv\,\frac{b_{\lambda_{1}}^{\alpha\dagger}(k)\, d_{\lambda_{2}}^{\beta\dagger}(p)}{(2\pi)^{3}}\left|0\right\rangle \quad\; \\
    &\left|\bar{q}_{\lambda_{2}}^{\beta}(p^{+},\,\mathbf{z}^{\prime})\, q_{\lambda_{1}}^{\alpha}(k^{+},\,\mathbf{z})\right\rangle \,\equiv\,\frac{b_{\lambda_{1}}^{\alpha\dagger}(k^{+},\,\mathbf{z})\, d_{\lambda_{2}}^{\beta\dagger}(p^{+},\,\mathbf{z}^{\prime})}{2\pi}\left|0\right\rangle ,\\
    \end{split}
  \end{equation}
  with the energy $E_{q\bar{q}}(k,\, p)=\frac{\mathbf{k}^{2}}{2k^{+}}+\frac{\mathbf{p}^{2}}{2p^{+}}$.

\end{itemize}
\newpage 
\section{The CGC Hamiltonian: Eikonal Approximation for  QCD Hamiltonian}
In order to introduce the eikonal approximation for the LC QCD Hamiltonian, we split the gluon fields into  the soft and valence modes: 
\begin{equation}\label{Am}
\underline{A}_{i}^{a}(x)=\int_{\Lambda}^{e^{\delta\mathsf{Y}}\Lambda}\frac{dk^{+}}{2\pi}\int\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{k}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2k^{+}}}\left(a_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})e^{-ik\cdot x}+a_{i}^{a\dagger}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})e^{ik\cdot x}\right),
 \end{equation}
and
\begin{equation}\label{Ap}
\overline{A}_{i}^{a}(x)=\int_{e^{\delta\mathsf{Y}}\Lambda}^{\infty}\frac{dk^{+}}{2\pi}\int\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{k}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2k^{+}}}\left(a_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})e^{-ik\cdot x}+a_{i}^{a\dagger}(k^{+},\mathbf{k})e^{ik\cdot x}\right).
 \end{equation}
 \begin{equation}
H_{int}=H_{g}+H_{gqq}+H_{ggg}+H_{gggg}+H_{ggqq}+H_{qq-inst}+H_{gg-inst}+H_{gq}+H_{V}.
  \end{equation}
After inserting $A_{i}^{a}(x)=\underline{A}_{i}^{a}(x)+\overline{A}_{i}^{a}(x)$  to $H_{int}$, we define the following components of the interaction Hamiltonian:
 \begin{equation}\label{Hg}
H_{g}\,\equiv\,-g\int dx^{-}\, d^{2}\mathbf{x}\,(\partial_{i}\underline{A}_{i}^{a})\left(f^{abc}\frac{1}{\partial^{+}}(\overline{A}_{j}^{b}\partial^{+}\overline{A}_{j}^{c})+2\frac{1}{\partial^{+}}(\overline{\Psi}_{+}^{\dagger}t^{a}\overline{\Psi}_{+})\right),
  \end{equation}
\begin{equation}\label{hggg}
H_{ggg}\,\equiv\,-gf^{abc}\int dx^{-}\, d^{2}\mathbf{x}\,\left((\partial_{i}\underline{A}_{j}^{a})\underline{A}_{i}^{b}\underline{A}_{j}^{c}+(\partial_{i}\underline{A}_{i}^{a})\frac{1}{\partial^{+}}(\underline{A}_{j}^{b}\partial^{+}\underline{A}_{j}^{c})\right),
 \end{equation}
  \begin{equation}\begin{split}\label{ggi}
&H_{gg-inst}\,\equiv\,g^{2}f^{abc}\int dx^{-}\, d^{2}\mathbf{x}\,\frac{1}{\partial^{+}}(\underline{A}_{i}^{b}\partial^{+}\underline{A}_{i}^{c})\left(f^{ade}\frac{1}{\partial^{+}}(\overline{A}_{j}^{d}\partial^{+}\overline{A}_{j}^{e})+2\frac{1}{\partial^{+}}(\overline{\Psi}_{+}^{\dagger}t^{a}\overline{\Psi}_{+})\right),
 \end{split}\end{equation}
 \begin{equation}H_{gggg}\,\equiv\,\frac{g^{2}}{4}f^{abc}f^{ade}\int dx^{-}\, d^{2}\mathbf{x}\,\left(\underline{A}_{i}^{b}\underline{A}_{j}^{c}\underline{A}_{i}^{d}\underline{A}_{j}^{e}-2\frac{1}{\partial^{+}}(\underline{A}_{i}^{b}\partial^{+}\underline{A}_{i}^{c})\frac{1}{\partial^{+}}(\underline{A}_{j}^{d}\partial^{+}\underline{A}_{j}^{e})\right),
 \end{equation}
For operational purposes, we require the interacting Hamiltonian to be written in terms of its creation and annihilation operators. Thus, the interacting Hamiltonian in oscillator modes is as follows:
 \begin{equation}\label{eikohg}
\mathsf{H}_{g}=\int_{\Lambda}^{e^{\delta\mathsf{Y}}\Lambda}\frac{dk^{+}}{2\pi}\int\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{k}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{g\mathbf{k}^{i}}{\sqrt{2}|k^{+}|^{3/2}}\left[a_{i}^{a\dagger}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{k})+a_{i}^{a}(k^{+},\, \mathbf{k})\rho^{a}(\mathbf{k})\right],
 \end{equation}
 \begin{equation}\begin{split}
\mathsf{H}_{g\: gg}=&-\int_{\Lambda}^{e^{\delta\mathsf{Y}}\Lambda}\frac{dk^{+}}{2\pi}\,\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\, dq^{+}\,\int\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{k}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\,\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{p}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\, d^{2}\mathbf{q}\,\frac{igf^{abc}}{2\sqrt{2k^{+}p^{+}q^{+}}}\\
&\times\left[\left(\mathbf{q}^{i}-\frac{q^{+}}{p^{+}+q^{+}}\mathbf{k}^{i}\right)a_{i}^{a}(k)a_{j}^{b\dagger}(p)a_{j}^{c\dagger}(q)\delta^{(3)}(-k+p+q)\right.\\
&+\left.\left(\mathbf{p}^{i}+\mathbf{q}^{i}+\frac{p^{+}+q^{+}}{q^{+}-p^{+}}\mathbf{k}^{i}\right)a_{i}^{a\dagger}(k)a_{j}^{b\dagger}(p)a_{j}^{c}(q)\delta^{(3)}(k+p-q),\,+\,h.c.\right]\\
 \end{split}\end{equation}

    \begin{eqnarray}
\mathsf{H}_{gg-inst}&&=\int_{\Lambda}^{e^{\delta\mathsf{Y}}\Lambda}\frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\frac{dq^{+}}{2\pi}\int\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{p}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{q}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}}{\sqrt{p^{+}q^{+}}}\,\left(\frac{q^{+}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p}-\mathbf{q})}{2(p^{+}+q^{+})^{2}}\, a_{i}^{b\dagger}(p^{+},\,\mathbf{p})\, a_{i}^{c\dagger}(q^{+},\,\mathbf{q})\right.\nonumber\\
&&\left.-\, h.c.\,-\,\frac{(p^{+}+q^{+})\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p}+\mathbf{q})}{2(p^{+}-q^{+})^{2}}\, a_{i}^{b\dagger}(p^{+},\,\mathbf{p})\, a_{i}^{c}(q^{+},\,\mathbf{q})\right),\end{eqnarray}

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%5
\section{Matrix elements}
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%5


\begin{itemize}
  
  \item   
    Gluon emission from valence $\rho$
    \begin{align}
      \label{m1}
      \left\langle g_{i}^{a}(k)\left|\, H_{g}\,\right|0\right\rangle &=
      %\tikzfig{LCPT_rhotog} = 
      \frac{g{k}^{i}\rho^{a}(-\v{k})}{4\pi^{3/2}|k^{+}|^{3/2}} 
    \end{align}
  
  \item Valence adds an extra gluon to an existing one 
    \begin{align}
      \left\langle g_{l}^{c}(q)\, g_{j}^{b}(p)\left|H_{g}\right|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle &\,=\,    %\tikzfig{LCPT_valenceaddglue}   
      \delta^{ac}\delta_{il}\delta^{(3)}(k-q)\,\frac{g {p}^{j}\rho^{b}(-\mathbf{p})}{4\pi^{3/2}|p^{+}|^{3/2}}
      \,+\,\delta^{ab}\delta_{ij}\delta^{(3)}(k-p)\,\frac{g {q}^{l}\rho^{c}(-\mathbf{q})}{4\pi^{3/2}|q^{+}|^{3/2}}
      \label{m2}
    \end{align}

  \item Three gluon interaction 
    \begin{align}
      \left\langle g_{l}^{c}(q)\, g_{j}^{b}(p)\left|H_{ggg}\right|g_{i}^{a}(k)\right\rangle &  =
      \frac{igf^{abc}\delta^{(3)}(k-p-q)}{8\pi^{3/2}\sqrt{k^{+}p^{+}q^{+}}}
      \Bigg[\bigg({p}^{i}-{q}^{i}+\frac{q^{+}-p^{+}}{k^{+}}{k}^{i}\bigg)\delta_{jl}+\bigg({k}^{j}+{q}^{j}-\frac{k^{+}+q^{+}}{p^{+}}{p}^{j}\bigg)
      \delta_{il}\notag \\ & +\bigg(\frac{k^{+}+p^{+}}{q^{+}}{q}^{l}-{p}^{l}-{k}^{l}\bigg)\delta_{ij}\Bigg]
      \label{m3}
    \end{align}
    \item Valence creates two gluons instantaneously
    \begin{equation}
\left\langle g_{j}^{c}(q)\, g_{i}^{b}(p)\left|H_{gg-inst}\right|0\right\rangle \,=\,\frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}(q^{+}-p^{+})\delta_{ij}\rho^{a}(-\mathbf{p}-\mathbf{q})}{2(2\pi)^{3}\sqrt{p^{+}q^{+}}(p^{+}+q^{+})^{2}}
\label{m4}
 \end{equation}
  \item Instantaneous  interaction between valence and gluon  
    \begin{align}
      \left\langle g_{j}^{c}(q)\left|H_{gg-inst}\right|g_{i}^{b}(p)\right\rangle \,
      =\,  
      \frac{ig^{2}f^{abc}(p^{+}+q^{+})\delta_{ij}\rho^{a}(\mathbf{p}-\mathbf{q})}{2(2\pi)^{3}\sqrt{p^{+}q^{+}}(p^{+}-q^{+})^{2}}
      \label{m5}
    \end{align}
  \end{itemize}
  \section{Fourier Transforms}
  The list of  necessary  integrals is presented below. Some of the integrals are defined as Cauchy principal value. 
  \begin{align}
    \label{i1} & \int\frac{d^{2}{k}}{2\pi}\,\frac{{k}^{i}}{\v{k}^{2}}e^{i\v{k}\cdot X}=\frac{iX^{i}}{X^{2}},  \\ 
    \label{i2} & \int\frac{d^{2}{k}}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}{p}}{2\pi}\,\frac{{k}^{i}\mathbf{p}^{j}}{\mathbf{k}^{2}\left(a\mathbf{k}^{2}+b\mathbf{p}^{2}\right)}e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot X+i\mathbf{p}\cdot Z}=-\frac{X^{i}Z^{j}}{Z^{2}\left(bX^{2}+aZ^{2}\right)},\\ 
  \label{i3} & \int\frac{d^{2}{k}}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}{p}}{2\pi}\,\frac{1}{\xi \mathbf{k}^{2}- \mathbf{p}^{2}}e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot X+i\mathbf{p}\cdot Z}=\frac{1}{\xi Z^2 -X^{2}}, \\
  \label{i4} & \int\frac{d^{2}{k}}{2\pi} \frac{d^{2}{p}}{2\pi}\,\frac{k^m - p^m }{(\v{k}-\v{p})^2} \,\frac{p^l - \xi k^l }{ \v{p}^2 -\xi \v{k}^2 }   e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot X+i\mathbf{p}\cdot Y}
  =- \frac{X^m + \xi Y^m}{ X^2 - \xi Y^{2}}  \frac{X^l+Y^l} {(X+Y)^2},\\
  \label{i5} & \int\frac{d^{2}{k}}{2\pi} \frac{d^{2}{p}}{2\pi}\,\frac{k^m - p^m }{  \v{p}^2 -\xi \v{k}^2 } \,\frac{p^l - \xi k^l }{  (\v{p} - \xi \v{k})^2  }   e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot X+i\mathbf{p}\cdot Y}
  =- \frac{X^l + Y^l}{ X^2 - \xi Y^{2}}  \frac{X^m+\xi Y^m} {(X+\xi Y)^2},\\
  \label{i7} & \int\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{k}}{2\pi}\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{p}}{2\pi}\,\frac{1}{\xi(1-\xi)\mathbf{k}^{2}+\mathbf{p}^{2}}e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot X+i\mathbf{p}\cdot Z}=\frac{1}{X^{2}+\xi(1-\xi)Z^{2}}, \\ 
  \label{i8} & \int\frac{d^{2}\mathbf{k}}{2\pi}\frac{k^{i}}{k^{2}}\ln\bigg(\frac{k^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\bigg)\,e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot X}= \frac{iX^{i}}{X^{2}}\Bigg(-2\gamma -\ln\bigg(\frac{X^{2}\mu^{2}}{4}\bigg)\Bigg) \\ 
  \label{i9} &  \int\, \frac{d^{2}k}{2\pi}\, d^{2}p\, \int_{x,y,z}\, e^{ik\cdot (z-x)}\, e^{ip\cdot (x-y)}\, \frac{k^{i}}{k^{2}\,p^{2}}\, \log{\left[\frac{p^{2}}{k^{2}}\right]}  = -i\pi\, \frac{\log{(x-y)^{2}}}{(x-y)^{2}}\, \left[\frac{(z-x)^{i}}{(z-x)^{2}}-\frac{(z-y)^{i}}{(z-y)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ & + i\log{(x-y)^{2}}\, \left[\frac{(z-x)^{i}\,\log{(z-x)^{2}}}{(z-x)^{2}}-\frac{(z-y)^{i}\, \log{(z-y)^{2}}}{(z-y)^{2}}\right]
\end{align}
\begin{align}
    \int\, d^{2}k\, d^{2}p\, \int_{x,z}\, e^{ik\cdot (x-z')}\, e^{ip\cdot (z-x)}\, \frac{(p^{m}-k^{m})\, (p^{l}-\xi\,k^{l})}{(p-k)^{2}\,(p^{2}-\xi\,k^{2})}
\end{align}
\section{BFKL evolution in the coordinate representation}


\begin{align}\label{v-bfkl}
   %&& \frac{\partial}{\partial Y}
   & \frac{d}{dY}
   \langle \rho^a(\v x)\rho^a(\v y)\rangle=
    - 
    {\frac{\alpha_s N_c}{2 \pi^2} } 
    \biggl\langle
    K(\v x,\v x,\v z)\rho^a(\v x)\rho^a(\v y)
    -2K(\v x,\v y,\v z)\rho^a(\v x)\rho^a(\v y)+K(\v y,\v y,\v z)\rho^a(\v y)\rho^a(\v x) \\
    & -2\delta^2(\v x-\v y)K(\v u,\v v,\v x)\rho^a(\v u)\rho^a(\v v)\nonumber
    \nonumber \\
    & +\left( 2 K(\v x, \v z, \v y)  - K(\v z,\v z,\v y) \right)   \rho^a(\v x)\rho^a(\v z) +\left( 2 K(\v y, \v z, \v x)  - K(\v z,\v z,\v x) \right)   \rho^a(\v y)\rho^a(\v z)\biggl\rangle 
\nonumber
\end{align}
Here (be careful with the order of the arguments!)
\begin{align}
    K(x,y,z) = \frac{X\cdot Y}{X^2 Y^2}
\end{align}
A more explicit form of the evolution 
\begin{align}
    \frac{d \langle \rho^f(x) \rho^f(y)\rangle }{dY}
    = - \frac{\alpha_s N_c}{2\pi^2} 
    &\Bigg[
    \int d^2z (  K(x,x,z) - 2 K(x,y,z)  + K(y,y,z)) \langle \rho^f(x) \rho^f(y)\rangle
    \notag \\ & 
    - 2 \delta^2(x-y) \int d^2z d^2z' K(z,z',x) \langle \rho^f(z) \rho^f(z')\rangle  
    \notag \\ & 
     + \int d^2z \left(  2 K(y,z,x) - K(z,z,x)  \right) \langle \rho^f(y) \rho^f(z)\rangle 
    \notag \\ & 
     + \int d^2z \left(  2 K(x,z,y) - K(z,z,y)  \right) \langle \rho^f(x) \rho^f(z)\rangle 
    \Bigg]
\end{align}
It is convenient to introduce the dipole kernel 
\begin{align}
    K_D(x,y,z) =  K(x,x,z) - 2 K(x,y,z)  + K(y,y,z)\,.
\end{align}
Applying the BFKL evolution to the leading order inclusive gluon production cross-section (the integration variables were renamed to get a common gluon distribution in the projectile)
\begin{align}
\label{Eq:LOx}
    \frac{dN}{d^2 w d^2 w' dy}= \frac{\alpha_s}{4 \pi^4(N_c^2-1)}
    \int_{x,y} {\cal K} (x-w) \cdot {\cal K} (y-w')  \langle \rho^f(x) \rho^f(y)\rangle {\rm Tr} \left\{  (U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w)) (U(y)-U(w')) \right\}
\end{align}
we get 
\begin{align}
\label{Eq:LOBFKL}
    \frac{dN}{d^2 w d^2 w' dy}= - &\frac{\alpha_{s}^2 N_c  \delta Y }{8\pi^{6} (N_c^2-1)}
    \int_{x,y,z} \langle \rho^f(x) \rho^f(y)\rangle 
    \notag \\ &
    \Bigg[
    {\cal K} (x-w) \cdot {\cal K} (y-w') K_D(x,y,z) {\rm Tr} \left \{  (U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w)) (U(y)-U(w')) \right\}
    \notag \\ &
    -2\, {\cal K} (z-w) \cdot {\cal K} (z-w') K(x,y,z) {\rm Tr} \left \{  (U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w)) (U(z)-U(w')) \right\}
    \notag \\ &
    +
    {\cal K} (z-w) \cdot {\cal K} (y-w') (2K(x,y,z) - K(x,x,z)) {\rm Tr} \left \{  (U^\dagger(z)-U^\dagger(w)) (U(y)-U(w')) \right\}
    \notag \\ &
    +
    {\cal K} (x-w) \cdot {\cal K} (z-w') (2K(x,y,z) - K(y,y,z)) {\rm Tr} \left \{  (U^\dagger(x)-U^\dagger(w)) (U(z)-U(w')) \right\}
    \Bigg]
\end{align}
%\begin{align}
%\frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k} & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{3}}\frac{\log{\left(\frac{\vee}{\Lambda}\right)}}{2\pi^{2}} \frac{1}{k^{+}}\int_{x,y,z,w,w^{\prime}}e^{-ik(w^{\prime}-w)} \Bigg\{\frac{( z- w' ) \cdot ( z- w ) (x - z) \cdot (y - z)}{( z- w' )^{2} ( z- w )^{2} (x - z)^{2} (y - z)^{2}}\,N_{c}\, \rho^{c}(x) \rho^{c}(y) \Bigg\}
%\end{align}

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%


\section{JIMWLK evolution of the leading order single gluons production cross section.}


\begin{equation}
\label{Eq:HLO}
H_{\text{JIMWLK}}= \frac{\alpha_s}{2 \pi^2} \int_{t,u,v} 
{\cal K}(u, v, t)
[U^\dagger(u)  - U^\dagger(t)]_{ce} J_R ^c(u)    [U(v)  - U(t)  ]_{ed} J_R ^d(v)
\end{equation}
where 
 \begin{align}
    [J_{R}^a (z), U(x)] &= \delta^{(2)}(x-z) U(x)T^a,  \hspace{1cm}    [J_{R}^a (z) ,U^\dagger(x)] =- \delta^{(2)}(x-z) T^a U^\dagger(x)\,,
\end{align}

We thus can rewrite this in the following form 
\begin{align}
\label{Eq:HLOComm}
H_{\text{JIMWLK}} &= \frac{\alpha_s}{2 \pi^2} \int_{t,u,v} 
{\cal K}(u, v, t)
[ (U^\dagger(u)  - U^\dagger(t)) (U(v)  - U(t) ) ]_{cd}
J_R ^c(u)
J_R ^d(v)
\nonumber \\ &+ {\color{red}
\frac{\alpha_s}{2 \pi^2} \int_{t,u,v} 
{\cal K}(u, v, t)
(\delta^2(u-v) - \delta^2(u-t))
[ (U^\dagger(u)  - U^\dagger(t)) U(u) T^c  ]_{cd}
J_R ^d(v) } 
\end{align}
In the last term,  the second delta-function sets the positions of the Wilson lines to the same point leading to vanishing contribution. Thus we have 
\begin{align}
\label{Eq:HLOfinal}
H_{\text{JIMWLK}} &= \frac{\alpha_s}{2 \pi^2} \int_{t,u,v} 
{\cal K}(u, v, t)
[ (U^\dagger(u)  - U^\dagger(t)) (U(v)  - U(t) ) ]_{cd}
J_R ^c(u)
J_R ^d(v)
\nonumber \\ &-  {\color{red}
\frac{\alpha_s}{2 \pi^2} \int_{t,u} 
{\cal K}(u, u, t)
{\rm tr}[ (U^\dagger(u)  - U^\dagger(t)) U(u) T^d  ]
J_R ^d(u) } 
\end{align}
where we took into account the properties of the adjoint generator. 
The last term  will be  considered at the end. For now we will concentrate on the first term. 

The leading order gluon production cross section is given by Eq.~\eqref{Eq:LOx}.


We need to obtain $\mathcal{M} = H_{\text{JIMWLK}}\times \mathcal{O}_{g}$. Thus, the principal part
\begin{equation}
    J_R^c(u) J_R^d(v) {\rm Tr}  \bigg[( U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(w) )( U(y) - U(w') )\bigg] 
\end{equation}
can be computed by transferring both right rotation operators to the right of all Wilson lines with the help of the commutator identities. 

\begin{align}
    [J^d(v), {\rm Tr}  \left\{ (U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(w)) (U(y) - U(w')) \right\} ]
    & = (\delta^2(y-v) - \delta^2(w'-v)) {\rm Tr} \left\{  (U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(w)) U(v) T^d  \right\} \notag \\ &- (\delta^2(x-v) - \delta^2(w-v)) {\rm Tr} \left\{ U^\dagger(v)  (U(y) - U(w')) T^d  \right\} . 
\end{align}
and 
\begin{align}
    &[J^c(u) , [J^d(v), {\rm Tr}  \left\{ (U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(w)) (U(y) - U(w')) \right\} ]] 
     = \notag \\  
    & - (\delta^2(x-v) - \delta^2(w-v))  (\delta^2(y-u) - \delta^2(w'-u)) {\rm Tr} \left\{ U^\dagger(v)  U(u) T^c T^d  \right\}  
    \notag \\ 
    & - (\delta^2(y-v) - \delta^2(w'-v))  (\delta^2(x-u) - \delta^2(w-u)) {\rm Tr} \left\{  U^\dagger(u) U(v) T^d T^c  \right\} 
    \notag \\ 
    & + \delta^2(u-v) (\delta^2(y-v) - \delta^2(w'-v))  {\rm Tr} \left\{  (U^\dagger(x)  - U^\dagger(w) )U(v) T^c T^d  \right\} 
    \notag \\ 
    & + \delta^2(u-v) (\delta^2(x-v) - \delta^2(w-v))  {\rm Tr} \left\{  U^\dagger(v) (U(y)-U(w')) T^d T^c  \right\} . 
\end{align}
The second terms can be obtained from the first by $u \Leftrightarrow v $ and $c  \Leftrightarrow d$. We thus have to explicitly consider the first term. The symmetry of the last two terms is not transparent and we would have to compute both of them explicitly. Lets start with the first one.    

We will utilize the following transformations quite often: 
\begin{align}
    &{\rm Tr} \left [ W T^c T^d \right] M_{cd} = W_{ab} T^c_{be} T^{d}_{ea} M_{cd} = T^{e}_{da} W_{ab} T^{e}_{bc} M_{cd} = {\rm Tr} \left[ T^e W T^e M \right] \\ 
    &
    {\rm Tr} \left [ W T^d T^c \right] M_{cd} =  {\rm Tr} \left[ T^e W T^e M^T \right]
\end{align}

For the first term we get (note that $d/dY = - H$)
\begin{align}
    &\frac{dN^{(1)}}{d^2 w d^2 w' d y} = \frac{\alpha_s^2 \delta Y}{8 \pi^6 (N_c^2-1)}
\int_{x,y,t} {\cal K}(x-w)\cdot {\cal K}(y-w') \langle\rho^f(x) \rho^f(y) \rangle \notag \\ & \times  
\Big[ 
 {\cal K}(y,x,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(x) U(y) T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(y) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )}\right\}
 \notag \\ & -  {\cal K}(w',x,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(x) U(w') T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(y) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(w') - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )}\right\}
 \notag \\ & -  {\cal K}(y,w,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(w) U(y) T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(y) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(w) - U(t) )}\right\}
 \notag \\ & +  {\cal K}(w',w,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(w) U(w') T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(y) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(w') - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(w) - U(t) )}\right\}
\Big] 
\end{align}
The prefactor in front of the square bracket is invariant under $ x \Leftrightarrow y $ and $ w \Leftrightarrow w' $.  This symmetry can be used to show that the second term equals the third. We do not perform this simplification here and below. 

Performing calculations for the second contribution: 
\begin{align}
    &\frac{dN^{(2)}}{d^2 w d^2 w' d y} = \frac{\alpha_s^2 \delta Y}{8 \pi^6 (N_c^2-1)}
\int_{x,y,t} {\cal K}(x-w)\cdot {\cal K}(y-w') \langle\rho^f(x) \rho^f(y) \rangle \notag \\ & \times  
\Big[ 
 {\cal K}(x,y,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(x) U(y) T^e ( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )\right\}
 \notag \\ & -  {\cal K}(x, w',t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(x) U(w') T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(w') - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )})\right\}
 \notag \\ & -  {\cal K}(w,y,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(w) U(y) T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(w) - U(t) )} )\right\}
 \notag \\ & +  {\cal K}(w, w',t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(w) U(w') T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(w') - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(w) - U(t) )} )\right\}
\Big] 
\end{align} 
Note that ${\cal K}(x,y,z) = {\cal K}(y,x,z)$. \textcolor{blue}{(E7) In $dN^{(1)}$--$dN^{(4)}$ the matrix must sit at the points $u,v$ of ${\cal K}(u,v,t)$ of each line: $(U^\dagger(u)-U^\dagger(t))(U(v)-U(t))$ in $dN^{(1)}$, its transpose $(U^\dagger(v)-U^\dagger(t))(U(u)-U(t))$ in $dN^{(2)}$, and $(U^\dagger(v)-U^\dagger(t))(U(v)-U(t))$ for ${\cal K}(v,v,t)$ in $dN^{(3)}$, $dN^{(4)}$.} 

Third term 
\begin{align}
    &\frac{dN^{(3)}}{d^2 w d^2 w' d y} = - \frac{\alpha_s^2 \delta Y}{8 \pi^6 (N_c^2-1)}
\int_{x,y,t} {\cal K}(x-w)\cdot {\cal K}(y-w') \langle\rho^f(x) \rho^f(y) \rangle \notag \\ & \times  
\Big[ 
 {\cal K}(y,y,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e (U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(w)) U(y) T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(y) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(y) - U(t) )}\right\}
 \notag \\ & -  {\cal K}(w', w',t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e (U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(w)) U(w') T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(y) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(w') - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(w') - U(t) )}\right\}
\Big] 
\end{align} 
The fourth term:
\begin{align}
    &\frac{dN^{(4)}}{d^2 w d^2 w' d y} = - \frac{\alpha_s^2 \delta Y}{8 \pi^6 (N_c^2-1)}
\int_{x,y,t} {\cal K}(x-w)\cdot {\cal K}(y-w') \langle\rho^f(x) \rho^f(y) \rangle \notag \\ & \times  
\Big[ 
 {\cal K}(x,x,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(x) ( U(y) - U(w') ) T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(x) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )} )\right\}
 \notag \\ & -  {\cal K}(w, w,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ T^e U^\dagger(w) ( U(y) - U(w') )  T^e \textcolor{red}{( U^\dagger(y) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(x) - U(t) )}\textcolor{blue}{( U^\dagger(w) - U^\dagger(t) )  ( U(w) - U(t) )} )\right\}
\Big] 
\end{align} 


And finally we need to account for the second term in Eq.~\eqref{Eq:HLOfinal} 
\begin{align}
    &\frac{dN^{(5)}}{d^2 w d^2 w' d y} =  \textcolor{red}{-}\textcolor{blue}{+}\frac{\alpha_s^2 \delta Y}{8 \pi^6 (N_c^2-1)}
\int_{x,y,t} {\cal K}(x-w)\cdot {\cal K}(y-w') \langle\rho^f(x) \rho^f(y) \rangle \notag \\ & \times  
\Big[ 
 {\cal K}(y,y,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ (U^\dagger(y) -U^\dagger(t) ) U(y) T^d \right\} \textcolor{blue}{{\rm Tr}}\left\{ (U^\dagger(x) -U^\dagger(w) ) U(y) T^d \right\} \notag \\ &
 -  {\cal K}(w',w',t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ (U^\dagger(w') -U^\dagger(t) ) U(w') T^d \right\} \textcolor{blue}{{\rm Tr}}\left\{ (U^\dagger(x) -U^\dagger(w) ) U(w') T^d \right\}
\notag \\ &
 -  {\cal K}(x,x,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ (U^\dagger(x) -U^\dagger(t) ) U(x) T^d \right\} 
 \textcolor{blue}{{\rm Tr}}\left\{ U^\dagger(x)  (U(y) - U(w')) T^d \right\}
\notag \\ & 
+ {\cal K}(w,w,t) {\rm Tr} \left\{ (U^\dagger(w) -U^\dagger(t) ) U(w) T^d \right\} 
 \textcolor{blue}{{\rm Tr}}\left\{ U^\dagger(w)  (U(y) - U(w')) T^d \right\}
\Big] 
\end{align} 


The sum of the above gives the needed target evolution of the leading order single inclusive production. 
Note that the terms can be combined, but it does not appear to be very useful while matching the results from the diagrams.
\end{document}

\section{Row V Group 1}
Now, let's handle the UV divergence. We will subtract the UV divergent term from the cross section 
\begin{align} 
     \frac{d^{3}N_{1\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,  \int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\,e^{-ik\,\left(\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}\,(z-x)\right)}\, \int_{0}^{k^{+}} \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(z-x)^{m}}{(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}\,\frac{1}{\left[p^{+}(y-x)^{2}+(k^{+}-p^{+})(y-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \frac{d_{\perp}\,\delta_{im}}{2(z-x)^{2}}\,\left[(y-z)^{2}-(y-x)^{2}\right]\times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\, k^{+}\,  \int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\,e^{-ik\,\left(\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}\,(z-x)\right)}\, \int_{0}^{k^{+}} \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(z-x)^{m}}{(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}\,\frac{1}{\left[p^{+}(y-x)^{2}+(k^{+}-p^{+})(y-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \left[\frac{1}{p^{+\,2}}\right]_{+}\, \times \left[\frac{(y-z)^{m}\,(z-x)^{i}}{(z-x)^{2}}-\frac{(y-z)^{m}\,(y-x)^{i}}{2(y-x)^{2}}\right]\times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \notag \\ & \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,  \int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\,e^{-ik\,\left(\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}\,(z-x)\right)}\, \int_{0}^{k^{+}} \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-x)}{(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}\,\frac{1}{\left[p^{+}(y-x)^{2}+(k^{+}-p^{+})(y-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \left[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\right]_{+}\,\left[\frac{(y-z)\cdot (z-x)}{(z-x)^{2}}-\frac{(y-z)\cdot (y-x)}{2(y-x)^{2}}-\frac{\left[(y-z)^{2}-(y-x)^{2}\right]}{2(z-x)^{2}}\right]\times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \notag \\ & \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\, \int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\,e^{-ik\,\left(\frac{k^{+}-q^{+}}{k^{+}}\right)\, (z-x)}\, \int_{0}^{k^{+}} \frac{dq^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-x)}{(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}\,\frac{1}{\left[\left(k^{+}-q^{+}\right)\,(y-x)^{2}+q^{+}\,(y-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \left[\frac{1}{q^{+}}\right]_{+}\,\left[\frac{(y-x)\cdot (z-x)}{(z-x)^{2}}+\frac{(y-z)\cdot (y-x)}{2(y-z)^{2}}-\frac{\left[(y-z)^{2}-(y-x)^{2}\right]}{2(z-x)^{2}}\right]\times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \notag \\ & \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\int_{w^\prime}\, e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\,e^{-ik\,\left(\frac{k^{+}-q^{+}}{k^{+}}\right)\, (z-x)}\,\int_{0}^{k^{+}} \frac{dq^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(z-x)^{m}}{(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}\,\frac{1}{\left[(k^{+}-q^{+})\,(y-x)^{2}+q^{+}(y-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \left[\frac{1}{q^{+}}\right]_{+}\,\left[\frac{(y-x)^{i}\,(z-x)^{m}}{(z-x)^{2}}+\frac{(y-z)^{m}\,(y-x)^{i}}{2(y-z)^{2}}+\frac{(y-z)^{i}\,(z-x)^{m}}{(z-x)^{2}}-\frac{(y-z)^{i}\,(y-x)^{m}}{2(y-x)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ & \times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,\int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\,e^{-ik\,\left(\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}}\,(z-x)\right)}\, \int_{0}^{k^{+}} \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(z-x)^{m}}{(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}\,\frac{1}{\left[p^{+}(y-x)^{2}+(k^{+}-p^{+})(y-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \left[\frac{1}{p^{+}}\right]_{+}\,\left[\frac{(y-x)^{i}\,(z-x)^{m}}{(z-x)^{2}}+\frac{(y-z)^{m}\,(y-x)^{i}}{2(y-z)^{2}}+\frac{(y-z)^{i}\,(z-x)^{m}}{(z-x)^{2}}-\frac{(y-z)^{i}\,(y-x)^{m}}{2(y-x)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ & \times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{8\pi^{4}}\,  \int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\,e^{-ik\,\left(\frac{(k^{+}-q^{+})}{k^{+}}\,(z-x)\right)}\, \int_{0}^{k^{+}} \frac{dq^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\frac{(x'-w')^{i}(z-x)^{m}}{(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}\,\frac{k^{+}}{\left[(k^{+}-q^{+})\,(y-x)^{2}+q^{+}(y-z)^{2}\right]}\notag \\ & \left[\frac{1}{q^{+\,2}}\right]_{+}\,\left[\frac{(y-x)^{m}\,(z-x)^{i}}{(z-x)^{2}}+\frac{(y-z)^{i}\,(y-x)^{m}}{2(y-z)^{2}}\right]\times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \notag \\ & \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)
\end{align} 
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{2\,\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & = -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'} \left[\frac{(y-z)\cdot (z-x)\,(z-x)\cdot (x'-w')}{(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}-\frac{(y-z)\cdot (z-x)\,(y-x)\cdot (x'-w')}{2(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ & \left[\frac{1}{\Lambda}-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\right]\times\,\left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'} \left[\frac{(y-z)\cdot (z-x)\,(z-x)\cdot (x'-w')}{(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}-\frac{(y-z)\cdot (z-x)\,(y-x)\cdot (x'-w')}{2(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\, (y-z)^{2}}\right]\notag \\ & \left(ik\cdot (z-x)\right)\times\,\log{\left[\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right]}\,\times\,\left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \log{\left[\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right]}\,\int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\bigg[\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-x)\, (y-z)\cdot (z-x)}{(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-x)\, (y-z)\cdot (y-x)}{2(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-x)\, \left[(y-z)^{2}-(y-x)^{2}\right]}{2(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg]\times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \notag \\ & \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & + \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\frac{1}{k^{+}}\, \log{\left[\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right]}\,\int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-x)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\bigg[\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-x)\, (y-z)\cdot (z-x)}{(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & -\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-x)\, (y-z)\cdot (y-x)}{2(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}-\frac{(x'-w')\cdot (z-x)\, \left[(y-z)^{2}-(y-x)^{2}\right]}{2(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg]\times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \notag \\ & \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ &-\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left[\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right]}\,  \int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-x)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\bigg[\frac{(y-x)\cdot (x'-w')\,(z-x)\cdot (z-x)}{(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(y-z)\cdot (z-x)\,(y-x)\cdot (x'-w')}{2(y-z)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}} +\frac{(y-z)\cdot (x'-w')\,(z-x)\cdot (z-x)}{(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}-\frac{(y-z)\cdot (x'-w')\,(y-x)\cdot (z-x)}{2(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-z)^{2}}\bigg]\notag \\ & \times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\, \frac{1}{k^{+}}\,\log{\left[\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right]}\,  \int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-z)}\, \int_{x,y,z,x'}\bigg[\frac{(y-x)\cdot (x'-w')\,(z-x)\cdot (z-x)}{(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}}\notag \\ & +\frac{(y-z)\cdot (z-x)\,(y-x)\cdot (x'-w')}{2(y-z)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}} +\frac{(y-z)\cdot (x'-w')\,(z-x)\cdot (z-x)}{(z-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}}-\frac{(y-z)\cdot (x'-w')\,(y-x)\cdot (z-x)}{2(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}}\bigg]\notag \\ & \times \left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & -\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-x)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\bigg[\frac{(y-x)\cdot (z-x)\,(z-x)\cdot (x'-w')}{(z-x)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}+\frac{(y-z)\cdot (x'-w')\,(y-x)\cdot (z-x)}{2(y-z)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}\bigg]\times\notag \\ & \left[\frac{1}{\Lambda}-\frac{1}{k^{+}}\right]\,\left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)\notag \\ & +\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}\,\frac{g^{4}}{16\pi^{5}}\,\int_{w^\prime} e^{-ik\,(w'-x)}\,\int_{x,y,z,x'}\,\bigg[\frac{(y-x)\cdot (z-x)\,(z-x)\cdot (x'-w')}{(z-x)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}+\frac{(y-z)\cdot (x'-w')\,(y-x)\cdot (z-x)}{2(y-z)^{2}\,(y-x)^{2}\,(x'-w')^{2}(z-x)^{2}}\bigg]\times\notag \\ & \left(ik\cdot (z-x)\right)\,\times \log{\left[\frac{k^{+}}{\Lambda}\right]}\times\,\left[U^{ab'}(x')-U^{ab'}(w')\right]\times \left[N_{c}\,U^{ac'}(y)-f^{adc}f^{c'be}\,U^{db}(x)U^{ce}(z)\right]\,\rho^{b'}(x')\rho^{c'}(y)
\end{align}

Have a look at this paper carefully. They have shown the origin of the large transverse logarithms associated with the QCD one loop
β function coefficient in the NLO JIMWLK Hamiltonian. We show that some of these terms
are not associated with the running of the QCD coupling constant but rather with the DGLAP
evolution. I am doing gluon production at NLO at order $g^{4}$, where I presume to observe this effect. Can I observe the same stuff over there. If yes, I can show the terms where one can find this is in production cross section. This corresponds to two rho terms that are present in this cross section. 
\begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k}  & = \frac{2}{(2\pi)^{3}}  \int_{w, w^\prime} e^{-ik(w^\prime-w)}\, \int\, \frac{dp^{+}}{2\pi}\,\int_{x,z}\, \bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})+\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b^{\prime}}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})U^{b^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})\bigg]\times\notag \\ & \bigg[-\Bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, bca}_{1jki}(k^{+},w-z;p^{+},z-x)\Bar{\mf{B}}^{bc}_{2jk}(k^{+}-p^{+},- z;p^{+},-x)+\bar{\mf{C}}^{\dagger\, dca}_{1jki}(k^+,w-z; p^+, z-x)\,U^{db}(x)\,U^{ce}(z) \notag \\ & {\mf{B}}^{be}_{2jk} (k^{+}-p^+, -z; p^+, -x) \bigg]\nonumber
\end{align} and \begin{align}
    \frac{d^{3}N_{\rm NLO}^{\rm}}{d^{3}k} &  = \frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3}}  \int_{w, w^\prime} e^{-ik(w^\prime-w)}\,\bigg[-\bar{\mf{A}}^{\dagger\,(1)\,a}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})+\mf{A}^{\dagger\, (1)\, b^{\prime}}_{i}(k^{+},-w^{\prime})U^{b^{\prime}a}(w^{\prime})\bigg]\times \notag \\ & \bigg[U^{ab}(w)\mf{A}^{(3)\, b}_{i}(k^{+},- w)-\Bar{\mf{A}}^{(3)\, a}_{i}(k^{+}, - w)\bigg]\nonumber
\end{align}
where: \begin{align}
     \mf{B}^{bc}_{2ji}(k^{+}, -\v{w}; p^{+}, -\v{z}) & =    \int_{\mathbf{x}}\frac{ig^{2}f^{cba}\rho^{a}(\mathbf{x})\sqrt{p^{+}k^{+}}}{4\pi^{2}\left[p^{+}(x-z)^{2}+k^{+} (x-w)^{2}\right]\,(p^{+}+k^{+})}\,\Bigg[\frac{\delta^{ij}}{2(z-w)^{2}}\left((x-z)^{2}-(x-w)^{2}\right)\notag \\ & +\frac{(p^{+}+k^{+})}{k^{+}}\,\left(\frac{(x-z)^{j}(z-w)^{i}}{(z-w)^{2}}-\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-w)^{i}}{2(x-w)^{2}}\right)\notag \\ & +\frac{(p^{+}+k^{+})}{p^{+}}\,\left(\frac{(x-w)^{i}(z-w)^{j}}{(z-w)^{2}} +\frac{(x-z)^{j}(x-w)^{i}}{2(x-z)^{2}}\right)\Bigg]
\end{align}
\begin{align}
\label{A1coeff}
  -\, \mf{A}^{a}_{i}(k^{+},\mathbf{z}) & = - \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}\pi}\frac{ig}{\sqrt{k^+}}\int_{x}\frac{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})^{i}}{(\mathbf{x}-\mathbf{z})^{2}} \rho^{a}(\mathbf{x}) 
\end{align}
\begin{align}
   \mf{C}^{bca}_{1jki}(k^{+},z; p^{+},x) = -\frac{gf^{abc}}{2\pi \sqrt{2k^{+}}}\,\frac{\sqrt{p^{+}\,(k^{+}-p^{+})}}{k^{+}}\, \delta^{(2)}\left(z+\frac{p^{+}}{k^{+}} x\right)\frac{x^{m}}{x^{2}}\left[ \delta_{im} \delta_{jk}
     - \frac{k^{+}}{p^{+}}\delta_{jm} \delta_{ik}
   - \frac{k^{+}}{k^{+}-p^{+}} \delta_{km}\delta_{ij}
 \right]
\end{align}
